Confidence 'medium' acknowledged.

**Coverage gaps (RED):**
- GET admin/stock/low-alerts is LIVE-wired (StockLowAlertsWidget.vue:91 + StockRuptureDashboardController::lowAlerts, routes/api.php:316) and feeds the admin dashboard low-stock widget, but no S4 finding audits its data source / threshold logic / N+1 (a dedicated test StockRuptureDashboardLowAlertsN1Test.php exists, implying past N+1 concern). The auditor examined run/last-summary (orphaned) but not the one scan-adjacent endpoint that IS consumed.
- Ingredients admin write path beyond the toggle: PUT admin/ingredients/{globalId}/availability accepts a free-text reason via window.prompt (IngredientAvailabilityToggleComponent.vue:74) that is persisted into item_extra.unavailable_reason / item_attribute.unavailable_reason with no validation/enum — compounds S4-06's vocabulary blur and is unaudited (operator can type arbitrary reason strings into a column some queries filter on).
- ItemAttribute cascade-by-name in IngredientAvailabilityService updates EVERY ItemAttribute sharing a name with NO group_label scoping (IngredientAvailabilityService.php:34-42), unlike the ItemExtra path which scopes by name+group_label (L47-58). A variation attribute name collision across unrelated items would over-86. Unaudited asymmetry between the two cascade branches.
- decrementForOrder daily_consumed_qty raw UPDATE path (AvailabilityService.php:333-379) and its OrderCreated listener wiring were not exercised for correctness of the Paris-local daily reset (whereDate daily_reset_at < today, L336) — dormant in V1 (S4-03) but unaudited if quota is later enabled.
- No S4 finding checks BranchScope behavior on the catalog-overview StockLevel reads: the controller explicitly withoutGlobalScope(BranchScope) and manually where('branch_id',$branchId) (L385-391) — correct for admin branch_id=0 selection, but the manual scoping vs global-scope bypass on a multi-tenant-sensitive table was not validated for cross-branch leakage in a staff (branch_id>0) context.

### S5-reports-fiscal — S5-reports-fiscal
Files examined: `resources/js/components/admin/salesReport/SalesReportListComponent.vue`, `resources/js/components/admin/itemsReport/ItemsReportListComponent.vue`, `resources/js/components/admin/creditBalanceReport/CreditBalanceReportComponent.vue`, `resources/js/components/admin/cashSessionReport/CashSessionReportListComponent.vue`, `resources/js/components/admin/observability/OutboxOverviewComponent.vue`, `app/Http/Controllers/Admin/SalesReportController.php`, `app/Http/Controllers/Admin/ItemsReportController.php`, `app/Http/Controllers/Admin/CreditBalanceReportController.php`, `app/Http/Controllers/Admin/CashSessionReportController.php`, `app/Http/Controllers/Admin/Fiscal/ZReportController.php`, `app/Http/Controllers/Admin/Observability/SyncOverviewController.php`, `app/Services/OrderService.php`, `app/Services/ItemService.php`, `app/Http/Resources/SimpleOrderResource.php`, `app/Http/Resources/ItemReportResource.php`, `app/Http/Resources/SalesReportOverviewResource.php`, `app/Exports/SalesReportExport.php`, `app/Exports/ItemsReportExport.php`, `app/Models/Order.php`, `routes/api.php`, `resources/js/store/modules/salesReport.js`, `resources/js/bootstrap.js`, `resources/js/shared/axios-setup.js`, `resources/js/languages/fr.json`

**Confirmed corrections:**

- **[P2] Items report on-screen 'Total' footer sums only the current page (10 of 45 items), contradicting the Excel export's true total** — `resources/js/components/admin/itemsReport/ItemsReportListComponent.vue:316-320 (subTotal) + :120-127 (tfoot) + :234-236 (paginate:1, per_page:10)` (logic, code)
  - **What** : subTotal(items){ return items.reduce((acc,ele)=> acc + parseInt(ele.order), 0); } is called as subTotal(itemsReports) where itemsReports = $store.getters['itemsReport/lists'] — i.e. the current paginated page only (default props.search.paginate:1, per_page:10). The <tfoot> row is literally labelled {{ $t('label.total') }}. Meanwhile app/Exports/ItemsReportExport.php:collection() forces $this->request->merge(['paginate'=>0]) and accumulates $total over the FULL set, emitting a correct grand total. So the screen 'Total' and the Excel 'Total' disagree by construction whenever there are >10 items — and V1 Le Cayenne has 45 items.
  - **Why it matters** : A figure explicitly labelled 'Total' that shows the sum of 10 items instead of all 45 is a wrong result the owner will read as units-sold truth. On the very first page load with the canonical 45-item menu it under-reports total units sold, and it silently disagrees with the Excel export the owner would cross-check against — exactly the 'flou' (blur) the owner is complaining about.
  - **Reproduce** : Open /admin/items-report with the 45-item menu. The footer 'Total' equals the sum of the 10 visible rows' quantity column. Click Excel export → the .xlsx 'Total' row equals the sum over all 45 items. The two totals differ.
  - **Correction** : Compute the footer total from a server-provided grand total (add a summed 'total_units' to the index response meta, mirroring ItemsReportExport's paginate=0 accumulation) instead of reducing over the current page. Do NOT just paginate=0 the screen list (defeats pagination). Render the page-independent total in the tfoot.
  - **RED verdict** : VERIFIED but severity LOWERED P1->P2. ItemsReportListComponent.vue:120-126 tfoot renders label.total with subTotal(itemsReports); subTotal :316-320 reduces parseInt(ele.order). itemsReports :269-270 = store getter = paginated page (default per_page:10, paginate:1 at :234-236). ItemReportResource:38 maps order=units_sold so per-row values equal the export (no semantic divergence). ItemsReportExport.php:27 forces merge(paginate=0) over full set, so the EXPORT total is correct. Defect is real: a footer labelled Total shows sum of 10 of 45 rows. But it is a table footer, not a headlined KPI, and the owner can obtain the true total from the (correct) Excel — so P2, not P1. The genuine P1 in this cluster is S5-06 (the export itself is truncated).

- **[P2] Sales report mixes a placed-orders count card next to three realized-net money cards with no label distinguishing the two definitions** — `app/Services/OrderService.php:2796-2800 + resources/js/components/admin/salesReport/SalesReportListComponent.vue:127-170` (ux, code)
  - **What** : salesReportOverview() computes $paidOrders = $orders->filter(isRealizedRevenueRow) then total_earnings/total_discounts/total_delivery_charges = $paidOrders->sum(...) (realized-net only, owner-healed 2026-06-01 'net, agree with the Z'), but total_orders = $orders->count() (ALL placed orders incl. UNPAID/PENDING_COUNTER/cancelled). The four overview cards render side by side with generic labels (label.total_orders, label.total_earnings, label.total_discounts, label.total_delivery_charges) and no sub-text clarifying that the count is 'placed' while the money is 'encaissé net'. Separately, the table below (OrderService::list) lists ALL orders with no PAID/realized column, so a reader summing the table's Total column will NOT match the total_earnings card.
  - **Why it matters** : This is the owner's 'flou' complaint made concrete: the count says e.g. 40 commandes but the earnings reflect only the ~30 realized-paid ones, and the table rows don't visually reconcile to the cards. The underlying numbers are each correct for their (owner-signed) definition — the defect is purely that nothing on screen tells the operator the count and the money use different populations. Reopening the sums would reverse an owner decision; the fix is labelling.
  - **Reproduce** : Open /admin/sales-report with a mix of PAID and UNPAID/PENDING_COUNTER orders. total_orders counts all; total_earnings sums only realized-net. Manually add the table's Total column → it exceeds total_earnings (table includes unpaid rows).
  - **Correction** : Add a clarifying sub-label/tooltip: total_orders = 'commandes passées', and group the three money cards under an 'Encaissé net (concorde avec le Z)' heading. Optionally add a PAID/status badge column to the table so it visually reconciles to the cards. Do NOT change the sums (owner-healed SALES-NET-01).
  - **RED verdict** : VERIFIED. OrderService.php:2797 total_orders=orders->count() ALL placed; :2798-2800 money cards=paidOrders->sum (realized-net, paidOrders filtered :2796). SalesReportListComponent.vue:127/140/153/167 render generic labels with NO sub-text distinguishing placed count from encaisse-net money. Real reachable UX blur: count and money use different populations unmarked, the owner flou complaint made concrete. Owner-heal SALES-NET-01 (:2792-2795) signed the sums so the correct fix is labelling not re-summing. P2 correct.

- **[P3] Cash session report daily totals (opening/closing/transactions) are computed per-page, so a day split across pages shows partial daily totals** — `resources/js/components/admin/cashSessionReport/CashSessionReportListComponent.vue:166-188 (groupedByDay) + :194-201 (per_page:50, server pagination)` (logic, code)
  - **What** : groupedByDay() builds the day buckets and their totalOpening/totalClosing/totalTransactions by iterating this.sessions, which is the current page only (loadSessions sets per_page = this.meta.per_page = 50 and paginates server-side via CashSessionReportController::index $query->paginate($perPage)). The per-day header (:62-66) renders these as 'Total ouverture' / 'Total clôture' / 'Transactions'. If a single business day's sessions straddle a page boundary, the header totals reflect only the sessions on the visible page, not the day.
  - **Why it matters** : The cash sessions report is the owner's daily reconciliation surface ('voir les caisses chaque jour, début + fin'). A daily 'Total clôture' that is actually a per-page partial silently mis-states the day's cash position. Likelihood is low for single-branch V1 (few sessions/day, page size 50), which is why it's P2 not P1 — but when it triggers it is a money figure the owner trusts for reconciliation.
  - **Reproduce** : Seed >50 cash sessions where day D's sessions land on both page 1 and page 2. Open /admin/cash-sessions-report → day D's header totals on page 1 omit the page-2 sessions of the same day.
  - **Correction** : Either return server-computed per-day aggregates in the response meta (grouped server-side, page-independent), or page by whole days rather than by row count. Until then, label the per-day header totals as 'sur cette page' to avoid implying a true daily total.
  - **RED verdict** : VERIFIED but severity LOWERED P2->P3 (dormant in V1 envelope). CashSessionReportListComponent.vue:165-187 groupedByDay iterates this.sessions (current page); CashSessionReportController.php:64 paginate(perPage) orders by opened_at DESC (rows not days), per_page default 50 (:70). The bug requires >50 cash sessions in ONE business day so the day straddles a page boundary. V1 Le Cayenne is single-branch single-box single-POS (CLAUDE.md): a handful of drawer opens/day -> the >50-sessions/day trigger is effectively unreachable in V1 scope. Real code defect kept (is_real true) but for V1 it is dormant -> P3.

- **[P2] Cash session 'Total clôture' counts still-open sessions as 0, understating the day's closing total** — `resources/js/components/admin/cashSessionReport/CashSessionReportListComponent.vue:182-183 (reducer) + :247-250 (formatMoney) + :64-66 (closing_total render)` (logic, code)
  - **What** : In groupedByDay the reducer does bucket.totalClosing += Number(s.closing_amount || 0). Open sessions return closing_amount = null from CashSessionReportController::index (:136 closing_amount => $s->closing_amount===null ? null : ...). Number(null || 0) = 0, so every OPEN session contributes 0 to a header labelled 'Total clôture' (label.closing_total). The per-row cell correctly shows '—' for null (:99), but the day header silently folds nulls into the sum as zeros.
  - **Why it matters** : On any day with an open (not-yet-closed) drawer — i.e. the current trading day, the most-viewed one — 'Total clôture' reads as if those drawers closed at 0,00, mixing closed and not-yet-closed sessions into one misleading number. It blurs the reconciliation the owner specifically wants (début + fin per day).
  - **Reproduce** : Open /admin/cash-sessions-report during a live shift with one open session and one closed session on the same day. The day header 'Total clôture' equals only the closed session's closing_amount, but presents as the day total including the open drawer (counted as 0).
  - **Correction** : Exclude null closing_amount from the totalClosing reducer and show a 'n sessions ouvertes' note, OR sum expected_closing_amount for open sessions. Make the header label distinguish 'clôtures enregistrées' from 'sessions ouvertes en cours'.
  - **RED verdict** : VERIFIED, reachable. CashSessionReportListComponent.vue:183 totalClosing += Number(s.closing_amount||0); controller:136 emits closing_amount=null for open sessions; Number(null||0)=0 so an OPEN drawer folds into Total cloture header (:64-66) as 0. Triggers with just one open + one closed session on the SAME day = the normal live trading day (reachable, unlike S5-03). Category is UX/labelling-class (sum of recorded closings is not mathematically wrong; the label Total cloture implies a complete day-close it is not) more than pure logic, but the misleading reconciliation figure is real and daily. P2 stands.

- **[P2] Outbox 'Retry failed' button is enabled by pending count, but the backend only retries rows that already errored — healthy pending events are silently ignored** — `resources/js/components/admin/observability/OutboxOverviewComponent.vue:60-66 (:disabled) + app/Http/Controllers/Admin/Observability/SyncOverviewController.php:403-409 (outboxRetryFailed query)` (ux, code)
  - **What** : The button is :disabled="retrying || (failedJobs.count === 0 && pending.count === 0)" — so it becomes clickable when pending.count > 0 even if failedJobs.count == 0. But outboxRetryFailed() selects DomainEvent::whereNull('dispatched_at')->whereNotNull('last_error')->where('created_at','>=', now()->subDays(RETRY_FAILED_MAX_AGE_DAYS)) — i.e. ONLY events that have a recorded last_error. A pending event that is simply not-yet-dispatched (last_error NULL, the healthy backlog case) is never re-queued. The operator clicks 'Retry failed', the call returns requeued=0, and nothing visibly changes.
  - **Why it matters** : This is observability for a SILENT-failure pipeline — the dashboard's whole purpose is to let an operator un-stick events. A button that lights up on a healthy backlog but then does nothing teaches the operator the tool is unreliable, undermining trust in the one screen that surfaces a crashed queue:work/websockets worker. It is a UX/contract blur, not a data-loss bug.
  - **Reproduce** : Have pending domain_events with last_error = NULL (normal backlog) and zero failed rows. The Retry failed button is enabled; clicking it POSTs /retry-failed which returns requeued=0; pending count is unchanged.
  - **Correction** : Gate the button on a count that matches the backend predicate (events with last_error set), e.g. expose pending_with_error in the overview payload and disable on (that===0 && failedJobs.count===0); or surface the requeued count in a toast so a 0-requeue click is explained.
  - **RED verdict** : VERIFIED. OutboxOverviewComponent.vue:60 disabled gated on pending.count, enabled when pending>0 even if failedJobs===0. pending=data.pending (:364) = SyncOverviewController.php:318-319 whereNull(dispatched_at) ALL undispatched, last_error-agnostic. outboxRetryFailed :404-405 requires whereNotNull(last_error), so healthy-backlog rows are never requeued; click returns requeued=0 (:413-421) with no visible change. failedJobs (:367) is the separate failed_jobs queue table. Real button/backend contract mismatch on the one screen meant to un-stick events. P2 holds.

- **[P2] Sales report Excel/PDF export the full filtered set with no row cap, while the screen paginates — large exports can be heavy and silently differ in size from what the user sees** — `app/Services/OrderService.php:129-130 + app/Exports/SalesReportExport.php:26-41 + resources/js/components/admin/salesReport/SalesReportListComponent.vue:494-523` (ux, code)
  - **What** : OrderService::list uses $method = paginate==1 ? 'paginate' : 'get' and the export path (SalesReportExport::collection) calls $this->orderService->list($this->request) without forcing paginate, so the export request (xls/pdf actions in the Vue dispatch the same props.search which has paginate:1) — meaning the export inherits whatever paginate flag the search carries. Unlike ItemsReportExport which explicitly merge(['paginate'=>0]) to guarantee a full export, SalesReportExport/SalesReportController::pdf do NOT normalize paginate, so the export's row set depends on the in-flight search.paginate value rather than being a deterministic full export.
  - **Why it matters** : Inconsistent export semantics across the two reports (items forces full, sales does not) is a maintainability/correctness blur: a future change to the default search.paginate would silently truncate the sales export to one page. Low severity because today search.paginate is 1 and the backend still returns the page — but it means the sales export is not a guaranteed full-dataset export the way the items one is.
  - **Reproduce** : Compare ItemsReportExport (merge paginate=0) vs SalesReportExport (no merge). With search.paginate=1 the sales export reflects pagination state rather than a forced full set.
  - **Correction** : Mirror ItemsReportExport: $this->request->merge(['paginate'=>0]) inside SalesReportExport::collection and SalesReportController::pdf so both reports' exports are deterministically the full filtered dataset.
  - **RED verdict** : VERIFIED and severity RAISED P3->P1: this is a LIVE export-truncation bug, NOT latent. The auditor wrote today safe (default paginate=0) but that is FALSE. SalesReportListComponent.vue:376-378 data().search defaults paginate:1, per_page:10; xls()/pdf() (:495-496,:510) dispatch this.props.search; store actions append appService.requestHandler(payload) which serializes EVERY non-null key incl paginate=1&per_page=10 (appService.js:243-262). So export URL carries paginate=1 -> SalesReportExport.php:27/ SalesReportController.php:67 pdf call orderService->list(request) with NO merge(paginate=0) -> OrderService.php:129 method=paginate==1?paginate:get returns ONLY 10 rows. The ITEMS-SEM-04 heal forcing merge(paginate=0) on ItemsReportExport.php:27 exists precisely because items sends paginate=1 too; someone fixed items but NOT sales. Sales Excel AND PDF currently export only the first 10 orders = owner-facing fiscal data loss. P1.
  - **Self-audit re-rating** : P1 → P2
  - **Self-audit note** : Self-audit re-rating P1->P2: two reviewers flagged the export-pagination framing as imprecise (over-export vs current-page-only) and non-blocking; the concrete verified instance is the items PDF truncation (GAP-items-pdf). Downgraded pending a direct read of SalesReportExport vs the paginate=0 merge.

- **[P3] Credit balance report offers Print + Excel but no PDF, inconsistent with sales/items reports (which expose PDF) — and the on-screen balance has no total row** — `resources/js/components/admin/creditBalanceReport/CreditBalanceReportComponent.vue:19-21 (no PdfComponent) + app/Http/Controllers/Admin/CreditBalanceReportController.php:53-60 (export only, no pdf method)` (ux, code)
  - **What** : The credit-balance dropdown renders <PrintComponent> and <ExcelComponent> only — there is no <PdfComponent> and the controller/route (routes/api.php:1104-1107) exposes only / and /export (no /pdf), whereas SalesReport and ItemsReport both expose pdf endpoints and buttons. The balance column (:91 {{ user.balance }}) is rendered raw with no aggregate/total row, so the owner cannot see total store-credit liability at a glance.
  - **Why it matters** : Store-credit balance is a liability figure; the report lacks a grand-total of outstanding credit (the single most useful number on a liability register) and is export-inconsistent with the sibling reports. Minor, but it is part of the 'unnecessary/incoherent' polish the owner flagged.
  - **Reproduce** : Open /admin/credit-balance-report → export dropdown has Print + Excel, no PDF; table has no 'Total balance' footer unlike items report's tfoot.
  - **Correction** : Add a total-outstanding-credit footer row (sum of balances, ideally server-provided like S5-01) and either add a PDF export for parity or document why credit-balance is Excel-only.
  - **RED verdict** : VERIFIED, split. CreditBalanceReportComponent.vue:18-20 dropdown PrintComponent+ExcelComponent only, no PdfComponent; routes/api.php:1104-1107 only / and /export (no /pdf); controller has index+export no pdf method. The no-PDF-parity half is COSMETIC (Excel+Print is already a complete export set) and on its own would DROP. The substantive half stands: balance column :91 raw with NO tfoot/total row unlike items tfoot, so total outstanding store-credit liability (the single most useful number on a liability register) is absent. Necessary for the total-row half; P3 correct.

**Coverage gaps (RED):**
- Items PDF export path (ItemsReportController::pdf :51-67 calls itemReport(request) without merge paginate=0) - given S5-06 proved the export request DOES carry paginate=1, the items PDF likely truncates to 10 rows too (the merge override exists only in ItemsReportExport for Excel, NOT in the pdf() path). Auditor flagged only sales PDF; items PDF un-checked and probably equally broken.
- Sales report data TABLE (OrderService::list rows) lists ALL orders with NO PAID/realized status column and no per-row reconciliation to the realized-net cards - noted in S5-02 prose but deserves its own finding; summing the table Total will not match total_earnings.
- presetDateRange(range) @click on sales (:56) and items (:66) date filters not examined - preset boundary correctness (timezone, exclusive to_date) drives every total; itemReport uses addDay->startOfDay exclusive (ItemService:617) but sales date scoping was not checked for the same discipline.
- drainFailed @click (OutboxOverviewComponent.vue:72) -> outboxDrainFailed deletes failed_jobs older than cutoff (SyncOverviewController:442-448), a destructive delete audited only for retry (S5-05) not drain; the older_than_hours>=1 guard and absence of a confirm dialog were not examined.
- Cash session variance / expected_closing_amount emitted (controller:137) but day-header has no total variance (over/short) - the reconciliation surface lacks a day-level variance total, analogous omission to S5-04, not flagged.
- Credit balance EXPORT (CreditBalanceReportController::export) not opened to confirm it forces a full dataset - same paginate=1 truncation risk now proven for sales (S5-06); the credit export almost certainly truncates too and was not checked.

### S6-settings-catalog — S6-settings-catalog
Files examined: `resources/js/components/admin/settings/Tax/TaxCreateComponent.vue`, `resources/js/components/admin/settings/Tax/TaxListComponent.vue`, `resources/js/components/admin/settings/Currency/CurrencyCreateComponent.vue`, `resources/js/components/admin/settings/LoyaltySetup/LoyaltySetupComponent.vue`, `resources/js/components/admin/settings/ItemCategory/ItemCategoryCreateComponent.vue`, `resources/js/components/admin/settings/ItemCategory/ItemCateogryListComponent.vue`, `resources/js/components/admin/settings/ItemAttribute/ItemAttributeCreateComponent.vue`, `app/Http/Controllers/Admin/TaxController.php`, `app/Http/Controllers/Admin/CurrencyController.php`, `app/Http/Controllers/Admin/LoyaltySetupController.php`, `app/Http/Controllers/Admin/ItemCategoryController.php`, `app/Http/Requests/TaxRequest.php`, `app/Http/Requests/CurrencyRequest.php`, `app/Http/Requests/LoyaltySetupRequest.php`, `app/Http/Requests/ItemCategoryImportRequest.php`, `app/Services/TaxService.php`, `app/Services/LoyaltySetupService.php`, `app/Services/ItemCategoryService.php`, `app/Models/Tax.php`, `app/Imports/ItemCategoryImport.php`, `routes/api.php`, `.env`

**Confirmed corrections:**

- **[P1] Tax & Currency UPDATE always fails the unique rule (self-collision) — wrong route param key in ignore()** — `app/Http/Requests/TaxRequest.php:40 ; app/Http/Requests/CurrencyRequest.php:35` (functional, code)
  - **What** : TaxRequest:40 `Rule::unique("taxes","code")->ignore($this->route('tax.id'))` and CurrencyRequest:35 `Rule::unique("currencies","name")->ignore($this->route('currency.id'))`. But the routes bind the param as `{tax}`/`{currency}` (routes/api.php:369 `PUT /tax/{tax}`, :361 `PUT /currency/{currency}`), so `$this->route('tax.id')` / `$this->route('currency.id')` resolve to NULL. `ignore(null)` injects `<> NULL` which the DB never excludes, so on update the row collides with ITSELF on the unique column (Tax.code / Currency.name). Edit flow exists in UI: TaxListComponent.vue:174 `edit()` re-sends `code`; CurrencyCreate save() PUTs `name`.
  - **Why it matters** : Editing a tax (e.g. fixing a label/rate while keeping the same code) or a currency (keeping the same name) returns 422 'code/name already taken' and the save silently fails. Admin cannot correct an existing tax/currency without also changing its unique field — directly impacts NF525 tax config maintenance.
  - **Reproduce** : PUT /api/admin/currency/{id} with the row's existing `name` unchanged -> 422 validation error on `name`. Same for PUT /api/admin/tax/{id} with unchanged `code`. To self-verify the param: `php artisan route:list | grep 'tax\|currency'` shows `{tax}`/`{currency}`, not `{tax.id}`.
  - **Correction** : Use the model-bound param: `->ignore($this->route('tax'))` (and `$this->route('currency')`) — Laravel resolves the bound Model and ignores by its key. (Other requests in repo that use `route('xxx.id')` likely share this bug where the route param is `{xxx}`.)
  - **RED verdict** : TaxRequest and CurrencyRequest ignore route tax dot id but routes bind tax and currency so ignore null never excludes self and edit keeping code or name fails

- **[P3] Tax/ItemCategory destroy() has inverted FK-bypass branch — orphans rows when only inactive items reference it** — `app/Services/TaxService.php:83-90 ; app/Services/ItemCategoryService.php:170-183` (logic, code)
  - **What** : TaxService:83 `$checkItem = $tax->items->whereNull('deleted_at');` — but Tax::items() (app/Models/Tax.php:21) filters `where('status' => ACTIVE)`. Logic: `if (!blank($checkItem)) { $tax->delete(); } else { FK_CHECKS=0; $tax->delete(); FK_CHECKS=1; }`. So when ACTIVE items exist -> plain delete -> blocked by FK (items.tax_id constrained to taxes, migration create_items_table.php:22). When NO active items but INACTIVE items still reference the tax -> `else` branch disables FK checks and HARD-deletes, leaving `items.tax_id` pointing at a non-existent tax (Tax has no SoftDeletes). Identical inverted pattern in ItemCategoryService:170 (items.item_category_id FK, create_items_table.php:21).
  - **Why it matters** : Orphaned tax_id/item_category_id FKs corrupt referential integrity; a later read of an inactive item then references a deleted tax, and pricing (PricingService:244 `$taxObj->tax_rate`) silently falls back to 0% VAT for that item — NF525-relevant under-taxation. The FK-bypass is applied in exactly the wrong branch.
  - **Reproduce** : Create a tax, attach it to an item, set the item status=INACTIVE (so Tax::items() returns empty), then DELETE the tax -> `else` branch fires, FK checks disabled, tax hard-deleted, item.tax_id now dangling. Verify orphan: `SELECT i.id,i.tax_id FROM items i LEFT JOIN taxes t ON t.id=i.tax_id WHERE i.tax_id IS NOT NULL AND t.id IS NULL`.
  - **Correction** : Invert the branches (only bypass FK when there are NO references) AND/OR null the dependent FK before delete. Better: block deletion (422) when any item — active or inactive — references the tax/category, instead of disabling FK_CHECKS. Use Item::withTrashed()->where('tax_id',...)->exists() for the real reference check.
  - **RED verdict** : Tax orphan real ItemCategory neutralized by soft deletes narrow trigger

- **[P3] tax_rate accepts up to 9 999 999 999 999 with no sane upper bound (no max:100) — a typo corrupts VAT/pricing/Z-report** — `app/Http/Requests/TaxRequest.php:42` (nf525, code)
  - **What** : `'tax_rate' => ['required','numeric','min:0','max:9999999999999']`. No `max:100` (or reasonable VAT ceiling). The value flows straight into pricing: PricingService.php:244 `$taxRate = (float)$taxObj->tax_rate`, then OrderService:520 / FrontendOrderService:436 snapshot it on order_items.tax_rate, and ZReportService aggregates `total_by_tax_rate` (ZReportService:455).
  - **Why it matters** : An admin fat-fingering '200' instead of '20' (or '2000') is accepted and immediately recomputes VAT on every new order at an absurd rate, polluting the NF525 Z-report tax buckets. VAT rate is a NF525-sensitive field; the validation provides no guardrail. (Note: status rule on the same form, TaxRequest:43, is `['required','numeric','max:24']` with no `min`/`in:[active,inactive]` — secondary laxity.)
  - **Reproduce** : POST /api/admin/tax with tax_rate=200 -> accepted (passes `max:9999999999999`). Create a new order with an item on that tax -> order_items.tax_rate=200, tax_amount = 2x the HT.
  - **Correction** : Tighten to `['required','numeric','min:0','max:100']` for a percentage VAT; constrain status to `Rule::in([Status::ACTIVE, Status::INACTIVE])`. Optionally warn the admin when editing a rate that already has historical orders (history is protected by snapshot, but UX clarity helps).
  - **RED verdict** : no upper bound but history snapshot immutable latent

- **[P3] Category drag-sort assigns sort=index+1 from the visible page only — collides across paginated pages** — `resources/js/components/admin/settings/ItemCategory/ItemCateogryListComponent.vue:238-248 ; app/Services/ItemCategoryService.php:211-235` (logic, code)
  - **What** : List is paginated (`paginate:1, per_page:50`, :158-164). sortCategory() (:239) posts `this.categories.map(c => c.id)` = only the current page's rows. Backend sortCategory (ItemCategoryService:221) does `update(['sort' => $index + 1])` starting at 1 for the first posted id. Sorting on page 2 therefore reassigns sort=1.. to page-2 categories, colliding with page-1 categories' sort values.
  - **Why it matters** : Reordering categories beyond the first page produces duplicate/incorrect sort ranks, so the catalog order (POS/kiosk category bar driven by `order_column:'sort'`) becomes inconsistent. V1-LATENT: Le Cayenne has ~10 categories under a 50/page limit, so it never reaches page 2 today — but the algorithm is incorrect if the catalog grows.
  - **Reproduce** : Seed 60 categories, go to page 2, drag a row -> backend sets its sort to 1, duplicating page-1's first category sort. Verify: `SELECT sort, COUNT(*) FROM item_categories GROUP BY sort HAVING COUNT(*) > 1`.
  - **Correction** : Compute the global base offset (e.g. (page-1)*per_page) on the client, or send the full ordered id list, or have the backend reorder using a contiguous renumber across all rows rather than 1-based per submitted batch.
  - **RED verdict** : sort collides across pages never reaches second page latent

- **[P3] Category Excel import bypasses the CategoryCreated event fan-out — imported categories may not sync to POS/kiosk** — `app/Imports/ItemCategoryImport.php:23-29 ; app/Services/ItemCategoryService.php:118-120` (logic, code)
  - **What** : Manual create goes through ItemCategoryService::store() which fires `event(new CategoryCreated($id))` after commit (ItemCategoryService:118-120), feeding the settings/catalog sync outbox. The bulk import path (ItemCategoryImport::model(), :23) builds `new ItemCategory([...])` directly via Maatwebsite ToModel — no CategoryCreated event, no slug/hierarchy validation through the service, and none of the catalog config columns (wizard_template, has_menu, kiosk_upsell_*) are set (DB defaults only).
  - **Why it matters** : Imported categories won't trigger the same real-time cache/outbox fan-out that single-create does, so POS/kiosk surfaces can show stale category lists until another event or manual refresh. Low impact in V1 (import is rarely used on a single-restaurant catalog) but it's an inconsistency between the two creation paths.
  - **Reproduce** : Import an .xlsx with a new category row; observe no CategoryCreated domain event emitted (grep DomainEvent/outbox table after import) versus a manual create which emits one.
  - **Correction** : Route the import through ItemCategoryService::store() per row (or dispatch CategoryCreated in a WithEvents/afterImport hook) so both creation paths share the same event fan-out and column defaults.
  - **RED verdict** : import skips event slug subclaim wrong latent

- **[P3] LoyaltySetupRequest & ItemCategoryImportRequest authorize() return true (defense-in-depth gap)** — `app/Http/Requests/LoyaltySetupRequest.php:11 ; app/Http/Requests/ItemCategoryImportRequest.php:14` (security, code)
  - **What** : Both FormRequests `authorize() { return true; }`. Controller middleware still protects the routes: LoyaltySetupController:19 applies `permission:settings` on index+update; ItemCategory import route is inside the admin settings group. So not currently exploitable. Contrast with TaxRequest:20 / CurrencyRequest:20 which were R7-healed to `$this->user()?->can('settings')`.
  - **Why it matters** : Inconsistent with the FormRequest authz hardening doctrine (CLAUDE.md §9 FormRequestAuthzDriftSentinel). Any future route refactor that drops the controller middleware would leave loyalty-rate edits (which feed POS redemption value via Settings::group('loyalty_setup')) and bulk category import unguarded at the request layer.
  - **Reproduce** : Static: grep `return true;` in these two requests; confirm protection comes only from controller middleware, not the request.
  - **Correction** : Mirror the Tax/Currency pattern: `return $this->user()?->can('settings') ?? false;` in both requests (and lower the FormRequestAuthz sentinel baseline accordingly per CLAUDE.md §9).
  - **RED verdict** : authorize returns true but middleware protects defense in depth only

**Dropped by adversarial pass:**
- ~~[P3] No warning when editing a live tax rate mid-service (history protected, operator not informed)~~ — UX wish not corruption snapshotted drop

**Coverage gaps (RED):**
- antipattern is systemic across many form requests
- ItemAttribute request has same bug and was unfindinged
- ItemCategory request also self collides on name
- no authorization check examined on global catalog reads
- loyalty bounds not probed against redemption math

### S7-settings-business — S7-settings-business
Files examined: `resources/js/components/admin/settings/Branch/BranchCreateComponent.vue`, `resources/js/components/admin/settings/Company/CompanyComponent.vue`, `resources/js/components/admin/settings/OrderSetup/OrderSetupComponent.vue`, `resources/js/components/admin/settings/Site/SiteComponent.vue`, `resources/js/components/admin/settings/TimeSlot/TimeSlotComponent.vue`, `resources/js/components/admin/settings/TimeSlot/TimeSlotCreateComponent.vue`, `app/Http/Controllers/Admin/BranchController.php`, `app/Http/Controllers/Admin/OrderSetupController.php`, `app/Http/Controllers/Admin/TimeSlotController.php`, `app/Http/Controllers/Admin/SiteController.php`, `app/Services/BranchService.php`, `app/Services/SiteService.php`, `app/Services/TimeSlotService.php`, `app/Http/Requests/BranchRequest.php`, `app/Http/Requests/OrderSetupRequest.php`, `app/Http/Requests/TimeSlotRequest.php`, `app/Http/Requests/SiteRequest.php`, `app/Http/Requests/OrderRequest.php`, `app/Http/Requests/PosOrderRequest.php`, `app/Services/Receipt/ReceiptDataService.php`, `app/Http/Resources/OrderDetailsResource.php`, `app/Models/Branch.php`, `app/Providers/AppServiceProvider.php`, `database/migrations/2026_04_20_210000_add_fiscal_identity_to_branches.php`

**Confirmed corrections:**

- **[P2] Branch fiscal identity (SIRET / TVA intra / register_id / legal_footer) has NO settings UI and is never seeded — receipt prints them blank** — `resources/js/components/admin/settings/Branch/BranchCreateComponent.vue:14-118 (form) + app/Http/Requests/BranchRequest.php:34-46 (rules) + app/Services/Receipt/ReceiptDataService.php:66-69 (consumer)` (nf525, code)
  - **What** : Migration 2026_04_20_210000_add_fiscal_identity_to_branches.php:12-23 adds nullable columns siret/vat_intra/register_id/legal_footer to branches. Branch.php:18 has them in $fillable. ReceiptDataService.php:66-69 reads them onto every receipt as pos_siret/pos_vat_intra/pos_register_id/pos_legal_footer, surfaced by ReceiptComponent.vue:67. BUT: (a) BranchCreateComponent.vue form (lines 14-118) and its reset/save payloads (lines 216-227, 246-257) contain ONLY name/email/phone/lat/long/city/state/zip/address/status — no fiscal inputs; (b) BranchRequest::rules() (lines 34-46) has no rule for any of the 4 columns, so even a hand-crafted POST is dropped by $request->validated() in BranchService::store/update; (c) grep of the entire resources/js tree returns ONLY i18n label strings (fr.json:532/589/594) and the read-only ReceiptComponent.vue:67 — zero <input> bound to these fields; (d) grep of database/seeders returns 0 hits — nothing populates them. Net: the columns are NULL forever and the receipt omits the mandatory mentions.
  - **Why it matters** : NF525 / French law (art. 286 CGI) requires the établissement's SIRET, TVA intracommunautaire and a register/caisse identifier on every customer ticket. The renderer is wired to print them, but because there is no admin surface to set them and no seed, every ticket Le Cayenne hands a customer is legally non-conform. This is the direct root of the receipt-omission P1 raised by the POS audit: it is not a printer bug, it is a missing settings field. An inspection (contrôle fiscal) can fine on a blank ticket.
  - **Reproduce** : Admin > Settings > Branches > edit Le Cayenne: no SIRET/TVA/N° caisse field exists. Place any POS/kiosk order, print receipt: pos_siret/pos_vat_intra/pos_register_id are null (optional($order->branch)->siret on a branch whose column was never written).
  - **Correction** : Add siret/vat_intra/register_id/legal_footer inputs to BranchCreateComponent.vue (and BranchShow), add their validation rules to BranchRequest (e.g. siret digits:14 nullable, vat_intra regex FR, register_id/legal_footer string max). For immediate V1 single-box compliance, also seed branch_id=1 with Le Cayenne's real values. No frozen-zone touch (BranchService/Request/Vue are all editable).
  - **RED verdict** : VERIFIED. BranchRequest::rules() (app/Http/Requests/BranchRequest.php:33-47) has NO rule for siret/vat_intra/register_id/legal_footer. BranchCreateComponent.vue form (lines 14-130) binds only name/lat/long/email/phone/city/state/zip_code/status/address; reset payload (216-227) and save payload (246-258) omit all 4 fiscal fields. ReceiptDataService.php:66-69 reads optional(order->branch)->{register_id,siret,vat_intra,legal_footer} onto every ticket; ReceiptComponent.vue:67-71 + posReceiptBuilder.js:121 render them. grep database/seeders = ZERO hits. Verified the Company settings panel is NOT an alternative source: CompanyComponent.vue holds only company_name/email/phone/website/city/state/country/zip/address (no SIRET/VAT), and ReceiptDataService reads branch columns ONLY (no Settings::group('company')). So there is no admin surface anywhere to populate the 4 columns and no seed -> they are NULL forever, receipt omits mandatory NF525/art.286 CGI mentions. Sufficient proof = no-UI + no-seed; the validated()-strips-them mechanism is moot (form never sends the fields). Matches known owner gap 'branche siret/vat vides'. Real P1: legally non-conform ticket, root of the receipt-omission P1.
  - **Self-audit re-rating** : P1 → P2
  - **Self-audit note** : Self-audit reframe + downgrade P1->P2: the 'receipt prints SIRET/TVA blank' impact is now STALE — branch 1 fiscal identity is populated (E.DELICE SAS: SIRET 10417050100019 / TVA FR19104170501 / register CAISSE-01 / legal_footer), verified live. The REAL residual is durability/UX: there is no admin Settings UI to set fiscal identity; it was set by the foodking:set-branch-legal CLI, so a reseed/migrate:fresh would blank it again with no in-app way to restore.

- **[P2] TimeSlot overlap guard misses full-containment and exact-duplicate slots — overlapping opening hours can be saved** — `app/Services/TimeSlotService.php:54-61` (logic, code)
  - **What** : store() overlap check has exactly 3 conditions (lines 55,57,59): (1) new is strictly inside existing; (2) new opening falls inside existing; (3) new closing falls inside existing. For an existing slot [10:00,12:00]: a new slot [09:00,13:00] that FULLY CONTAINS it passes all three (09>10 false; 09>10 false; 13>10 && 13<12 false) -> status stays true -> created. An EXACT DUPLICATE [10:00,12:00] also passes (10>10 false on every comparison). Both are real overlaps the guard fails to reject.
  - **Why it matters** : Opening hours feed FrontendTimeSlotService::todayTimeSlot (FrontendTimeSlotService.php:29) which generates the customer-facing schedule-order slot picker. Overlapping/duplicate time slots produce duplicated or contradictory pickup windows, and a containing slot silently widens hours the owner thinks are bounded. This is precisely the 'flou' (logic blur) the owner ranks first: a validation that looks complete but leaks the two simplest overlap cases.
  - **Reproduce** : Settings > Time Slots: add Mon 10:00-12:00, save. Add Mon 09:00-13:00 (or 10:00-12:00 again) -> saves without the 'time_slot_exist' error instead of being rejected.
  - **Correction** : Replace the 3-branch check with the canonical interval-overlap test: reject when (new.opening < existing.closing) AND (new.closing > existing.opening). That single condition covers partial overlap, containment (both directions) and exact duplicates.
  - **RED verdict** : VERIFIED at app/Services/TimeSlotService.php:55,57,59. store() overlap guard has exactly 3 conditions: (1) new strictly inside existing (opening>existing.opening && closing<existing.closing); (2) new.opening inside existing; (3) new.closing inside existing. For existing [10:00,12:00]: full-container [09:00,13:00] -> 09>10=F, 09>10=F, 13>10&&13<12=F -> status stays true -> CREATED. Exact duplicate [10:00,12:00] -> 10>10=F on every comparison -> CREATED. Both are genuine overlaps the guard fails to reject. Feeds FrontendTimeSlotService customer slot picker -> duplicate/contradictory windows + silently-widened hours. Canonical fix (new.opening<existing.closing && new.closing>existing.opening) covers all cases. Real P2 logic hole.

- **[P1] Site settings exposes an 'App Debug -> Enable' toggle that writes APP_DEBUG=true to .env — self-inflicted prod boot failure / debug leak** — `resources/js/components/admin/settings/Site/SiteComponent.vue:331-354 (toggle) -> app/Services/SiteService.php:48 (.env write)` (security, code)
  - **What** : SiteComponent.vue:331-354 renders an Enable/Disable radio bound to form.site_app_debug. On save (SiteComponent.vue:535 -> site/save) SiteService::update writes to .env via EnvEditor: line 48 'APP_DEBUG' => $request->site_app_debug == Activity::ENABLE ? 'true' : 'false', then Artisan::call('optimize:clear') (line 64). AppServiceProvider.php:202-208 confirms the consequence in its own comments (lines 193-208: 'ALLOWS admin-UI writing of APP_DEBUG into .env ... refuses to boot when APP_DEBUG=true ... If APP_DEBUG was set true via the admin UI (SiteService), revoke') and throws RuntimeException at line 203 when config('app.debug') is true under app()->environment('production') (gate at line 158).
  - **Why it matters** : One admin mis-click on a settings screen rewrites the production .env: in production the NF525 boot guard then REFUSES TO BOOT, taking POS + Kiosk + KDS offline mid-service (self-inflicted DoS during a shift). In any non-prod/un-guarded run it leaks stack traces, SQL and secrets to customers. A destructive, irreversible-from-the-UI toggle sitting in plain Site settings is exactly an 'unnecessary / not good' control the owner wants removed; the codebase authors documented the footgun but left the toggle.
  - **Reproduce** : Settings > Site > App Debug -> Enable -> Save. .env now has APP_DEBUG=true. On next boot in production AppServiceProvider throws; in dev, any error page shows full stack/SQL.
  - **Correction** : Remove the App Debug radio from SiteComponent.vue and stop writing APP_DEBUG from SiteService::update (drop the APP_DEBUG key from the addData array, line 48). Debug state is an ops/deploy concern, never a runtime admin toggle. No frozen-zone touch.
  - **RED verdict** : VERIFIED but finding's 'leaks stack/SQL/secrets TO CUSTOMERS in production' prong is FACTUALLY WRONG and corrected here. SiteComponent.vue:331-354 renders the App Debug Enable/Disable radio (form.site_app_debug); SiteService.php:48 writes APP_DEBUG='true'/'false' to .env via envService->addData, then Artisan optimize:clear (line 64). AppServiceProvider.php:202-208 throws RuntimeException when config('app.debug')==true under app()->environment('production') (gate line 158). CORRECTED RISK: in production the boot guard REFUSES TO BOOT after debug=true is saved + cache cleared -> app hard-DOWN mid-service (self-inflicted DoS taking POS/Kiosk/KDS offline). The stack/SQL/secret leak only materializes in non-prod/un-guarded runs, NOT against production customers (the guard blocks that). Documented V1.0.2 backlog (M-P0-D/E/F per AppServiceProvider:198-206 comment) WITH compensating boot guard. Still real: a destructive env-rewriting toggle in plain Site settings should be removed. P1 retained on the DoS severity (mid-shift outage); compensating guard makes P2 also defensible -- P1 kept as deliberate call given outage blast radius.
  - **Self-audit note** : Self-audit: keep P1 but frame as an OPERATIONAL footgun (not an external security breach): the admin 'App Debug -> Enable' toggle writes APP_DEBUG=true to .env; in production the AppServiceProvider boot guard then REFUSES to boot (self-inflicted outage) and in any env it leaks stack/SQL. Especially dangerous during the in-progress cloud cutover.

- **[P2] Order Setup lets admin disable BOTH takeaway and delivery, leaving zero web fulfillment channels with no guard** — `app/Http/Requests/OrderSetupRequest.php:30-31 + app/Http/Requests/OrderRequest.php:238-244` (logic, code)
  - **What** : OrderSetupRequest::rules() (lines 30-31) validates order_setup_takeaway and order_setup_delivery independently as numeric/min:0 — no cross-field rule preventing both = DISABLE. These flags ARE consumed: OrderRequest.php:238-244, PosOrderRequest.php:172-174, TableOrderRequest.php:57-59 each reject the order_type when its channel is DISABLE. With both disabled, every web/POS DELIVERY and TAKEAWAY submit returns the 'This order type is disabled' 422 (OrderRequest.php:239/241) — no remaining channel except the kiosk-token bypass (OrderRequest.php:232-235, allowed regardless of setting).
  - **Why it matters** : An owner toggling both off (or a partial save) silently bricks all web + POS ordering with a generic 'try another order type' error and no warning at the settings screen — the customer sees a dead end, the cashier sees a 422. The asymmetry (kiosk always works, web/POS dies) is non-obvious and hard to diagnose live. A settings form that can put the business in a no-fulfillment state without guarding it is a logic blur the owner ranks high.
  - **Reproduce** : Settings > Order Setup: set Takeaway=Disable and Delivery=Disable, save (passes validation). Attempt a POS takeaway order -> 422 'This order type is disabled'. Kiosk order still succeeds.
  - **Correction** : Add a cross-field rule in OrderSetupRequest (withValidator) rejecting the save when both order_setup_takeaway and order_setup_delivery are DISABLE, with a clear message ('au moins un canal de commande doit rester actif'). Optionally surface the active-channel state in OrderSetupComponent.
  - **RED verdict** : VERIFIED. OrderSetupRequest::rules() (app/Http/Requests/OrderSetupRequest.php:30-31) validates order_setup_takeaway and order_setup_delivery independently (required/numeric/min:0); grep confirms NO withValidator/after cross-field rule -> both can be saved DISABLE. Flags consumed: OrderRequest.php:238-241, PosOrderRequest.php:172-174, TableOrderRequest.php:57-59 each reject the matching order_type when DISABLE. Both-disabled scenario: explicit order_type (DELIVERY/TAKEAWAY, not blank) hits the specific DISABLE branches -> 422 for all web+POS submits. Kiosk-token path bypasses (OrderRequest.php:233-235 returns before the guard). Net: owner can brick all web+POS ordering with no settings-screen warning while kiosk silently survives -- asymmetric, hard to diagnose live. Real P2.

- **[P3] TimeSlot has NO update path — opening hours can only be created or deleted, not edited** — `app/Http/Controllers/Admin/TimeSlotController.php:20 + app/Services/TimeSlotService.php (no update method)` (functional, code)
  - **What** : TimeSlotController defines only index/store/destroy (no update; middleware line 20 lists only store/destroy). TimeSlotService.php exposes list/store/destroy only — no update method. TimeSlotCreateComponent.vue dispatches timeSlot/save which only creates. To change a single slot's time the owner must delete it and re-add; combined with the S7-02 overlap-guard hole, re-adding a corrected slot that still touches the old one can throw 'time_slot_exist' unexpectedly.
  - **Why it matters** : Editing opening hours is a routine restaurant operation (holiday hours, seasonal changes). Forcing delete+recreate is error-prone (lose the slot, mistype the re-entry) and there is no inline edit affordance — a functional gap that makes a daily settings task clumsy. Lower than the fiscal/security items but a real 'not good' workflow.
  - **Reproduce** : Settings > Time Slots: there is an Add and a Delete action but no Edit. To shift Mon 10:00-12:00 to 11:00-12:00 you must delete and re-create.
  - **Correction** : Add an update endpoint (TimeSlotController::update + TimeSlotService::update) reusing the corrected overlap check, and an edit modal in TimeSlotCreate/List. Scope-minimal, no frozen-zone.
  - **RED verdict** : VERIFIED but OVER-RATED -> downgraded P2->P3. routes/api.php:503-506 registers time-slot GET/POST(store)/DELETE(destroy) only -- NO PUT/PATCH update route. TimeSlotController (app/Http/Controllers/Admin/TimeSlotController.php) defines index/store/destroy only; middleware line 20 covers store+destroy. TimeSlotService grep shows list/store/destroy only, NO update method. To change a slot the owner must delete+recreate. This is a MISSING FEATURE, not a malfunction -- delete+recreate works, nothing is broken. P2 implies something misbehaves; a missing edit affordance is a P3 functional/workflow gap. is_real/is_necessary kept true (worth adding), severity lowered to P3.

- **[P3] SiteController::update has an identical if/else DEMO branch — both arms run the same code** — `app/Http/Controllers/Admin/SiteController.php:33-37` (deadcode, code)
  - **What** : update() wraps the call in 'if (env('DEMO')) { $resource = new SiteResource($this->siteService->update($request)); } else { $resource = new SiteResource($this->siteService->update($request)); }' — the then-branch and else-branch are byte-identical, so the DEMO check has zero effect. The real DEMO gating actually lives inside SiteService::update (line 58 getValue('DEMO')) for the map key, making this controller-level check dead.
  - **Why it matters** : Dead conditional that reads as if DEMO mode changes behavior here when it does not — misleads the next maintainer and calls env('DEMO') on a hot settings-save path for nothing. Low impact but exactly the kind of 'unnecessary thing' the owner flagged.
  - **Reproduce** : Read SiteController.php:33-37; both branches are identical.
  - **Correction** : Delete the if/else and keep a single line: $resource = new SiteResource($this->siteService->update($request));
  - **RED verdict** : VERIFIED at app/Http/Controllers/Admin/SiteController.php:33-37. update() wraps the call in if(env('DEMO')){ $resource = new SiteResource($this->siteService->update($request)); } else { $resource = new SiteResource($this->siteService->update($request)); } -- both branches byte-identical, so the DEMO check has ZERO effect (genuinely dead conditional; real DEMO gating lives in SiteService::update line 58 getValue('DEMO')). Conscious call: by strict 'really must be done' this is a no-op cleanup and a DROP candidate, but it is correctly stated and correctly low, and it calls env('DEMO') on the hot settings-save path for nothing while misleading the next maintainer. Kept as P3 dead-code cleanup (deliberate, not reflexive). Trivial 4-line deletion.
