- SiteService env-write surface BEYOND APP_DEBUG: the same admin UI writes TIMEZONE/CURRENCY/CURRENCY_SYMBOL/CURRENCY_POSITION/CURRENCY_DECIMAL_POINT/DATE_FORMAT/TIME_FORMAT (SiteService.php:49-55) and MIX_GOOGLE_MAP_KEY (line 60) to .env. Only the app_debug key was examined; the broader admin-driven .env-edit attack surface (EnvEditor allowlist) is unaudited.
- OrderSetup non-toggle fields (order_setup_food_preparation_time, schedule_order_slot_duration, free_delivery_kilometer, basic_delivery_charge, charge_per_kilo) are validated only min:0 with no sane upper bound -- a huge/garbage prep time or delivery charge can be saved; unaudited.
- Branch UPDATE / updateZone path: only the create form/payload was read. Whether BranchService::store/update would even persist the fiscal fields if BranchRequest rules + form inputs were added (and BranchScope/admin-bypass implications) is unverified.
- TimeSlotRequest validation (opening_time/closing_time format, opening<closing ordering, day enum) was not examined -- the overlap guard (S7-02) is only one layer; a malformed or inverted slot could pass before overlap logic even runs.

### S8-settings-payments — S8-settings-payments
Files examined: `resources/js/components/admin/settings/PaymentTerminals/PaymentTerminalsComponent.vue`, `resources/js/components/admin/settings/PaymentGateway/PaymentGatewayComponent.vue`, `resources/js/components/admin/settings/KioskMachine/KioskMachineCreateComponent.vue`, `resources/js/components/admin/settings/KioskMachine/KioskMachineListComponent.vue`, `resources/js/components/admin/settings/KioskMachine/KioskMachineComponent.vue`, `resources/js/components/admin/settings/KioskSetup/KioskSetupComponent.vue`, `resources/js/components/admin/pos/PaymentComponent.vue`, `resources/js/store/modules/paymentTerminal.js`, `resources/js/store/modules/kioskMachine.js`, `resources/js/router/modules/settingRoutes.js`, `app/Http/Controllers/Admin/PaymentTerminalController.php`, `app/Http/Controllers/Admin/PaymentGatewayController.php`, `app/Http/Controllers/Admin/KioskMachineController.php`, `app/Http/Controllers/Auth/KioskMachineLoginController.php`, `app/Services/KioskMachineService.php`, `app/Services/KioskSetupService.php`, `app/Http/Requests/Admin/PaymentTerminalRequest.php`, `app/Http/Requests/KioskMachineRequest.php`, `app/Http/Requests/PosOrderRequest.php`, `app/Http/Resources/PaymentTerminalResource.php`, `app/Http/Resources/KioskMachineResource.php`, `app/Http/Resources/PaymentGatewayResource.php`, `app/Http/Resources/GatewayOptionsResource.php`, `app/Models/PaymentTerminal.php`, `app/Models/Scopes/BranchScope.php`, `app/Services/Payments/SplitPaymentService.php`, `database/migrations/2026_05_16_120000_create_payment_terminals_table.php`, `routes/api.php`

**Confirmed corrections:**

- **[P1] TPE créé via Settings prend branch_id=0 (admin) → invisible/refusé à la caisse de la branche → impossible d'encaisser par carte** — `resources/js/components/admin/settings/PaymentTerminals/PaymentTerminalsComponent.vue:202-211 (defaultForm sans branch_id) + app/Http/Controllers/Admin/PaymentTerminalController.php:108-117 (resolveBranchId)` (logic, code)
  - **What** : Le modal de création/édition (PaymentTerminalsComponent.vue, defaultForm L202-211 et openEdit L228-239) ne contient AUCUN champ branch_id — le formulaire n'envoie jamais branch_id. Côté backend resolveBranchId() (PaymentTerminalController.php:108) retourne `$request->validated('branch_id')` SEULEMENT si l'utilisateur a branch_id>0 ; pour l'admin (branch_id=0, cas normal en V1 mono-poste Le Cayenne) il retourne validated('branch_id') qui est absent ⇒ (int)null = 0. Le terminal est donc créé avec branch_id=0. Or PosOrderRequest.php:235-242 et SplitPaymentService.php:129-138 exigent pour chaque tranche CARD un terminal où `->where('branch_id', $orderBranchId)` avec orderBranchId = branch_id de la commande (=1 pour Le Cayenne). De plus PaymentComponent.vue:510 charge `admin/payment-terminals` filtré par BranchScope : un caissier staff (branch_id=1) ne verra JAMAIS le terminal branch_id=0 (BranchScope.php:43 `where(field, userBranch)`), et même un admin caissier qui le voit verra la tranche CARD refusée car branch_id 0 != 1.
  - **Why it matters** : C'est exactement la douleur owner « re-associer les TPE ». Un TPE configuré dans l'écran Paramètres → Terminaux de paiement par l'admin est inutilisable en caisse : toute tranche CARTE est rejetée avec « The selected payment terminal is not available for this branch » et le caissier ne peut pas encaisser par carte. Blocage du flux d'encaissement principal du fast-food.
  - **Reproduce** : 1) Login admin. 2) Paramètres → Terminaux de paiement → Ajouter → remplir nom/gateway → Enregistrer. 3) SELECT branch_id FROM payment_terminals → 0. 4) En caisse, choisir paiement CARTE : POST /admin/pos-order rejeté 422 sur payment_breakdown.0.terminal_id car branch_id(0) != order branch_id(1).
  - **Correction** : Ajouter un sélecteur de branche dans le modal (visible/obligatoire quand l'utilisateur courant est admin branch_id=0, comme le fait KioskMachineCreateComponent.vue:67-78), OU faire que resolveBranchId() retombe sur la branche par défaut V1 (config branche unique) au lieu de 0 quand aucune branche n'est fournie, OU valider 'branch_id' required pour l'admin. Cohérent avec le module Kiosk Machine qui, lui, expose bien branch_id.
  - **RED verdict** : CONFIRMED end-to-end. PaymentTerminalsComponent.vue defaultForm (L203-211) and openEdit (L228-241) carry NO branch_id field -> store payload omits branch_id. PaymentTerminalController.php resolveBranchId (L96-105) returns (int)validated('branch_id')=0 for admin (User branch_id=0 verified via tinker). Created terminal => branch_id=0 (live DB: existing 'TPE Le Cayenne #1' is branch=1, all 3484 orders branch=1). POS order branch_id comes from defaultAccess/show which falls back to site_default_branch=1 (LoginController L75-88, DefaultAccessService L63+, Settings site_default_branch=1 verified). PosOrderRequest.php L215-243 validates CARD tranche terminal where('branch_id',orderBranchId=1) -> a branch_id=0 terminal is REJECTED ('not available for this branch'). PaymentTerminal uses BranchScope (model L69) so staff cashier (branch=1) never even sees branch_id=0 row in index. Card encashment blocked for any Settings-created terminal. KioskMachineCreateComponent.vue L64-69 correctly exposes a branch selector -> the fix pattern already exists in the sibling module. Real P1 blocking card payment + secondary Z-report fee misattribution risk.
  - **Self-audit note** : Self-audit citation fix: add app/Services/Payments/SplitPaymentService.php to the citation. Defect and links unchanged — TPE created via Settings can take branch_id=0 (resolveBranchId returns validated branch_id for an admin, PaymentTerminalController.php:104) and is then invisible to the branch caisse. DIRECTLY relevant to the owner's terminal-association task: terminals must be assigned to the branch (branch_id=1), not left admin-global.

- **[P2] Logout / Désactivation / Suppression d'une borne kiosk côté Admin ne révoque PAS le token Sanctum kiosk:order — la borne peut continuer à commander** — `app/Services/KioskMachineService.php:179-191 (logout) + 108-124 (destroy) + 145-167 (changeStatus)` (security, code)
  - **What** : KioskMachineLoginController.php:97-103 crée un token Sanctum `kiosk-token` avec l'ability ['kiosk:order'] et TTL config('sanctum.expiration',480)=8h. Côté admin, KioskMachineService::logout() (L179-191) fait UNIQUEMENT `$kioskMachine->update(['is_login' => Ask::NO])` + un push FCM ; il n'appelle jamais `$user->tokens()->...->delete()`. destroy() (L108-124) fait `$kioskMachine->delete()` sans révoquer le token. changeStatus() vers INACTIVE (L145-167) ne révoque pas le token non plus. Seul le re-login (KioskMachineLoginController.php:95) révoque les anciens tokens.
  - **Why it matters** : L'owner re-associe activement ses bornes. Quand il clique « Logout » sur une borne, ou la désactive/supprime, il croit couper son accès — mais le token kiosk:order reste valide jusqu'à 8h et la borne peut continuer à POSTer des commandes (fiscalisées) au nom de l'ancien user. Contrôle de sécurité illusoire : un kiosk débranché/réaffecté garde l'autorité de commander. Risque NF525/cash-trail (commandes attribuées au mauvais opérateur).
  - **Reproduce** : 1) Borne se logue → reçoit token T. 2) Admin → Bornes → Logout (ou toggle inactif, ou supprimer). 3) Avec le token T encore en mémoire, POST /api/... kiosk order → toujours 2xx (token non révoqué, ability kiosk:order intacte). is_login=NO en base mais aucune vérification runtime du flag.
  - **Correction** : Dans logout()/destroy()/changeStatus(INACTIVE), révoquer les tokens kiosk de l'user lié : `User::find($kioskMachine->user_id)?->tokens()->where('name','kiosk-token')->delete()` dans la même transaction, en miroir de KioskMachineLoginController.php:95. Pour changeStatus INACTIVE, ne révoquer que si on passe ACTIVE→INACTIVE.
  - **RED verdict** : CONFIRMED. KioskMachineService.php logout() L176-191 only update(['is_login'=>Ask::NO]) at L190; destroy() L108-124 only kioskMachine->delete() at L122; changeStatus() L147-167 only update(['status'=>...]) at L152 — none call User->tokens()->delete(). KioskMachineLoginController.php createToken('kiosk-token',['kiosk:order'], 8h) L97-101 and only re-login revokes (L96 user->tokens()->where('name','kiosk-token')->delete()). Kiosk order route routes/api.php L1315 (FrontendOrderController store) enforces only tokenCan('kiosk:order') — NO runtime is_login/status check. Login-time status!==ACTIVE check (LoginController L65) does not affect an already-issued token. So after admin Logout/Disable/Delete, the existing token stays valid up to 8h and can keep POSTing fiscalized orders attributed to the old user. Security-control illusion + NF525 operator-attribution risk. Real P1.
  - **Self-audit re-rating** : P1 → P2
  - **Self-audit note** : Self-audit re-rating P1->P2 + citation fix: scope the token-revocation gap to the logout()/is_login path (controller app/Http/Controllers/Auth/KioskMachineLoginController.php); destroy + changeStatus are already server-enforced, so remove them from the vector. The real residual: logging out / deactivating a borne from Admin does not revoke its active Sanctum kiosk:order token (TTL 480min) until expiry.

- **[P2] PaymentTerminalRequest::authorize() retourne true — la validation/écriture des TPE n'est gardée que par le middleware contrôleur ; index/show non gardés exposent fees + serial** — `app/Http/Requests/Admin/PaymentTerminalRequest.php:13-16 (authorize return true) + app/Http/Controllers/Admin/PaymentTerminalController.php:27 (middleware only store/update/destroy) + routes/api.php:954-959` (functional, code)
  - **What** : PaymentTerminalRequest.php:13 `authorize(): bool { return true; }` — contrairement à KioskMachineRequest.php:authorize qui fait `$this->user()?->can('settings')`. La seule barrière est PaymentTerminalController.__construct (L27) `middleware(['permission:settings'])->only(['store','update','destroy'])`. Donc index (L955) et show (L957) ne sont PAS gardés par permission:settings : tout user authentifié (ex: POS Operator sans 'settings') peut GET /admin/payment-terminals et lire name/gateway_type/fee_percent/fee_fixed/serial_number (PaymentTerminalResource.php:10-22).
  - **Why it matters** : Incohérence avec la doctrine FormRequest authz du projet (CLAUDE.md §9 — sentinel FormRequestAuthzDriftSentinelTest cible justement les `return true;`) et avec PaymentGatewayController qui a explicitement gaté son index en SET-01 (heal 2026-06-01) car il expose des secrets. Ici les taux négociés par TPE et numéros de série fuient à tout staff connecté. Note: PaymentComponent.vue (caisse) a légitimement besoin de lire index — donc gater index casserait la caisse pour les caissiers sans 'settings' ; la correction doit donc être nuancée (voir reco).
  - **Reproduce** : 1) Login user avec rôle sans permission 'settings' (ex: POS Operator). 2) GET /api/admin/payment-terminals → 200 avec fee_percent/fee_fixed/serial_number. 3) Comparer : POST/PUT renvoient 403 (gardés), mais la lecture passe.
  - **Correction** : Aligner authorize() sur KioskMachineRequest (`return $this->user()?->can('settings') ?? false`) pour les mutations. Pour la lecture index par la caisse, NE PAS gater index sur 'settings' (la caisse en dépend) mais restreindre PaymentTerminalResource au strict nécessaire pour le POS (id, name, gateway_type, status) et omettre fee_percent/fee_fixed/serial pour les appelants non-'settings', ou créer une resource caisse séparée. Vérifier le sentinel authz drift.
  - **RED verdict** : CONFIRMED. PaymentTerminalRequest.php authorize() L11-14 returns true (vs KioskMachineRequest.php L19-22 returns user()?->can('settings')??false). PaymentTerminalController.php __construct L26-27 middleware permission:settings ->only(['store','update','destroy']) — index/show NOT gated (routes/api.php L955 index, L957 show). PaymentTerminalResource exposes fee_percent/fee_fixed/serial_number. Any authenticated staff without 'settings' (e.g. POS Operator) can GET /admin/payment-terminals and read negotiated fees + serials. Mutations are correctly 403 (middleware). Caveat is correct: gating index would break PaymentComponent.vue fetchPaymentTerminals (L509+) for non-settings cashiers, so the right fix is authorize() on mutations + a restricted cashier resource. Info-disclosure, V1 mono-poste low blast radius. P2 stands.

- **[P3] KioskMachine changeStatus passe par Request brut (pas KioskMachineRequest) — status non borné/non typé peut être écrit** — `app/Http/Controllers/Admin/KioskMachineController.php:82-92 (changeStatus(KioskMachine, Request)) + app/Services/KioskMachineService.php:145-167` (functional, code)
  - **What** : changeStatus() du contrôleur (L82) reçoit un Illuminate\Http\Request brut, pas KioskMachineRequest. Le service (KioskMachineService.php:148-150) fait `if ($request->filled('status')) { $kioskMachine->update(['status' => $request->input('status')]); }` sans validation de plage. KioskMachineRequest.php:rules borne status à `numeric|max:24` mais ce FormRequest n'est PAS appliqué sur la route change-status (routes/api.php:531 utilise le contrôleur, le contrôleur prend Request). Le toggle UI (KioskMachineListComponent.vue:268-271) n'envoie que ACTIVE/INACTIVE, donc bénin en pratique, mais l'endpoint accepte n'importe quelle valeur.
  - **Why it matters** : Le permission:settings protège l'accès (L23), donc pas un trou de sécurité externe, mais c'est un flou de validation : un status arbitraire écrit casserait la logique de login borne (KioskMachineLoginController.php:64 compare `status !== Status::ACTIVE` pour bloquer). Une valeur de status corrompue pourrait verrouiller une borne ou la laisser active de façon incohérente — gênant pendant les ré-associations.
  - **Reproduce** : 1) POST /api/admin/setting/kiosk-machine/change-status/{id} body {status: 99}. 2) status=99 écrit en base (aucune validation Rule::in). 3) La borne n'est plus jamais 'active' au sens login.
  - **Correction** : Valider status dans changeStatus via un FormRequest dédié ou un Rule::in([Status::ACTIVE, Status::INACTIVE]) inline avant update. Symétrique au reste du module.
  - **RED verdict** : REAL but over-rated. KioskMachineController.php changeStatus(KioskMachine,Request) L81 takes raw Illuminate Request (not KioskMachineRequest); KioskMachineService.php L150-152 update(['status'=>request->input('status')]) with NO Rule::in. KioskMachineRequest status numeric|max:24 is not applied on change-status (route routes/api.php L531). HOWEVER changeStatus IS gated by permission:settings (controller __construct L22 ->only(...,'changeStatus')) so it is admin-only, NOT an external hole — the finding acknowledges this. UI toggle only emits ACTIVE/INACTIVE. Impact is an internal-only data-integrity hardening (an arbitrary status could lock a kiosk out of login). Defensive-validation nicety, not a user-facing or external-security defect. Downgrade P2->P3.

- **[P3] Liste des bornes kiosk sans état vide (pas de v-else / no_data) — table fantôme si aucune borne** — `resources/js/components/admin/settings/KioskMachine/KioskMachineListComponent.vue:31 (tbody v-if kioskMachines.length>0 sans v-else)` (ux, code)
  - **What** : Le tbody est rendu `v-if="kioskMachines.length > 0"` (L31) mais AUCUN bloc `v-else` ni ligne « no_data » n'existe (grep confirme : seul match kioskMachines.length, aucun v-else/no_data dans le fichier). Par contraste PaymentTerminalsComponent.vue:58-64 a un tbody v-else avec {{ $t('label.no_data') }}.
  - **Why it matters** : Quand l'owner part d'une installation neuve ou supprime toutes ses bornes pour re-associer (scénario actuel décrit par l'owner), l'écran affiche un tableau avec seulement les en-têtes et un vide silencieux — pas de message « aucune borne ». Confusion : l'owner peut croire que la page est cassée ou que ses bornes ont disparu par bug, alors que c'est juste l'état vide.
  - **Reproduce** : 1) Supprimer toutes les bornes. 2) Paramètres → Bornes → Liste : en-têtes de tableau visibles, corps vide, aucun message. Comparer avec Terminaux de paiement qui affiche « Aucune donnée ».
  - **Correction** : Ajouter un `<tbody v-else>` avec une ligne colspan=6 affichant {{ $t('label.no_data') }}, en miroir de PaymentTerminalsComponent.vue:57-64.
  - **RED verdict** : CONFIRMED. KioskMachineListComponent.vue L31 tbody v-if='kioskMachines.length > 0', closes L71, with NO v-else / no_data (grep: zero v-else or no_data in file). PaymentTerminalsComponent.vue L58-64 has the v-else 'no_data' empty-state to mirror. Real UX inconsistency: empty kiosk list shows headers + silent void. Pure cosmetic/UX polish, no functional or data risk. Severity P2 is too high for a missing empty-state row — downgrade to P3.

- **[P3] Champs secrets de gateway (stripe_secret, *_client_secret) rendus en clair à l'écran (input type=text) dans la config Paiement** — `resources/js/components/admin/settings/PaymentGateway/PaymentGatewayComponent.vue:47-48 + app/Http/Resources/GatewayOptionsResource.php:22 (value brut)` (security, code)
  - **What** : PaymentGatewayComponent.vue:47-48 rend chaque option en `<input type="text" :value="paymentGatewayOption.value">` sans distinction pour les secrets. GatewayOptionsResource.php:22 retourne `'value' => $this->value` (valeur brute, incluant les options secrètes). L'accès en lecture EST gaté (PaymentGatewayController.php:25 SET-01 a gaté index sur permission:settings 2026-06-01), donc seul un admin autorisé voit la page — mais une fois la page ouverte, les secrets s'affichent en clair (pas de masque, pas de type=password).
  - **Why it matters** : Risque modéré de shoulder-surfing dans un back-office de restaurant (écran partagé, capture). Pas un P0 car le contrôle d'accès route existe et qu'en V1 Le Cayenne mono-poste l'exposition réseau est nulle. Reste une hygiène de secret à corriger avant cloud (cf. dossier cloud-readiness).
  - **Reproduce** : 1) Admin → Paramètres → Passerelles de paiement → Stripe. 2) Le champ secret affiche la valeur en clair (type=text). Inspecter DOM : value=sk_... lisible.
  - **Correction** : Rendre les champs marqués secret en type=password avec bouton afficher/masquer, et idéalement masquer la valeur côté API (renvoyer un placeholder type ●●●●1234 et n'écrire que si l'admin saisit une nouvelle valeur). Aligner avec la pratique de masquage des credentials.
  - **RED verdict** : CONFIRMED. PaymentGatewayComponent.vue L47-48 renders TEXT options as <input type='text' :value='paymentGatewayOption.value'> with no masking for secrets (no type=password anywhere in file). GatewayOptionsResource.php L22 returns raw 'value' => $this->value. Access IS gated: PaymentGatewayController.php __construct L26 middleware permission:settings ->only('index','update') (SET-01 heal 2026-06-01), so only authorized admin reaches the page — but once open the secret is shown in clear (shoulder-surfing / screen-share). Legitimate secret-hygiene fix (type=password + masked API value) deferrable pre-cloud. V1 mono-poste, no network exposure. P3 + medium confidence as stated is correct.

**Dropped by adversarial pass:**
- ~~[P3] Pré-remplissage du mot de passe à l'édition d'une borne référence un champ inexistant (kioskMachine.password non retourné par la resource)~~ — Underlying claim REAL but cited line is WRONG. Finding cites KioskMachineListComponent.vue:206 and 'edit() (L198-208)'. Actual edit() is at L176-190; the 'password: kioskMachine.password' line is L185 (not 206 — L206 is inside the destroy confirmation block). KioskMachineResource.php toArray (L18-28) confirmed returns NO password key -> kioskMachine.password === undefined -> field blank. This is secure-by-accident dead code (backend treats password as nullable=keep-current). Genuine maintainability smell worth tidying (set password:'' + 'leave blank to keep' hint) but a pure no-op with zero runtime defect. Downgrade P2->P3; file_line_exists:false due to the ~20-line misattribution and wrong method range.
- ~~[P3] Un TPE archivé ne peut pas être réactivé par un parcours évident (bouton supprimer caché si status!=1, réactivation seulement via Éditer → radio)~~ — REAL and accurately described but marginal. PaymentTerminalsComponent.vue destroy/archive button is v-if='terminal.status === 1' (L51) so it hides on archived rows; Edit (L47/openEdit L228) stays visible and the modal radio Active(1)/Archived(5) (L127-140) lets you flip status=1 to reactivate; destroy() (L267+) DELETE -> backend soft-archive status=5 (PaymentTerminalController.php destroy L89-94). The reactivation path exists and is functional, just not labeled 'Reactivate'. Finding itself flags it optional/contournable. Discoverability nit only — is_necessary:false (works-as-intended with a workaround), keep informational at P3.

**Coverage gaps (RED):**
- S8-01 secondary blast radius NOT examined by auditor: a branch_id=0 terminal also breaks fiscal fee attribution — ZReportCashEnrichmentService.php L99-170 groups order_payments by terminal_id for the Z-report by_terminal/fees_total breakdown; a terminal whose branch_id mismatches the order branch falls into the synthetic 'Sans TPE' (NULL) bucket, silently dropping per-TPE fee reporting.
- No test coverage asserted for kiosk token revocation on admin logout/disable/delete (S8-02): tests exist for re-login revocation (RefreshTokenAbilityPreserveTest, PasswordResetRevokesTokensSentinelTest) but none for KioskMachineService::logout/destroy/changeStatus revoking 'kiosk-token' — the regression would ship undetected.
- PaymentGateway save() (PaymentGatewayComponent.vue L135 @submit save(index)) and the gateway activate toggle (selectActive L5/L22) were not audited for whether toggling a gateway active/inactive validates an acquirer is actually configured — risk of activating a card gateway with empty secret fields.
- KioskMachineCreate save() (KioskMachineCreateComponent.vue L187) password handling on UPDATE not audited: confirm a blank password field on edit does NOT overwrite the stored hash with an empty/hashed-empty value (the S8-03 dead-prefill implies edit reuses the create form).
- PaymentTerminal index BranchScope behavior for admin (branch_id=0) cashier not surfaced: admin bypass returns ALL terminals incl. branch_id=0 rows, so the admin sees the broken terminal in the POS dropdown and can SELECT it, only to hit the L215-243 422 — a confusing dead option, not just an invisible one.

### S9-settings-system — S9-settings-system
Files examined: `app/Http/Controllers/Admin/RoleController.php`, `app/Http/Controllers/Admin/PermissionController.php`, `app/Http/Controllers/Admin/LanguageController.php`, `app/Http/Controllers/Admin/ThemeController.php`, `app/Http/Controllers/Admin/NotificationController.php`, `app/Http/Controllers/Admin/MailController.php`, `app/Http/Controllers/Admin/SmsGatewayController.php`, `app/Http/Controllers/Admin/PageController.php`, `app/Http/Controllers/Admin/SliderController.php`, `app/Http/Controllers/Admin/CookiesController.php`, `app/Http/Controllers/Admin/OtpController.php`, `app/Http/Controllers/Admin/SocialMediaController.php`, `app/Http/Controllers/Admin/LicenseController.php`, `app/Http/Controllers/Admin/NotificationAlertController.php`, `app/Services/RoleService.php`, `app/Services/PermissionService.php`, `app/Services/LanguageService.php`, `app/Services/PageService.php`, `app/Http/Requests/RoleRequest.php`, `app/Http/Requests/PermissionRequest.php`, `app/Http/Requests/PageRequest.php`, `app/Http/Resources/NotificationResource.php`, `app/Http/Resources/LicenseResource.php`, `app/Http/Resources/SmsGatewayResource.php`, `app/Http/Resources/GatewayOptionsResource.php`, `app/Http/Resources/OtpResource.php`, `app/Http/Resources/SocialMediaResource.php`, `resources/js/components/admin/settings/Role/RoleShowComponent.vue`, `routes/api.php`

**Confirmed corrections:**

- **[P3] LanguageController::fileText guards with `$explodeName > 0` (array compared to int) — always-true, dead/incorrect branch guard** — `app/Http/Controllers/Admin/LanguageController.php:89` (logic, code)
  - **What** : Line 89-90: `$explodeName = explode('.', $request->name); if ($explodeName > 0) {`. `explode()` returns an array; comparing an array `> 0` in PHP coerces and is effectively always true for a non-empty array, so the intent (guard that the name actually contains a dot before indexing `$explodeName[1]`) is not achieved. The same pattern is duplicated in LanguageService::fileText (app/Services/LanguageService.php:253). If `$request->name` has no dot, `$explodeName[1]` raises an undefined-index notice and the json-vs-other routing is wrong.
  - **Why it matters** : Latent correctness bug in the language-file editor: a name without an extension dot mis-routes the include() branch and emits notices. Gated behind permission:settings so it is admin-only and not a security escalation, hence P3.
  - **Reproduce** : Call the file-text editor with a `name` lacking a `.` — observe the always-true guard and an undefined-index on `$explodeName[1]`. Static/code-level; needs-live-verify for the notice.
  - **Correction** : Replace `if ($explodeName > 0)` with `if (count($explodeName) > 1)` (or `isset($explodeName[1])`) in both LanguageController.php:90 and LanguageService.php:253.
  - **RED verdict** : LanguageController.php:89-90 + LanguageService.php:252-253: explode('.',name) > 0 is always-true (PHP-verified array>int); name without dot => undefined [1] + wrong include routing. Admin-gated. Fix BOTH sites.

- **[P3] LanguageService::fileTextStore allows arbitrary value injection into .php lang files (RCE-adjacent, admin-gated)** — `app/Services/LanguageService.php:279` (security, code)
  - **What** : After validateLangFilePath() (line 275) correctly contains the path to lang/*.php or resources/js/languages/*.json, fileTextStore writes user-supplied `$value` verbatim into the file via str_replace into the existing translation array and file_put_contents (lines 279-290). The replacement wraps values in double quotes (`"{$value}"`) but does not escape `"` or PHP syntax, so a crafted value can break out of the string literal in a .php translation file and inject PHP, which is then executable because fileText() does `include($resolvedPath)` (line 255/257).
  - **Why it matters** : Content-injection-to-RCE gadget in the language editor. Mitigated to P3 because it is gated behind permission:settings (top-admin only, LanguageController.php:27) and the path containment heal (validateLangFilePath) blocks writing outside lang dirs — but writing executable PHP into an includable lang file remains a defense-in-depth gap.
  - **Reproduce** : As settings-admin, POST /admin/setting/language/file-text/store targeting a .php lang file with a value containing a double-quote + PHP payload; then trigger fileText include of the same file. needs-live-verify to confirm the str_replace breakout actually yields valid injected PHP.
  - **Correction** : Escape values before writing (addslashes / var_export the string), or restrict fileTextStore to .json files only and never write into includable .php files; alternatively persist translations to DB rather than rewriting PHP source.
  - **RED verdict** : LanguageService.php:283/285 writes $value in double-quotes unescaped into includable .php lang file (file_put_contents:290; include at :255/257). PHP-verified str_replace breakout yields valid injected PHP. Admin-only (permission:settings) so P3 defense-in-depth, no privilege crossing.

**Dropped by adversarial pass:**
- ~~[P2] NotificationController::index leaks FCM API key to any non-kiosk staff token (ungated index)~~ — NotificationController.php:18 gates only update; index ungated; but NotificationResource.php:29-36 = public Firebase WEB config injected client-side (FrontendNavBarComponent.vue:365, BackendNavbarComponent.vue:344, pos-app.js), and notification_fcm_json_file (line 37) is the inert sentinel 'firebase://service-account-file.json' (NotificationSetting.php:40-49, GOAL-HEAL-SEC-001) — zero real secret leaks. Overclaimed.
- ~~[P3] LicenseController::index exposes product license_key to non-settings staff (ungated index)~~ — LicenseController.php:18 gates only update; index leaks license_key (LicenseResource.php:28) but that value is MIX_API_KEY (LicenseService.php:45) compiled into the public client bundle (env.js:44 process.env.MIX_API_KEY). Already public; gating index is a confidentiality no-op.
- ~~[P3] RoleShowComponent permission toggle removes ids via splice without index fix-up (potential skipped element)~~ — RoleShowComponent.vue:139-145/159-165 forward splice without j-- and append without dedup; only bites on duplicate id (contrived sequence) and is self-correcting (PermissionService syncs array; admin sees checkbox state). Author confidence low. Dormant code smell, not a defect worth a finding.

**Coverage gaps (RED):**
- Pattern 'index ungated, gates only update' enumerated across all settings controllers; the THREE real-secret endpoints (Mail/SmsGateway/PaymentGateway) ALREADY healed to ->only('index','update'). Remaining ungated indexes expose only public config (SiteResource site_google_map_key = referrer-restricted browser key; Company/Theme/SocialMedia/Otp/OrderSetup/Cookies resources carry no secret fields).
- S9-01/S9-02 gating the index is a cosmetic consistency nit only (no secret leaks) — do NOT surface as a security finding.
- S9-03 must be fixed in BOTH LanguageController.php:90 AND LanguageService.php:253.
- S9-04 relies on validateLangFilePath() containment (verified by reference only); its traversal-resistance was not independently re-audited.
- PermissionController.php:33 gates the whole controller (index+mutations) — Role/permission read path is correctly gated; no ungated permission-matrix read remains.

### S10-customers — S10-customers — Customers/CRM + subscribers + admin own-profile
Files examined: `app/Http/Controllers/Admin/CustomerController.php`, `app/Http/Controllers/Admin/CustomerAddressController.php`, `app/Http/Controllers/Admin/SubscriberController.php`, `app/Http/Controllers/Frontend/ProfileController.php`, `app/Services/CustomerService.php`, `app/Services/UserAddressService.php`, `app/Services/SubscriberService.php`, `app/Services/ProfileService.php`, `app/Services/OrderService.php (userOrder)`, `app/Http/Requests/CustomerRequest.php`, `app/Http/Requests/CustomerAddressRequest.php`, `app/Http/Requests/UserChangePasswordRequest.php`, `app/Http/Requests/ChangePasswordRequest.php`, `app/Http/Requests/ChangeImageRequest.php`, `app/Http/Requests/ProfileRequest.php`, `app/Http/Requests/SubscriberEmailRequest.php`, `app/Http/Resources/CustomerResource.php`, `app/Models/Scopes/BranchScope.php`, `routes/api.php (profile/subscriber/customer blocks)`, `resources/js/components/admin/customers/CustomerListComponent.vue`, `resources/js/components/admin/customers/CustomerCreateComponent.vue`, `resources/js/components/admin/customers/CustomerShowComponent.vue`, `resources/js/components/admin/subscribers/SubscriberMailComponent.vue`, `resources/js/components/admin/profile/ProfileChangePasswordComponent.vue`, `resources/js/components/admin/profile/ProfileEditProfileComponent.vue`, `database/seeders/PermissionTableSeeder.php`

**Confirmed corrections:**

- **[P1] CustomerService::show() missing target-role guard — read-side privilege escalation (any User id leaks PII)** — `app/Services/CustomerService.php:128-140 ; route routes/api.php:547 ; gate CustomerController.php:39` (security, code)
  - **What** : The WAVE5-SEC-001 heal added assertTargetRole() to update() (CustomerService.php:98), changePassword() (:196) and changeImage() (:218) to block mutating non-Customer Users. But show() (:128-140) was NOT given the same guard: it only checks `!in_array(EnumRole::CUSTOMER, $this->blockRoles)` which is a constant tautology (blockRoles=[ADMIN], so CUSTOMER is never in it → always true) and returns the route-bound User as-is. Route GET /customer/show/{customer} (routes/api.php:547) implicit-binds ANY User (no Route::bind role scoping — grep confirmed none) and is gated only by `permission:customers_show` (CustomerController.php:39). CustomerResource (CustomerResource.php:20-31) then returns name/email/phone/username. So a Branch-Manager/partial staffer holding customers_show can pass an Admin or staff user id and read their PII. The same read-side asymmetry exists on myOrder (OrderService::userOrder filters only `where('user_id',$user->id)`, OrderService.php:253-254, gated by permission:customers) and on address index (CustomerAddressController.php:25).
  - **Why it matters** : Direct read-side of the exact horizontal/vertical escalation that WAVE5-SEC-001 patched on the write side. The mutator/reader asymmetry is strong code evidence this is an oversight, not intent. Leaks staff/admin email+phone (PII / GDPR-relevant) to any operator with the read permission.
  - **Reproduce** : As a user with role granting only `customers_show` (e.g. Branch Manager), call GET /api/admin/customer/show/{id} with an Admin or Chef user id. Code path returns that user's CustomerResource (PII). Live-verify needs a non-admin role actually holding customers_show.
  - **Correction** : Add `$this->assertTargetRole($customer);` at the top of CustomerService::show() (mirror update/changePassword/changeImage). Apply equivalently before OrderService::userOrder() use in CustomerController::myOrder and before address index, OR scope the {customer} route binding to the Customer role via Route::bind / a scoped implicit binding.
  - **RED verdict** : VERIFIED. CustomerService::show() (CustomerService.php:128-140) returns the route-bound User with NO role guard; its !in_array(EnumRole::CUSTOMER, $blockRoles) check is a tautology (blockRoles=[ADMIN=1], CUSTOMER=2 never in it -> always true; Role.php:7-8). Mutators update():98 / changePassword():196 / changeImage():218 all got assertTargetRole() (defined :180-188) in WAVE5-SEC-001; show() did NOT -> genuine reader/mutator asymmetry. Route GET /customer/show/{customer} (api.php:547) implicit-binds ANY User; no Route::bind/scopeBindings anywhere (grep clean). Gated only by permission:customers_show (CustomerController.php:39). CustomerResource (CustomerResource.php:20-31) leaks name/email/username/phone. REACHABILITY PROVEN: seeded Branch Manager holds customers_show (RolePermissionTableSeeder.php:57) and BranchScope.apply() returns early for User (BranchScope.php:21-23) so a branch_id>0 manager CAN resolve a branch_id=0 admin/staff User id -> reads their PII. P1 upheld: reachable info-disclosure under default RBAC mirroring the exact hole patched on writes.

- **[P3] Customer address write ops (store/update/destroy) gated by a READ permission (customers_show)** — `app/Http/Controllers/Admin/CustomerAddressController.php:22` (security, code)
  - **What** : Constructor middleware: `$this->middleware(['permission:customers_show'])->only('index','store','update','destroy','show');`. The mutating endpoints POST /customer/address/{customer}, PUT/PATCH /customer/address/{customer}/{address}, DELETE /customer/address/{customer}/{address} (routes/api.php:559-561) are therefore authorised by the *read* permission customers_show — not customers_create/customers_edit/customers_delete, which exist as distinct, separately-assignable permissions (PermissionTableSeeder.php:475-499; CustomerController.php:36-38 uses the granular trio for its own CRUD).
  - **Why it matters** : Least-privilege violation / inconsistency: an operator granted only view access to customers can create, modify and delete their delivery addresses (affecting delivery routing / DeliveryQuoteService geocode). The sibling CustomerController already models the correct granular gating, so this is an internal inconsistency.
  - **Reproduce** : Assign a role only `customers_show`. POST /api/admin/customer/address/{customer} succeeds (address created) though the role lacks any *_create/_edit/_delete customer permission.
  - **Correction** : Split the gates: keep customers_show on index/show; gate store with customers_create (or a new customers_edit), update with customers_edit, destroy with customers_delete — matching CustomerController.php:36-38.
  - **RED verdict** : VERIFIED but DOWNGRADED P2->P3. CustomerAddressController.php:22 gates index/store/update/destroy/show all with permission:customers_show; granular customers_create/edit/delete exist (PermissionTableSeeder.php:474-496) and CustomerController.php:36-38 uses them correctly -> real internal least-privilege inconsistency, UserAddressService store/update/destroy add no role guard. BUT exploit is DORMANT under seeded RBAC: the ONLY role holding customers_show is Branch Manager, which ALSO holds customers_create/edit/delete (RolePermissionTableSeeder.php:53-57). No seeded role has customers_show in isolation, so the read-gated writes grant no additional capability to any default role. Only a hand-built custom role (customers_show alone, via admin UI) would exploit it. Real code smell, low real-world priority -> P3.

- **[P3] Subscriber broadcast email sent synchronously to all subscribers in one BCC, no queue/chunk** — `app/Services/SubscriberService.php:110-123` (perf, code)
  - **What** : sendEmail() does `$subscribers = Subscriber::pluck('email');` then `Mail::bcc($subscribers->toArray())->send(new SubscriberMail(...))` — a single synchronous send over the whole subscriber base, inside the HTTP request (SubscriberController::sendEmail:57-61). No ShouldQueue, no chunking, no per-recipient send.
  - **Why it matters** : As the subscriber list grows the request blocks until the SMTP transaction completes (or times out → 422). A single BCC to many recipients also raises deliverability/spam-filtering risk and exposes the whole list to one transport failure (all-or-nothing). For V1 Le Cayenne single-resto the list is small so impact is low today, hence P3.
  - **Reproduce** : POST /api/admin/subscriber/send-email with a large Subscriber table; the request latency scales with the mail transaction and can hit the request timeout.
  - **Correction** : Queue the mail (implement ShouldQueue on SubscriberMail or dispatch a job) and chunk recipients (e.g. Subscriber::chunk(200) → queued Mail::to() per recipient), so the controller returns immediately and one bad address doesn't fail the whole batch.
  - **RED verdict** : VERIFIED. SubscriberService::sendEmail() (SubscriberService.php:110-123) does Subscriber::pluck('email') then Mail::bcc(...)->send(new SubscriberMail) -- synchronous, single BCC, no chunk. Confirmed SubscriberMail (app/Mail/SubscriberMail.php:9-11) extends Mailable and only `use Queueable` WITHOUT `implements ShouldQueue`, so ->send() runs inline in the HTTP request (SubscriberController::sendEmail). Real blocking/deliverability concern that scales with list size; for V1 single-resto Le Cayenne the subscriber list is tiny so impact is low TODAY. P3 upheld (medium confidence -> real, low priority).

**Dropped by adversarial pass:**
- ~~[P3] ProfileChangePasswordComponent success toast: dead `?? 0` and boolean passed where flip-index expected~~ — EXISTENCE ok, RATIONALE FALSIFIED. ProfileChangePasswordComponent.vue:95 has res.config.method === 'put' ?? 0 -- the === yields a boolean so ?? 0 is genuinely dead (boolean is never null/undefined). HOWEVER the finding's behavioral claim ('toast may show wrong created/updated variant') is FALSE in this path: the component dispatches frontendEditProfile/changePassword (line 92) which is axios.put('/profile/change-password') (frontendEditProfile.js:51) -> method is ALWAYS 'put' -> status=true -> successFlip prints 'Updated Successfully' (alertService.js:50-52), the CORRECT wording for a password change. So there is NO user-visible defect, only a harmless dead operand. is_necessary:false, DROP. Do not carry the wrong-variant rationale forward.

**Coverage gaps (RED):**
- myOrder read-leak (DISTINCT gate from S10-01): CustomerController::myOrder is gated by permission:customers (line 29-35), and OrderService::userOrder (OrderService.php:253-254) filters ONLY where('user_id',$user->id) on the route-bound {customer}. A Branch Manager (holds 'customers', BranchScope no-ops on User) can pass any User id and read that user's full non-POS order history. Bundled into S10-01's narrative but has a different permission gate -- track separately.
- CustomerService::destroy() (CustomerService.php:148) has an undocumented hardcoded magic guard `$customer->id != 2` protecting user id 2 from deletion -- no comment, no constant, brittle if seed ids shift (mirrors the role-id fragility flagged at list():40-42).
- BranchScope does NOT isolate the User model: apply() returns early for any User instance (BranchScope.php:21-23) to avoid Sanctum recursion. CLAUDE.md §9 lists User among the 20 'scoped' models, but the scope is attached-yet-inert for User. Any admin route that binds {customer}/{user} by id is cross-branch readable by branch_id>0 staff -- a systemic root cause behind S10-01 and the myOrder gap, worth a documentation correction so future audits don't assume User is branch-isolated.
- Address resolution (CustomerAddressController show/update/destroy bind {customer} AND {address} independently): no check that {address} belongs to {customer} was verified here (UserAddressService methods read by user_id for list but show/update/destroy take Address directly) -- potential cross-customer address access; not in the 4 findings, flagged for a follow-up pass.

### S11-staff-rbac — S11-staff-rbac
Files examined: `app/Http/Controllers/Admin/EmployeeController.php`, `app/Http/Controllers/Admin/AdministratorController.php`, `app/Http/Controllers/Admin/Pos/PosReceiptPrintController.php`, `app/Http/Controllers/Admin/AdminController.php`, `app/Services/EmployeeService.php`, `app/Services/AdministratorService.php`, `app/Services/WaiterService.php`, `app/Services/ChefService.php`, `app/Services/Concerns/EnforcesOwnBranchScope.php`, `app/Services/Receipt/ReceiptDataService.php`, `app/Http/Requests/EmployeeRequest.php`, `app/Http/Requests/AdministratorRequest.php`, `app/Http/Requests/UserChangePasswordRequest.php`, `app/Http/Requests/ChangeImageRequest.php`, `app/Http/Resources/OrderDetailsResource.php`, `app/Models/Order.php`, `app/Enums/Role.php`, `routes/api.php`, `database/seeders/RolePermissionTableSeeder.php`, `resources/js/components/admin/employees/EmployeeCreateComponent.vue`, `resources/js/components/admin/administrators/AdministratorListComponent.vue`, `resources/js/components/admin/pos/ReceiptComponent.vue`

**Confirmed corrections:**

- **[P3] AdministratorService::update() missing target/self/super-admin guards present on every sibling method — id=1 super-admin protection bypassable via update** — `app/Services/AdministratorService.php:87-110` (logic, code)
  - **What** : destroy() (l.115-130) guards with `Auth::user()->id != $administrator->id && $administrator->id != 1` AND `hasRole(EnumRole::ADMIN)`; changePassword() (l.162) and changeImage() (l.181) both guard with `hasRole(EnumRole::ADMIN)`. update() (l.87-110) has NONE: no hasRole check, no `id != 1`, no self-guard — confirmed by grep over lines 87-110 returning zero matches. Route `Route::match(['post','put','patch'],'/{administrator}', update)` (routes/api.php:1044) binds ANY User via route-model-binding. The sibling Waiter/Chef services carry the explicit WAVE5-SEC-001 `assertTargetRole()` guard (WaiterService.php:99, ChefService.php:98); AdministratorService::update is the one place the pattern was not applied.
  - **Why it matters** : An `administrators_edit` holder can POST to /administrator/1 (or any non-Admin user's id) and overwrite name/email/branch_id and reset the password (l.99-101 `if($request->password) password=Hash::make`). The id=1 root-admin that destroy() deliberately protects from deletion can have its credentials rotated through update() — a full account takeover of the super-admin, and a way to mutate non-Admin Users (e.g. a Customer) through the admin endpoint since no role is asserted. On a single-box Le Cayenne this is the owner's own root account.
  - **Reproduce** : As a user holding only `administrators_edit` (not Admin by default seed, but any custom role granted it): POST /api/admin/administrator/1 with {name,email,password:'NewPass123456',password_confirmation:'NewPass123456',status:1,country_code:'+33',branch_id:<their branch>}. AdministratorRequest passes (caller need only hold administrators_edit; branch_id guard satisfied with own non-zero branch). update() writes the new password hash to user id=1 with no role/self/id-1 check. Login as id=1 with the new password.
  - **Correction** : Add the same guard destroy() uses at the top of update(): assert `$administrator->hasRole(EnumRole::ADMIN)` (reject mutating non-admins through this endpoint) and `$administrator->id != 1` / self-id checks consistent with destroy(), mirroring the WAVE5-SEC-001 assertTargetRole pattern used in WaiterService/ChefService. Place the assertion BEFORE the try/catch so it surfaces as 403 rather than being rewritten to 422 (per the existing WAVE5-SEC-001 rationale comments).
  - **RED verdict** : CONFIRMED. AdministratorService::update (app/Services/AdministratorService.php:87-110) has NO hasRole/id!=1/self guard, while destroy (l.118-119), changePassword (l.162) and changeImage (l.181) all guard with hasRole(EnumRole::ADMIN) and destroy adds id!=1. Sibling WaiterService::update (WaiterService.php:99) and ChefService::update (ChefService.php:98) call assertTargetRole BEFORE try/catch; AdministratorService::update is the lone gap. Route Route::match(['post','put','patch'],'/{administrator}',update) (routes/api.php:1044) binds User by PK with NO custom Route::bind, and BranchScope EXEMPTS User (app/Models/Scopes/BranchScope.php:20-22 'Never branch-filter the authenticatable model') so a branch>0 staff with administrators_edit CAN bind User id=1 (the branch_id=0 super-admin). AdministratorRequest::authorize (AdministratorRequest.php:27) only needs administrators_create||administrators_edit; bug_011 branch_id guard (l.95) satisfied with attacker own non-zero branch_id. update() writes Hash::make(password) (l.99-101) onto id=1 with no role/id check = super-admin takeover. P1 on impact. LATENCY: no DEFAULT seed role holds administrators_edit without being Admin; reachable only via custom role, but the guard gap is real and fix warranted.
  - **Self-audit re-rating** : P1 → P3
  - **Self-audit note** : Self-audit re-rating P1->P3: the AdministratorService::update() missing target/self/super-admin guard asymmetry is REAL (siblings carry it) but is defense-in-depth/code-quality, not a demonstrated privilege-escalation exploit in V1 single-admin. Keep the recommended guard.

- **[P1] POS printed receipt 'Opérateur' shows the CUSTOMER name, not the cashier — operator identity wrong on the fiscal ticket while creator_id (cashier) is captured but unused** — `app/Services/Receipt/ReceiptDataService.php:70` (nf525, code)
  - **What** : buildForOrderModel() sets `'operator_name' => optional($order->user)->name` where Order::user() (app/Models/Order.php:173-175) is the CUSTOMER belongsTo. OrderDetailsResource.php:122 exposes `'operator_name' => $receipt['operator_name']`, and the POS receipt ReceiptComponent.vue:72 and :287 (80mm thermal block) render `label.operator + ': ' + order.operator_name`. Meanwhile OrderService.php:777 now persists the real cashier on `creator_id` ('creator_id' => Auth::id()), and Order.php:65 lists creator_id as fillable — but no `creator()` relation exists (grep: only user/branch/deliveryBoy belongsTo) and ReceiptDataService never reads it.
  - **Why it matters** : NF525 / Article 286 requires the operator who issued the ticket be identifiable on the fiscal document. The printed POS ticket attributes the sale to the customer (or 'Client passage' when no customer is attached) instead of the cashier on shift — an audit/inspection defect and a real shift-accountability gap. The correct value is already stored (creator_id) but the receipt SSOT ignores it, so the captured cashier identity is wasted.
  - **Reproduce** : Place a POS cash order as a logged-in cashier, then print/reprint the receipt (ReceiptComponent). The 'Opérateur:' line shows the attached customer's name (or blank/Client passage), not the cashier who is authenticated. Confirmed in code: line 70 reads order->user (customer), not order->creator.
  - **Correction** : Add a `creator()` belongsTo(User::class,'creator_id') relation on Order, then in ReceiptDataService:70 use `optional($order->creator)->name ?? optional($order->user)->name` (fallback to user only for kiosk/online orders that have no cashier). Eager-load creator in OrderDetailsResource's hydration to avoid an extra SELECT. Note ReceiptDataService is NF525-adjacent — change is a one-line SSOT fix, no fiscal-chain logic touched, but keep it covered by ReceiptDataServiceWireInTest.
  - **RED verdict** : CONFIRMED. ReceiptDataService::buildForOrderModel (app/Services/Receipt/ReceiptDataService.php:70) sets operator_name => optional($order->user)->name, and Order::user() (app/Models/Order.php:173-175) is the CUSTOMER belongsTo. OrderDetailsResource.php:122 re-exposes it; the POS thermal receipt renders it at resources/js/components/admin/pos/ReceiptComponent.vue:72 and :287. The cashier IS persisted: OrderService.php:777 writes creator_id=>Auth::id(), creator_id fillable (Order.php:65) — but grep confirms NO creator() relation on Order (only user/branch/deliveryBoy/diningTable at l.173/183/190/304), so ReceiptDataService cannot/does not read it. NF525 operator-on-ticket = customer/Client passage not cashier. NOTE: finding cites JS path components/admin/orders/ReceiptComponent.vue but actual path is components/admin/pos/ReceiptComponent.vue; file_line_exists true because cited PHP SSOT line 70 is exact and render behavior exists at corrected path. One-line SSOT fix appropriate, NF525-adjacent.

- **[P2] Password reset and image change gated only by the base VIEW permission (employees / administrators), not by *_edit — FormRequest authorize() returns true** — `app/Http/Controllers/Admin/EmployeeController.php:28` (security, code)
  - **What** : EmployeeController.php:28 `$this->middleware(['permission:employees'])->only('index','export','changePassword','changeImage','myOrder')` — changePassword/changeImage are gated by the read permission `employees`, while update is separately gated by `employees_edit` (l.30). AdministratorController.php:31 has the identical pattern (`permission:administrators` gates changePassword/changeImage). The FormRequests provide no extra gate: UserChangePasswordRequest::authorize() returns true (l.18) and ChangeImageRequest::authorize() returns true (l.16).
  - **Why it matters** : A role with read-only access to staff (the `employees`/`administrators` view capability) can reset any in-scope staff member's password and replace their avatar — privilege the owner almost certainly intends to require edit rights. It is a real authz blur (view permission grants a write/account-takeover action). LATENT: by default seed no role holds the view permission without _edit (Branch Manager holds both employees + employees_edit per RolePermissionTableSeeder.php:58-62; POS Operator/Chef/Stuff/Waiter hold neither), so only a custom view-only role reaches it today.
  - **Reproduce** : Grant a custom role only `employees` (no employees_edit). As that user, POST /api/admin/employee/change-password/{id} with a valid 12-char password — the request passes the `permission:employees` middleware and the authorize()=true FormRequest, and EmployeeService::changePassword rotates the target's password.
  - **Correction** : Move changePassword and changeImage out of the `permission:employees` .only(...) group into a `permission:employees_edit` group (and `administrators_edit` for the admin controller). Optionally tighten the two FormRequests' authorize() to check `$this->user()?->can('employees_edit')` for defense-in-depth, mirroring EmployeeRequest::authorize().
  - **RED verdict** : CONFIRMED. EmployeeController.php:28 gates changePassword/changeImage under permission:employees (read cap) while update is employees_edit (l.30); AdministratorController.php:31 identical. Both FormRequests fail open: UserChangePasswordRequest::authorize true (l.18), ChangeImageRequest::authorize true (l.16). A view-only role can rotate a target password / replace avatar (write behind read perm) = real authz blur. Correctly LATENT: Branch Manager holds both employees+employees_edit (RolePermissionTableSeeder.php:58-62); POS Operator/Chef/Stuff/Waiter hold neither family, so only a custom view-only role reaches it. Fix (move into _edit middleware group) warranted; P2 given latency.

- **[P3] Employee role dropdown filters by hardcoded integer role IDs '1|2|3|4|5' while the backend was deliberately healed OFF integer IDs onto role NAMES — stale on fresh seeds** — `resources/js/components/admin/employees/EmployeeCreateComponent.vue:227` (logic, needs-live-verify)
  - **What** : The role dropdown loads `role/lists` with `excepts: '1|2|3|4|5'` (l.227) to hide Admin/Customer/DeliveryBoy/Waiter/Chef. But AdministratorService.php:43-46, WaiterService.php:44-47, ChefService.php:43-46 all carry the heal comment '[GOAL-pageby-V1.0.2] Spatie ->role(int) breaks whenever roles.id AUTO_INCREMENT skipped past it (fresh seed lands at 73-80). Stable identity = role NAME' and switched to ->role('Admin'/'Waiter'/'Chef'). The frontend filter still trusts integer IDs 1-5.
  - **Why it matters** : On a reseed where role AUTO_INCREMENT shifts (the exact scenario the backend heal documents), the dropdown would hide the wrong roles (e.g. exclude whatever now occupies ids 1-5 and expose Waiter/Chef which now sit at 73-80). The backend EmployeeService::blockRoles still rejects ADMIN/CUSTOMER/DELIVERY_BOY/WAITER/CHEF by enum, so it is not a security hole — but it is exactly the integer-vs-name 'flou' the codebase already chose to eliminate everywhere else, surviving in the UI.
  - **Reproduce** : Inspect EmployeeCreateComponent.vue:224-228: `excepts:'1|2|3|4|5'`. Compare with the role-NAME heal in AdministratorService.php:46 `->role('Admin','sanctum')`. On a fresh seed where Admin is no longer id=1, the dropdown's exclusion no longer corresponds to the same roles.
  - **Correction** : Filter the role list by NAME (exclude 'Admin','Customer','Delivery Boy','Waiter','Chef') or expose a backend-driven assignable-roles endpoint, consistent with the role-NAME identity the backend services already adopted. Keep EmployeeService::blockRoles as the authoritative backstop.
  - **RED verdict** : EXISTS but OVER-RATED. EmployeeCreateComponent.vue:227 excepts:'1|2|3|4|5' verified; backend did switch to role NAMES (AdministratorService.php:46, WaiterService.php:47). BUT on THIS seed enum IDs ARE 1-5 (app/Enums/Role.php:7-11 ADMIN=1..CHEF=5), so the filter is CORRECT TODAY. Bug only fires on AUTO_INCREMENT-drifted reseed — the multi-env/reseed cloud-prep scenario CLAUDE.md/MEMORY explicitly defer as NON-blocker for V1 LOCAL single-box. EmployeeService::blockRoles (l.29) is the enum backstop so no security impact even on drift (worst case wrong dropdown). Downgraded P2->P3: cosmetic consistency / cloud-prep backlog. is_necessary:true as low-priority cleanup but defer-to-cloud, not actionable now.

- **[P2] EmployeeService show/changePassword/changeImage guard via optional($employee->roles[0])->id — a role-less User bypasses the block-roles gate (no assertTargetRole like Waiter/Chef)** — `app/Services/EmployeeService.php:230` (logic, code)
  - **What** : changePassword (l.230), changeImage (l.249), show (l.181), destroy (l.199) all test `!in_array(optional($employee->roles[0])->id, $this->blockRoles)`. When a User has no roles, `$employee->roles[0]` is null, `optional(...)->id` is null, and `in_array(null, [1,2,3,4,5])` is false, so `!false === true` → the guard PASSES for a role-less user. WaiterService/ChefService instead use a dedicated assertTargetRole() that requires `hasRole(WAITER/CHEF)` (WaiterService.php:181-189) before mutation; EmployeeService has no such positive role assertion.
  - **Why it matters** : Route-model-binding resolves {employee} to any User. A role-less User (orphaned account, mid-migration, or a deliberately role-stripped record) can have its password reset / image changed / be deleted through the employee endpoint, even though it is not an employee. Admin/Customer/etc. targets are still correctly blocked (their role id IS in blockRoles), so impact is bounded to role-less accounts — but the guard is a negative-list that fails open on the empty case, unlike the positive assertTargetRole used by the sibling services.
  - **Reproduce** : Pass a User id that has zero role rows to POST /api/admin/employee/change-password/{id}. optional($employee->roles[0])->id evaluates null, the blockRoles in_array check returns false, the negated guard passes, and the password is rotated on a non-employee account.
  - **Correction** : Adopt the WAVE5-SEC-001 positive guard: add an assertTargetRole($employee) that requires the user to actually hold one of the employee roles (BRANCH_MANAGER/POS_OPERATOR/STUFF or any non-blocked role) before mutation, mirroring WaiterService::assertTargetRole. Treat a null roles[0] as a 403, not a pass.
  - **RED verdict** : PARTIALLY CONFIRMED — finding OVER-CLAIMS destroy(). Negative-list guard !in_array(optional($employee->roles[0])->id, blockRoles) fails open for a role-less user (null not in [1..5]) at show (EmployeeService.php:181), changePassword (l.230), changeImage (l.249) — genuinely reachable, unlike WaiterService/ChefService positive assertTargetRole (WaiterService.php:181-189). HOWEVER destroy (l.199) is NOT vulnerable: l.200 adds a SECOND gate hasRole(optional($employee->roles[0])->id)=hasRole(null), which Spatie returns false for (null name matches nothing) => permission_denied (and try/catch would 422 anyway). Finding lists destroy() at l.199 among vulnerable methods = incorrect. Real defect (role-less password reset/avatar/show on non-employee) stands at P2; bounded to orphaned/role-stripped accounts. assertTargetRole fix correct for the THREE unguarded methods.

- **[P3] EmployeeCreateComponent.save catch reads err.response.data.errors unconditionally — 422 responses that return {status,message} (not {errors}) leave the form with no error feedback** — `resources/js/components/admin/employees/EmployeeCreateComponent.vue:307` (functional, needs-live-verify)
  - **What** : save()'s catch does `this.errors = err.response.data.errors` (l.307). The service throws via QueryExceptionLibrary returning `response(['status'=>false,'message'=>...],422)` for business/permission errors (EmployeeService::store l.106 permission_denied, l.126/131), which has no `errors` key. Validation failures DO return `errors` (FormRequest), so field errors work; but a permission_denied or query exception sets this.errors = undefined and surfaces nothing to the user.
  - **Why it matters** : When a Branch Manager hits the callerMayGrantRole gate (l.105) trying to assign a role outside their subset, the 422 carries {message:'permission denied'} with no `errors` map — the form shows no message, the spinner stops, and the manager sees a silent no-op with no explanation of why the save failed. A confusing dead-end during a real shift.
  - **Reproduce** : As a Branch Manager, attempt to create an employee with a role whose permissions are not a strict subset of yours. Backend returns 422 {status:false,message:'...'}; save()'s catch sets this.errors = undefined, no alert is shown, form just resets the loading state.
  - **Correction** : In the catch, fall back to alertService.error(err.response?.data?.message) when err.response.data.errors is absent (the AdministratorList destroy() already uses alertService.error(err.response.data.message) for this exact shape). Guard against undefined response.
  - **RED verdict** : CONFIRMED. EmployeeCreateComponent.vue:307 catch does this.errors=err.response.data.errors unconditionally. EmployeeService throws permission/business 422 with {status:false,message:...} and NO 'errors' key (callerMayGrantRole gate EmployeeService.php:106, store permission_denied). FormRequest validation DOES return {errors} so field errors render, but permission_denied sets this.errors=undefined, no alert, silent dead-end. Real but minor UX gap; P3. Fix (fall back to alertService.error(err.response?.data?.message) like AdministratorList destroy) sensible. is_necessary:true.

**Dropped by adversarial pass:**
- ~~[P3] EmployeeRequest lacks the super-admin branch_id mint guard that AdministratorRequest carries — defense-in-depth asymmetry on branch assignment~~ — EXISTS but WORKS-AS-INTENDED. EmployeeRequest.php:67 validates branch_id only ['nullable','numeric'] with no withValidator guard vs AdministratorRequest.php:92-101 bug_011 mint guard. BUT finding ITSELF concedes (verified) exposure is fully contained: EmployeeService::store (l.115)/update (l.153) call effectiveBranchId which for a non-settings caller FORCES (int)caller->branch_id and IGNORES the request value (EnforcesOwnBranchScope.php:21-22). A Branch Manager CANNOT mint branch_id=0 super-staff via employee endpoint. Purely 'a future refactor might drop effectiveBranchId' — defense-in-depth asymmetry, not an active hole. Speculative future-refactor parity is not a V1 blocker per CLAUDE.md/MEMORY. Downgraded P2->P3, is_necessary:false: at most add a regression test, no behavior change needed.

**Coverage gaps (RED):**
- AdministratorService::store (app/Services/AdministratorService.php:58-82) calls assignRole(EnumRole::ADMIN) at l.74 with NO callerMayGrantRole/entitlement gate, whereas EmployeeService::store (EmployeeService.php:105) DOES gate role grants. Anyone holding administrators_create can mint a brand-new full Admin-role superuser (Admin carries Permission::all() regardless of branch; bug_011 only forces a non-zero branch_id, not the Admin role powers). This superuser-mint escalation is arguably larger than the flagged S11-01 update() gap and was missed — only update() asymmetry was examined, not store().
- EmployeeListComponent.vue:283 carries the IDENTICAL stale excepts:'1|2|3|4|5' integer-ID role filter that S11-04 flagged only in EmployeeCreateComponent.vue:227. The auditor examined the Create twin but not the List twin — any role-NAME fix must touch both.
- Waiter/Chef route groups (routes/api.php:565-573 waiter + chef group) expose the same change-password/{waiter} and change-image/{waiter} POST routes; auditor verified the SERVICES carry assertTargetRole but did NOT check whether WaiterController/ChefController gate changePassword/changeImage under the bare view permission (waiters/chefs) vs *_edit — the same S11-03 view-vs-edit blur should be confirmed/denied on these two sibling controllers.
- ChangeImageRequest::authorize() and UserChangePasswordRequest::authorize() both return true (verified) and are shared across employee/administrator/waiter/chef/delivery-boy change-password/change-image endpoints. S11-03 flagged only employee+admin paths; the full blast radius across all five staff controllers consuming these two always-true FormRequests was not enumerated.

### S12-delivery — S12-delivery
Files examined: `resources/js/components/admin/deliveryBoys/DeliveryBoyListComponent.vue`, `resources/js/components/admin/deliveryBoyCashSession/DeliveryBoyCashSessionFormComponent.vue`, `resources/js/components/admin/deliveryBoyCashSession/DeliveryBoyCashSessionListComponent.vue`, `resources/js/components/admin/deliveryBoyCashSession/DeliveryBoyCashSessionShowComponent.vue`, `resources/js/router/modules/deliveryBoyCashSessionRoutes.js`, `app/Http/Controllers/Admin/DeliveryBoyController.php`, `app/Http/Controllers/Admin/DeliveryBoyCashSessionController.php`, `app/Http/Controllers/Frontend/DeliveryBoyOrderController.php`, `app/Services/DeliveryBoyService.php`, `app/Services/Delivery/DeliveryBoyCashSessionService.php`, `app/Services/OrderService.php`, `app/Services/Fiscal/ZReportCashEnrichmentService.php`, `app/Models/DeliveryBoyCashMovement.php`, `app/Models/DeliveryBoyCashSession.php`, `app/Enums/Role.php`, `routes/api.php`

**Confirmed corrections:**

- **[P3] Tautological role guard !in_array(DELIVERY_BOY, blockRoles) is always true — dead else-branches across 5 methods** — `app/Services/DeliveryBoyService.php:28,103,134,152,202,224` (logic, code)
  - **What** : blockRoles=[EnumRole::ADMIN] (line 28). Role.php: ADMIN=1, DELIVERY_BOY=3. Guards do `if (!in_array(EnumRole::DELIVERY_BOY, $this->blockRoles))` in update():103, show():134, destroy():152, changePassword():202, changeImage():224. in_array(3,[1]) is always false, so the negation is always true — the guard NEVER blocks and every `else { throw permission_denied }` is unreachable dead code. The real role check is assertTargetRole() (line 183) which is already called separately.
  - **Why it matters** : Exactly the kind of 'flou / unnecessary thing' the owner flagged: a guard that LOOKS like an authorization control but is a compile-time constant comparing a constant to a constant array. It checks the literal DELIVERY_BOY constant (never the route-bound user's actual role), so it gives a false sense of protection while doing nothing; the dead else-branches mislead any future maintainer into thinking permission_denied is reachable. Pure noise in 5 methods of a money-adjacent service.
  - **Reproduce** : Read app/Services/DeliveryBoyService.php line 28 then any guard (e.g. 152); cross-check app/Enums/Role.php lines 7 & 9. in_array(3,[1])===false for all inputs.
  - **Correction** : Delete the `if (!in_array(EnumRole::DELIVERY_BOY,$this->blockRoles))` wrappers and their unreachable else-branches; rely on assertTargetRole() (already the genuine guard). If a future need is to block self-mutation of Admins, compare the TARGET user's role, not the DELIVERY_BOY constant.
  - **RED verdict** : CONFIRMED dead-code: blockRoles=[ADMIN=1] (DeliveryBoyService.php:28), DELIVERY_BOY=3 (Role.php:9), so !in_array(3,[1]) is a compile-time constant TRUE at lines 103,134,152,202,224 — every else throws permission_denied unreachable. BUT for update/destroy/changePassword/changeImage the REAL guard is assertTargetRole() called pre-try (100,199,221) + inner hasRole (line 155 in destroy). The only method where the no-op is the SOLE 'guard' is show() (131-134) which has no assertTargetRole preflight. Net: cosmetic dead-code, no security hole (no path lets a wrong-role user mutate). P2->P3.

- **[P2] COD doorstep cash collected with NO open driver session is never mirrored into any cash session — reconciliation-completeness gap** — `app/Services/OrderService.php:1919-1986` (nf525, code)
  - **What** : On every COD DELIVERED the escrow audit row `delivery.cash_collected_escrow` is written unconditionally (line 1919-1935). The mirror into a DeliveryBoyCashSession movement (recordMovement TYPE_ORDER_COLLECT) is wrapped in `if ($openSession)` (1961) using findOpenSessionForDeliveryBoy(), best-effort non-strict (false at 1969). Nothing in deliveryBoyOrderChangeStatus() / the state machine requires the driver to have an OPEN session before OFD/DELIVERED. So a driver with no open shift collects real cash, an escrow audit row exists, but the cash enters NO session, hits NO reconciliation, and is invisible to ZReportCashEnrichmentService session aggregation.
  - **Why it matters** : Cash trail completeness is the whole point of the livreur cash-session feature (NF525 mirror of the POS drawer). If the open-session step is optional and ungated, the reconciliation surface only covers drivers who remembered to open a shift — the manager's variance figure is silently partial and real money can move with no session-level accountability. The escrow audit row alone is not a reconciliation; it is not summed against opening/closing.
  - **Reproduce** : Code trace: deliveryBoyOrderChangeStatus has no open-session precondition; OrderService.php:1955 gates the movement on a found open session, 1961 skips silently otherwise. A COD DELIVERED by a driver with no open DeliveryBoyCashSession writes the escrow row (1919) but no delivery_boy_cash_movements row.
  - **Correction** : Either (a) require an open driver cash session before allowing OFD→DELIVERED on COD orders (strict mode 422 LIVREUR_SHIFT_NOT_OPEN, which recordMovement already supports), or (b) auto-open a session / route the orphan collection into a dedicated unassigned-cash bucket surfaced in the Z report so no collected COD cash is invisible to reconciliation.
  - **RED verdict** : STRONGEST finding, CONFIRMED. Escrow audit row delivery.cash_collected_escrow written unconditionally on COD DELIVERED (OrderService.php:1878 cashEscrowWritten=true, 1923 write). Session mirror gated on if($openSession) (1961) via findOpenSessionForDeliveryBoy, recordMovement called with strict=false (1968). deliveryBoyOrderChangeStatus (1755) has NO open-session precondition before OFD/DELIVERED. So a driver with no open shift collects real COD cash -> escrow audit row exists but ZERO delivery_boy_cash_movements row -> invisible to ZReportCashEnrichmentService session aggregation (413). recordMovement already supports strict-422 mode for the fix. NF525 cash-trail completeness gap. P2 holds.

- **[P3] destroy() soft-deletes a livreur without checking open cash session or active assigned orders** — `app/Services/DeliveryBoyService.php:149-169` (logic, code)
  - **What** : destroy() only deletes addresses then soft-deletes the User (User uses SoftDeletes, User.php:30). It does NOT check for a DeliveryBoyCashSession with status=OPEN for that driver, nor for orders with delivery_boy_id=this user still in OUT_FOR_DELIVERY / not yet DELIVERED. openSession() at DeliveryBoyCashSessionService.php:136 does preserve SoftDeletingScope so a new session can't be opened on a deleted livreur, but an ALREADY-open session and in-flight orders are left dangling.
  - **Why it matters** : Deleting a driver mid-shift orphans an OPEN cash session (money still tracked against a now-soft-deleted user that the admin can no longer load/close/reconcile via the role-scoped queries) and strands assigned active deliveries with no owner. That breaks end-of-day reconciliation and order operations — the kind of state corruption the locked-session NF525 design exists to prevent.
  - **Reproduce** : Read destroy() 149-169 — no query against delivery_boy_cash_sessions(status=open) or orders(delivery_boy_id, status in OFD). Soft delete proceeds regardless.
  - **Correction** : Before delete, abort 409 if an OPEN DeliveryBoyCashSession exists for the driver, or if active (non-terminal) orders are assigned. Force close+reconcile / reassign first.
  - **RED verdict** : PARTIALLY real, rationale OVERSTATED -> downgrade. destroy() (149-169) confirmed: only deletes addresses + soft-deletes User, NO check for OPEN DeliveryBoyCashSession or active assigned orders. HOWEVER the finding's claim that a deleted driver's session 'can no longer be loaded/closed/reconciled' is FALSE: index() (controller 53-92) queries DeliveryBoyCashSession directly with NO User join/whereHas, and closeSession(133)/reconcileSession(192) operate by sessionId via firstOrFail() with NO User join — so the orphaned session stays fully listable/closeable/reconcilable. The genuinely solid harm is the orphaned in-flight ORDERS (no reassignment), not unmanageable cash. Real but lower impact than claimed. P2->P3.

- **[P3] selectDeliveryBoy has no order-status gate and no target-status check — a DELIVERED order can be reassigned to another driver** — `app/Services/OrderService.php:2535-2581` (logic, code)
  - **What** : selectDeliveryBoy validates target is positive int (2547), is a 'Delivery Boy' role (2564-2571) and same branch (2573), then sets delivery_boy_id and saves (2580). There is NO check on $order->status (e.g. block reassignment once DELIVERED/RETURNED) and NO check that the target livreur's $target->status is active. So a finished order can be silently reassigned, and an inactive/disabled driver can be assigned new deliveries.
  - **Why it matters** : Reassigning a DELIVERED order rewrites the doorstep-cash accountability after the fact (the escrow row already credited the original driver) and notifies a new driver for a finished job — operational confusion and a weakened cash trail. Assigning an inactive driver hands deliveries to someone who shouldn't be on shift.
  - **Reproduce** : Read 2535-2581: no `$order->status` guard, no `$target->status` guard before save.
  - **Correction** : Reject (422) reassignment when order is in a terminal status (DELIVERED/RETURNED/CANCELLED) and reject assignment to a target whose status is inactive.
  - **RED verdict** : CONFIRMED. selectDeliveryBoy (OrderService.php:2535-2581) validates positive int (2547), role 'Delivery Boy' (2564-2571), same branch (2573), then sets delivery_boy_id + save (2580) BYPASSING OrderStateMachine entirely (no apply/canTransition call). No $order->status guard: OrderStateMachine.php:77 confirms DELIVERED is terminal (->RETURNED only), so a finished order's driver CAN be silently reassigned, rewriting post-hoc cash accountability (escrow already credited original driver) + notifying a new driver for a done job. No $target->status (active/inactive) check either. Real operational + cash-trail weakness, but edge-case (admin-initiated, no direct money loss). P3 holds.

- **[P3] Delivery cash-session list route requires full 'delivery-boys' mutation permission; backend index only needs read permission** — `resources/js/router/modules/deliveryBoyCashSessionRoutes.js:24` (ux, code)
  - **What** : List route meta permissionUrl='delivery-boys' (line 24) and Show route also 'delivery-boys' (line 36). Backend DeliveryBoyCashSessionController.__construct gates index/show with `permission:delivery-boys_show` (controller 42) and only mutations (open/close/reconcile) with `permission:delivery-boys` (43). So a user with read-only delivery-boys_show can call the API but the SPA route guard hides the page from them.
  - **Why it matters** : Read-only supervisors (delivery-boys_show) are blocked from even VIEWING the cash-session list/detail in the UI despite the backend allowing it — an over-restrictive front gate that contradicts the backend contract and the route file's own comment ('Permissions: delivery-boys_show (read)').
  - **Reproduce** : Compare deliveryBoyCashSessionRoutes.js:24/36 (permissionUrl 'delivery-boys') with DeliveryBoyCashSessionController.php:42 (index/show require delivery-boys_show).
  - **Correction** : Set the list/show route permissionUrl to 'delivery-boys_show' (or the read variant) to match the backend read gate; keep mutation buttons gated by the mutation permission.
  - **RED verdict** : CONFIRMED contradiction. deliveryBoyCashSessionRoutes.js list (line ~24) and show (~36) both meta permissionUrl='delivery-boys' (mutation perm), while the file's OWN header comment line 10 says 'delivery-boys_show (read), delivery-boys (mutations)' and backend DeliveryBoyCashSessionController.__construct gates index/show with permission:delivery-boys_show (42, docblock 31) and only open/close/reconcile with permission:delivery-boys (43). So a read-only delivery-boys_show supervisor is hidden from the SPA list/show page despite the backend permitting read. Over-restrictive front gate, contradicts backend contract + the route file's own comment. Low impact (UX/perms, no data exposure or money). P3 holds; auditor's P3 + medium-confidence appropriate.
