### S13-marketing — S13-marketing
Files examined: `app/Http/Controllers/Admin/CouponController.php`, `app/Services/CouponService.php`, `app/Models/Coupon.php`, `app/Http/Requests/CouponRequest.php`, `app/Http/Requests/CouponCheckRequest.php`, `app/Services/Pricing/PricingService.php`, `app/Services/Pricing/DiscountCalculator.php`, `app/Services/OrderService.php`, `app/Services/FrontendOrderService.php`, `app/Services/Fiscal/ZReportService.php`, `app/Http/Controllers/Admin/OfferController.php`, `app/Services/OfferService.php`, `app/Http/Controllers/Admin/PushNotificationController.php`, `app/Services/PushNotificationService.php`, `app/Http/Requests/PushNotificationRequest.php`, `app/Services/FirebaseService.php`, `app/Http/Controllers/Admin/MessageController.php`, `app/Services/MessageService.php`, `app/Http/Controllers/Admin/AdminController.php`, `config/pos.php`, `config/features.php`, `routes/api.php`, `resources/js/components/admin/coupons/CouponListComponent.vue`, `resources/js/components/admin/pushNotification/PushNotificationCreateComponent.vue`

**Confirmed corrections:**

- **[P1] Per-order persisted total_tax is computed on PRE-discount subtotal; only the Z-report re-nets TVA by discount ratio — order/receipt TVA disagrees with Z (residual F1 surface)** — `app/Services/OrderService.php:551-563 ; app/Services/Fiscal/ZReportService.php:672-685` (nf525, needs-live-verify)
  - **What** : OrderService persists $this->order->total_tax = $totalTax where $totalTax is the sum of per-line tax computed on the full (pre-discount) line totals; the coupon discount is then subtracted only from the order TOTAL (line 570), never from total_tax. ZReportService (lines 672-685) introduces a discount-netting ratio (subtotal-discount)/subtotal and scales per-rate TVA by it for the signed Z. So the Z TVA reflects the discount but the order row's stored total_tax (used on the customer receipt / order resource) does not. For a discounted order the receipt TVA line and the Z report TVA bucket diverge.
  - **Why it matters** : NF525 expects the per-ticket fiscal TVA and the daily Z aggregate to be coherent. A coupon-discounted order shows a TVA on the receipt computed on the pre-discount base while the signed Z books a smaller (netted) TVA — an internal inconsistency a fiscal audit would flag. The coupon discount is exactly the marketing->pricing->F1 link the system must get right.
  - **Reproduce** : Enable discounts (POS_MANUAL_DISCOUNT_ENABLED=true). Order 20 EUR TTC at 10% with a 2 EUR coupon. Inspect orders.total_tax (still full-base ~1.82) vs the Z bucket for that order (netted by 18/20). Compare receipt TVA vs Z total_by_tax_rate.
  - **Correction** : Net the per-order total_tax by the same discount ratio at order creation (or recompute TVA on the post-discount base) so the persisted/receipt TVA matches the Z netting. Cover with a behavioral test asserting order.total_tax == Z per-rate contribution for a discounted order. Touches frozen pricing/fiscal zone -> requires lock-plan.
  - **RED verdict** : Confirmed and LIVE. OrderService.php:561-562 persists order->total_tax on the full pre-discount base; the discount is subtracted only from order->total (no total_tax netting anywhere in OrderService). The F1 fix (commits 1ff06f171, 747204e9c) netted discount VAT ONLY inside the signed Z (ZReportService.php:677-685 orderDiscountRatio + line 724 scaling). The pre-discount total_tax is surfaced on the receipt/order resource (OrderDetailsResource.php:60). Decisive: ManualDiscountDisabledV1SentinelTest docstring states the default is now ENABLED and the sentinel only tests the kill-switch=false refusal path — it does NOT assert order.total_tax == Z per-rate contribution for a discounted order. With the live default (flag=true) a coupon-discounted order is creatable and its receipt TVA (pre-discount base) diverges from the netted Z bucket -> reachable NF525 receipt-vs-Z divergence. The F1 fix was incomplete (Z side netted, order/receipt side not). Frozen pricing/fiscal zone -> lock-plan. P1 stands.
  - **Self-audit note** : Self-audit: keep P1 — with manual_discount_enabled=true (verified at runtime, no .env override) the coupon/discount divergence is LIVE, not residual.

- **[P2] Push notification broadcast fires immediately on submit with no confirmation dialog — irreversible mass send is one click** — `resources/js/components/admin/pushNotification/PushNotificationCreateComponent.vue:169-205 ; app/Services/PushNotificationService.php:55-116` (functional, code)
  - **What** : PushNotificationCreateComponent save() builds a FormData and dispatches pushNotification/save with no confirm()/modal gate. The backend store() synchronously fans out to FCM for every matching token (PushNotificationService.php:113-115 -> FirebaseService::sendNotification). With role_id=0 AND user_id=0 the fan-out selects ALL User rows with web_token/device_token (lines 84-92). There is no draft/preview/confirm step and no scheduled-send — the broadcast is irreversible the moment the Save button is pressed.
  - **Why it matters** : A mistyped title/body or an accidental click pushes a permanent notification to every device. Unlike coupons/offers there is no undo, no edit-after-send, and no toggle. Modern admin tooling (Toast, Olo) gates broadcast sends behind an explicit 'Send to N recipients?' confirmation showing the audience size. Its absence here is a real operational hazard.
  - **Reproduce** : Open admin push-notification drawer, leave role/user empty (=0/0), type any title+description, click Save once -> notification is delivered to all tokened users with no confirmation prompt.
  - **Correction** : Add a confirmation step before dispatch that states the resolved audience ('Envoyer a N destinataires ? Action irreversible.'). Optionally surface the computed recipient count from the backend before committing the send.
  - **RED verdict** : Real: PushNotificationService::store (PushNotificationService.php:55-116) synchronously fans out to FCM via FirebaseService::sendNotification, and role_id=0 && user_id=0 selects ALL tokened users (lines 83-91, branch filter only when pushBranchId!==0); the create component dispatches save with no confirm/preview gate. But P1 overstates a UX-safety gap: single-branch V1, bounded token set, broadcast is an intended permission-gated admin action. 'No confirmation dialog' is operational-safety polish, not correctness/security. Downgrade P1->P2.

- **[P3] Push notification store() trusts request branch_id with no ownership check and no Rule::exists — branch user can broadcast globally by sending branch_id=0** — `app/Services/PushNotificationService.php:63,82-92 ; app/Http/Requests/PushNotificationRequest.php:37 ; app/Http/Controllers/Admin/PushNotificationController.php:37-44` (security, code)
  - **What** : store() sets $pushNotification->branch_id = $request->branch_id directly. PushNotificationRequest validates branch_id only as required|numeric (no Rule::exists, no clamp, authorize() returns true). The controller never calls AdminController::authorizeBranchScope() (which exists, AdminController.php:14-27). The 2026-05-18 tenant-isolation heal only filters the fan-out WHEN $pushBranchId !== 0; a branch-scoped sender POSTing branch_id=0 takes the role_id==0 && user_id==0 path with no branch filter -> reaches ALL users across ALL branches.
  - **Why it matters** : A branch manager with push-notifications_create can escalate a branch-local promo into a cross-tenant global broadcast simply by forcing branch_id=0 in the request (the Vue UI sources it from the sender's own branch, but the API does not enforce that). In V1 single-branch this is latent, but it is a real branch-isolation weakening that becomes exploitable the moment a second branch/staff exists.
  - **Reproduce** : As a branch_id=1 staff with push-notifications_create, POST /api/admin/push-notification with branch_id=0, role_id=0, user_id=0 -> backend broadcasts to every tenant's users; no 403.
  - **Correction** : In store() (or PushNotificationRequest), force branch_id to the authenticated non-admin user's branch via AdminController::authorizeWritableBranchScope(), and add Rule::exists('branches','id'); only Admin/Tenant Admin may target branch_id=0 (global).
  - **RED verdict** : Confirmed exactly. PushNotificationService.php:63 sets branch_id=request->branch_id direct, no clamp. PushNotificationRequest authorize() returns true; branch_id validated only as required|numeric (no Rule::exists). PushNotificationController::store (lines 37-44) never calls branch-scope authz. The 2026-05-18 tenant-isolation heal only filters when pushBranchId!==0, so a branch-scoped sender POSTing branch_id=0 + role/user 0 reaches every tenant's users with no 403. Real branch-isolation weakening, latent in single-branch V1 (UI sources branch_id from sender), exploitable on a second branch. P2 correct.
  - **Self-audit re-rating** : P2 → P3
  - **Self-audit note** : Self-audit re-rating P2->P3: push-notification target lacks Rule::exists + branch-ownership — V2 multi-tenant hardening; low risk on V1 single-branch.

- **[P2] Push send swallows every per-token FCM error silently — failed/invalid tokens never logged or pruned, admin sees success for a no-op send** — `app/Services/FirebaseService.php:56-66` (functional, code)
  - **What** : sendNotification() posts to FCM per token inside try{...}catch(\Throwable $th){} with an EMPTY catch body (lines 61-62) — every HTTP failure (401 bad credentials, 404 invalid registration token, network) is discarded. The outer catch only Log::info()s. The method returns void; the controller always responds 200 with the created row. There is no dead-token cleanup (invalid web_token/device_token are never nulled), and no per-token failure surfaced.
  - **Why it matters** : An admin can fire a 'broadcast' that silently delivers to zero devices (e.g. expired FCM access token or all-stale tokens) and still gets a success toast. Stale tokens accumulate forever, degrading future sends. For a marketing broadcast feature, 'did it actually send and to whom' is unanswerable from the UI.
  - **Reproduce** : Misconfigure FCM (or use an expired token). Trigger a push -> controller returns 200, alert shows success, no error logged at warning/error level, recipients receive nothing.
  - **Correction** : Log per-token failures at warning level with the FCM error code; on UNREGISTERED/INVALID_ARGUMENT responses, null out the offending web_token/device_token so they are not retried. Optionally return a delivered/failed count to the UI.
  - **RED verdict** : Confirmed. FirebaseService::sendNotification posts to FCM per token inside try{...}catch(Throwable){} with an EMPTY catch body (FirebaseService.php:70-72) — every per-token HTTP failure is silently discarded; the outer catch only Log::info; method returns void so the controller always responds 200. No dead-token pruning. For a broadcast feature 'did it send and to whom' is unanswerable. Real reliability/observability defect. P2 correct.

- **[P3] Coupon::isUsableNow() checks max_uses_global against the dead usage_count column (always 0) — global cap unenforced for any caller relying on the model alone** — `app/Models/Coupon.php:150-155 ; app/Services/CouponService.php:236,449-459` (logic, code)
  - **What** : Coupon::isUsableNow() (line 152) gates max_uses_global with `(int)$this->usage_count >= max_uses_global`, but usage_count is only ever written to 0 at create (CouponService.php:236) and NEVER incremented anywhere (grep usage_count across app/ shows only the cast, the seed-to-0, the resource echo, and this check). The CouponService order path compensates by separately counting OrderCoupon rows (lines 453-459) BEFORE calling isUsableNow, so the HTTP order path is safe — but any code that trusts isUsableNow() for the global cap (e.g. a future kiosk_promo/availability listing) gets a check that can never trip.
  - **Why it matters** : The model method advertises a global-usage cap that is structurally inert. It is a correctness trap: the comment at CouponService:449 acknowledges usage_count is a dead column, yet the model's own guard still reads it, so the two enforcement sites disagree. A maintainer reusing isUsableNow() would ship an unenforced max_uses_global.
  - **Reproduce** : Create coupon max_uses_global=1, redeem it once (OrderCoupon row created, usage_count stays 0). Call $coupon->fresh()->isUsableNow() -> returns true (cap not tripped) even though the global limit is reached.
  - **Correction** : Either remove the usage_count branch from isUsableNow() and make it count OrderCoupon (matching CouponService), or actually increment usage_count atomically on redemption. Drop the dead column if unused.
  - **RED verdict** : Confirmed maintainer-trap, NOT a live exploit. Coupon::isUsableNow (Coupon.php:151-154) gates max_uses_global against usage_count, a dead column (grep across app/+migrations: only fillable/cast Coupon.php:37,59, seed-to-0 CouponService.php:236, resource echo CouponResource.php:57, this check — never incremented). The LIVE HTTP path is SAFE because validateCouponForOrder independently counts OrderCoupon rows for the global cap before calling isUsableNow (CouponService.php:454-458, comment at :450 acknowledges usage_count is dead). Defect = the two enforcement sites disagree and any future reuse of isUsableNow ships an unenforced cap. P2 correct.
  - **Self-audit re-rating** : P2 → P3
  - **Self-audit note** : Self-audit re-rating P2->P3: Coupon::isUsableNow() max_uses_global check is a latent trap / dead path (every live caller pre-checks); frame as deadcode, not an active over-redemption hole.

- **[P2] Coupon limit_per_user and max_uses_global enforced via non-atomic count-then-create (TOCTOU) — concurrent redemptions can exceed the cap** — `app/Services/CouponService.php:437-459 ; app/Services/OrderService.php:589-595` (logic, code)
  - **What** : validateCouponForOrder() enforces both per-user (OrderCoupon::where(user_id,coupon_id)->count() >= limit, lines 439-447) and global (OrderCoupon::where(coupon_id)->count() >= max, lines 454-458) caps by a plain COUNT, then OrderCoupon::create() happens later during order persistence (OrderService.php:589). No row lock, no unique constraint, no SELECT ... FOR UPDATE between the count and the insert. Two concurrent checkouts each read count=limit-1 and both insert.
  - **Why it matters** : A single-use or limited promo can be redeemed N times under concurrency, over-discounting beyond the configured cap. CLAUDE.md notes single-box V1 has 'same non-atomic semantics' as limit_per_user (so this is acknowledged/low-risk for one machine), but it is a real correctness gap that becomes a money leak in any multi-terminal or cloud deployment.
  - **Reproduce** : Coupon limit_per_user=1. Fire two near-simultaneous order creations for the same user with the same coupon -> both pass validation (count==0<1) and both create OrderCoupon rows; user redeemed twice.
  - **Correction** : Add a UNIQUE(user_id, coupon_id) DB constraint (or a redemption ledger with unique key) for single-use-per-user, and wrap the global-cap check + insert in a locked transaction (Cache::lock or SELECT FOR UPDATE) mirroring the fiscal-sequence triple-defense pattern.
  - **RED verdict** : Confirmed TOCTOU. Per-user and global caps enforced via plain COUNT (CouponService.php:438-458), then OrderCoupon::create happens later in persistence (OrderService.php:589-595). Verified NO unique constraint: migration 2022_11_17_120625_create_order_coupons_table.php declares no unique(user_id,coupon_id), and there is no Cache::lock / SELECT FOR UPDATE between count and insert. Concurrent redemptions can exceed the cap. Single-box V1 low-risk (acknowledged same non-atomic semantics), real over-discount/money-leak on multi-terminal/cloud. P2 correct.

- **[P3] Coupon active/inactive toggle has no confirmation — one click silently activates/deactivates a live promo** — `resources/js/components/admin/coupons/CouponListComponent.vue:182-188,382-398` (ux, code)
  - **What** : The toggle-status icon button @click="toggleStatus(coupon)" dispatches coupon/toggleStatus immediately (CouponListComponent.vue:383-398) with no confirm() dialog, unlike destroy() which typically confirms. Backend CouponController::toggleStatus + CouponService::toggleStatus flip ACTIVE<->INACTIVE and persist instantly. A mis-click deactivates a running promo (customers' codes start failing) with only a success toast.
  - **Why it matters** : Deactivating an active coupon is a customer-facing state change. A guardless single-click toggle in a dense table row is easy to fumble; there is no 'are you sure' and no visual diff before/after beyond the badge. Low blast radius but a polish/UX gap on a state-mutating control.
  - **Reproduce** : On the coupon list, accidentally click the eye/eye-off icon -> coupon status flips with no confirmation.
  - **Correction** : Add a lightweight confirm (or an undo toast) on toggleStatus, especially when deactivating a currently-active coupon.
  - **RED verdict** : Confirmed. CouponListComponent.vue:181-188 toggle button -> toggleStatus (lines 382-398) dispatches coupon/toggleStatus immediately with no confirm() (unlike destroy), instant ACTIVE<->INACTIVE flip on a live promo, success toast only. Low blast radius, state-mutating UX polish gap. P3 correct.

**Dropped by adversarial pass:**
- ~~[P3] Coupon UI fully operational but coupon discount is REFUSED at order time when discount kill-switch is off — create/validate/apply disconnect~~ — Mechanically accurate: CouponService::couponChecking->resolveCouponByCode->validateCouponForOrder returns a redeemable discount with no kill-switch awareness, while order chokepoints throw via assertDiscretionaryDiscountAllowed (OrderService.php:2863-2870). But the P1 'broken promise to customer' premise is FALSE in both flag states: (1) the recommended fix 'hide coupon entry on customer surfaces' is ALREADY implemented — master.blade.php:161 exposes discountsEnabled=config('pos.manual_discount_enabled'), CheckoutComponent.vue:219 gates <CouponComponent v-if=discountsEnabled>, KioskCartComponent.vue:275,328 gate the kiosk promo; (2) the live default is flag=true (ManualDiscountDisabledV1SentinelTest docstring: 'default is now ENABLED') so coupons redeem end-to-end with no dead-end. Only true residual = raw /coupon-checking API does not honor the flag (defense-in-depth on a UI-unreachable path). Downgrade P1->P3.

**Coverage gaps (RED):**
- OFFERS sub-surface entirely unaudited — all 8 findings hit only coupons + push-notifications; no finding covers offer model/service/UI, offer scheduling/validity, or offer-vs-coupon interplay.
- MESSAGES sub-surface entirely unaudited — no finding covers Message model/controller, message broadcast/targeting, or message branch-isolation; given S13-04's push branch_id=0 cross-tenant gap, Message broadcast deserves the same Rule::exists/ownership check.
- No sentinel/boot-guard pins order.total_tax == Z per-rate netting for a discounted order (the coherence S13-02 breaks). ManualDiscountDisabledV1SentinelTest only exercises the kill-switch=false refusal path, never the enabled-and-correct path now that the F1 default is ENABLED.
- Raw /coupon-checking + /apply-coupon API endpoints do not honor config('pos.manual_discount_enabled') — only the Vue surfaces are gated via discountsEnabled (master.blade.php:161). Defense-in-depth gap on the API (S13-01 residual): a direct API caller still gets a redeemable preview when the kill-switch is engaged.
- No FCM credential health/preflight check found; a fully misconfigured FCM (expired access token) yields a 'successful' broadcast to zero devices with no error-level log (ties to S13-05's swallowed errors).

### S14-kds — S14-kds
Files examined: `resources/js/components/admin/kitchenDisplaySystem/KitchenDisplaySystemComponent.vue`, `resources/js/components/admin/kitchenDisplaySystem/KdsV2Grid.vue`, `resources/js/components/admin/kitchenDisplaySystem/KdsOrderCard.vue`, `resources/js/components/admin/kitchenDisplaySystem/KdsOrderLine.vue`, `resources/js/components/admin/kitchenDisplaySystem/KdsStatusBanner.vue`, `resources/js/components/admin/kitchenDisplaySystem/KdsHistoryDrawer.vue`, `resources/js/store/modules/kds.js`, `resources/js/store/modules/kitchenDisplaySystemOrder.js`, `resources/js/helpers/kdsDisplay.js`, `resources/js/helpers/kdsCustomization.js`, `resources/js/services/WebSocketService.js`, `resources/js/services/kdsSyncService.js`, `resources/js/bootstrap.js`, `app/Http/Controllers/Admin/KitchenDisplaySystemController.php`, `app/Services/KitchenDisplaySystemOrderService.php`, `app/Services/KdsSyncService.php`, `app/Domain/Kds/KitchenReleaseRule.php`, `app/Listeners/DispatchKdsTicket.php`, `app/Listeners/PersistOrderCreatedToOutbox.php`, `app/Providers/EventServiceProvider.php`, `routes/api.php`, `routes/channels.php`, `database/seeders/UserTableSeeder.php`, `resources/js/languages/fr.json`, `resources/js/languages/en.json`

**Confirmed corrections:**

- **[P2] KDS sous compte admin (branch_id=0) n'a AUCUN push temps-reel : nouvelle commande visible jusqu'a 60s plus tard, sans aucun indicateur** — `resources/js/components/admin/kitchenDisplaySystem/KitchenDisplaySystemComponent.vue:1911-1914 + :1889-1896 + resources/js/services/kdsSyncService.js:301-302 + resources/js/services/WebSocketService.js:79-111` (logic, code)
  - **What** : subscribeEcho() (KitchenDisplaySystemComponent.vue:1913-1914) fait `const branchId = this.authBranchId(); if (branchId <= 0) return;` -> un admin (branch_id=0) ne s'abonne JAMAIS au canal Echo `branch.{id}`, donc ne recoit ni OrderCreated ni OrderStatusChanged en push. Par ailleurs WebSocketService.js:83-86/108-111 met _state=CONNECTED des que le SOCKET Pusher brut est 'connected' (niveau socket, PAS abonnement canal) -> wsConnected devient true (component _bindWsService:1871-1872). Consequence: (a) _pollingInterval() (:1896) renvoie 60000ms quand wsConnected, (b) le poller adaptatif kdsSyncService renvoie interval:Infinity quand WS_CONNECTED (kdsSyncService.js:301-302) donc s'ARRETE. Resultat net pour un admin: pas d'Echo + poll passif 60s + sync adaptatif stoppe. La banniere 'compte central en polling' qui pourrait avertir est explicitement MASQUEE en mono-branche (kdsIsCentralAdmin:1351-1361 renvoie false si branchCount<=1). admin@lecayenne.fr est seede avec branch_id=0 (UserTableSeeder.php:34-44).
  - **Why it matters** : Sur le box V1 Le Cayenne mono-poste, si la cuisine est ouverte avec le compte admin (le compte 'tout-en-un' du proprietaire, branch_id=0, defaut), une commande borne/POS peut rester invisible jusqu'a 60 secondes en plein rush, sans aucun signal visuel ni sonore pendant ce delai. Le systeme assimile 'socket connecte' a 'push temps-reel actif', ce qui est FAUX pour branch_id=0. Le carillon ne se declenche qu'a l'arrivee reelle de la commande (watcher orders:1432-1443), donc pas de perte permanente, mais une latence de service inacceptable pour de la restauration rapide (cible prep < 5 min). NOTE: si la cuisine se logue avec chef@lecayenne.fr (branch_id=1, seede UserTableSeeder.php:101-115), Echo fonctionne et le bug ne se produit PAS. Le defaut est donc CONDITIONNEL au modele de login.
  - **Reproduce** : 1) Se loguer KDS avec admin@lecayenne.fr (branch_id=0). 2) Verifier console: aucun abonnement `branch.0` (subscribeEcho retourne tot). 3) Confirmer window._wsService.state === 'connected'. 4) Passer une commande borne payee. 5) Observer: la carte n'apparait qu'au prochain tick de poll (jusqu'a 60s), pas instantanement. Comparatif: meme test logue chef@lecayenne.fr (branch_id=1) -> apparition sub-seconde.
  - **Correction** : Pour un admin mono-branche, soit (a) s'abonner au canal Echo de la branche reelle (resoudre la branche unique cote front via foodkingConfig.branchCount===1 -> branch.1) au lieu du early-return, soit (b) ne PAS basculer le polling a 60s / ne PAS stopper kdsSyncService tant qu'aucun abonnement Echo KDS actif n'existe (garder 5s). Idealement: decoupler wsConnected (socket) de 'pushKdsActif' (abonnement au canal de la commande). A minima documenter que la cuisine DOIT utiliser chef@lecayenne.fr (branch_id=1) et afficher la banniere polling meme en mono-branche.
  - **RED verdict** : VERIFIED. subscribeEcho early-returns for branch_id<=0 (KitchenDisplaySystemComponent.vue:1913-1914), admin@lecayenne.fr is branch_id=0 (UserTableSeeder.php:34-44), chef@lecayenne.fr branch_id=1 (UserTableSeeder.php:101-115). _pollingInterval returns 60000ms when wsConnected (line ~1896 _pollingInterval), kdsSyncService _baseCadence returns interval:Infinity on WS_CONNECTED (kdsSyncService.js:301-303), wsConnected becomes true on raw socket 'connected' (WebSocketService.js:80-86, not channel subscription). BROADCAST_DRIVER=pusher -> 127.0.0.1:6001 local Reverb IS part of box, so socket CAN connect and wsConnected CAN become true for admin -> bug is reachable, NOT dormant. central-admin polling banner hidden mono-branch (kdsIsCentralAdmin:1351-1361 returns false when branchCount<=1). HOWEVER downgraded P1->P2: TWO conditions must both hold (kitchen logged in as admin branch_id=0 AND WS socket up) and chef@lecayenne.fr (branch_id=1, 'toujours cree') is the intended kitchen account for which Echo works correctly. Chime watcher (orders:1432-1443) still fires on actual arrival so NO permanent loss, only up-to-60s latency conditional on login model. Real latency defect, conditional, well-mitigated -> P2.

- **[P3] Conflit 409 sur le board V2 (defaut prod) affiche une cle i18n inexistante au lieu du message d'avertissement** — `resources/js/components/admin/kitchenDisplaySystem/KitchenDisplaySystemComponent.vue:1646` (ux, code)
  - **What** : Le chemin V2 onV2ChangeStatus (chemin de PRODUCTION par defaut depuis 2026-05-16, computed useV2Layout:1247-1264) gere le 409 ainsi: `this.kdsErrorBanner.message = this.$t('label.kds_status_conflict')` (ligne 1646). Or la cle n'existe QUE sous le namespace `message`: verifie via node -> `label.kds_status_conflict => undefined`, `message.kds_status_conflict => 'Cette commande a ete modifiee ailleurs...'`. Le chemin legacy (ligne 2280) utilise correctement `message.kds_status_conflict`. Donc en V2 la banniere d'erreur affiche soit la chaine brute 'label.kds_status_conflict' soit du vide selon le fallback i18n.
  - **Why it matters** : Quand deux postes/onglets bumpent la meme commande (ou apres un refresh decale), le backend renvoie 409 (changeStatus optimistic-lock, KitchenDisplaySystemOrderService.php:405-418). Le chef doit comprendre 'la commande a ete modifiee ailleurs, verifie l'etat'. A la place il voit une cle technique brute ou une banniere vide -> confusion, risque de re-clic. Faible frequence (mono-chef la plupart du temps) d'ou P2, mais c'est un raw-label expose au personnel en exploitation, exactement le type de 'flou' a corriger.
  - **Reproduce** : Ouvrir 2 onglets KDS sur la meme commande PREPARING, cliquer Pret dans les deux. Le 2e POST renvoie 409. Observer la banniere d'erreur: 'label.kds_status_conflict' (cle brute) au lieu du texte FR.
  - **Correction** : Ligne 1646: remplacer `this.$t('label.kds_status_conflict')` par `this.$t('message.kds_status_conflict')` (cle existante, deja utilisee par le chemin legacy 2280).
  - **RED verdict** : PARTIALLY MISDESCRIBED. Line 1646 confirmed: this.$t('label.kds_status_conflict'). Verified via node: message.kds_status_conflict EXISTS (fr.json:1464), label.kds_status_conflict is UNDEFINED -> vue-i18n renders raw key string. Line 2280 legacy correctly uses message namespace. BUT the finding's 409 trigger is FALSE: onV2ChangeStatus catch block (1640-1647) does `if (err.response.status===409){ this._debouncedRefresh(); return; }` BEFORE reaching line 1646 -> on 409 the banner is intentionally SILENT, never hits 1646. Line 1646 only fires on NON-409 errors (500/network). For those errors, 'kds_status_conflict' (="modified elsewhere") is the WRONG message regardless of namespace. So the title/why_it_matters/reproduction (all '409 -> raw label') will NOT reproduce. The underlying raw-label/undefined-namespace bug at 1646 is real but rare (only non-409 errors) and the surfaced text is semantically wrong anyway. Downgraded P2->P3: real raw-label exposure but misframed trigger + low frequency + wrong-message-regardless. Fix: use a generic error i18n key on the non-409 branch, not message.kds_status_conflict.
  - **Self-audit note** : Self-audit reasoning fix: this is a raw missing-i18n label (KitchenDisplaySystemComponent.vue:1646 this.$t('label....')), NOT the 409-conflict path. Keep P3.

- **[P3] Board V2 limite a 8 cartes (slice 0,8) + raccourcis clavier A-H seulement : commandes 9+ visibles uniquement via une puce de comptage, non actionnables tant qu'une des 8 ne sort pas** — `resources/js/components/admin/kitchenDisplaySystem/KdsV2Grid.vue:55-56 + :247-249 + :291-307` (ux, code)
  - **What** : Le grid rend `activeOrders.slice(0, 8)` (KdsV2Grid.vue:56). overflowActiveCount (:247-249) = max(0, activeOrders.length-8) alimente une puce d'alerte (:82-90) mais AUCUNE carte n'est rendue pour les commandes 9+. Les raccourcis clavier ne couvrent que SHORTCUTS=['A'..'H'] (:140) et onKey teste `idx < this.activeOrders.length` mais l'index max reste 7 (:296-298). Pas de scroll/pagination du grid. Le tri est FIFO (plus ancienne d'abord, visibleOrders:190-201) donc la #9 finit par remonter quand le chef bumpe les 8 plus anciennes — ce n'est PAS une perte permanente.
  - **Why it matters** : En rush (>8 commandes actives simultanees), le chef voit '+N en attente' mais ne peut ni lire le detail ni bumper les commandes 9+ avant d'avoir traite des plus anciennes. Risque de priorisation/visibilite en heure de pointe (une commande urgente coincee derriere 8 cartes), pas une commande silencieusement oubliee. La puce overflow est une vraie mitigation deja en place (commentaire Wave N M-KDS-6 F1). Reste un plafond ergonomique a >8 commandes.
  - **Reproduce** : Injecter 10+ commandes ACCEPT/PREPARING actives sur une branche. Observer le grid: 8 cartes + puce '+2 en attente'. Confirmer qu'aucune interaction n'est possible sur la 9e/10e tant que les 8 visibles ne sont pas bumpees.
  - **Correction** : Permettre l'acces aux commandes 9+ : soit scroll vertical du grid avec densite reduite, soit pagination/'voir plus' depliant les cartes suivantes, soit etendre les raccourcis au-dela de H. La puce overflow peut rester comme filet de securite mais ne doit pas etre le seul acces aux commandes en surplus.
  - **RED verdict** : VERIFIED. KdsV2Grid.vue:56 renders activeOrders.slice(0,8); overflowActiveCount (lines ~245-249)=max(0,len-8) feeds an informational chip only, no card rendered for 9+. SHORTCUTS=['A'..'H'] (8 letters), onKey indexes activeOrders[idx] but idx capped at 7. Confirmed kdsOverflowSeeMore (KitchenDisplaySystemComponent.vue:2160-2164) is UNRELATED: it clears search filters and re-lists for the backend 50-order cap (kdsOrderListAtCap banner line 116), NOT the V2 8-card ceiling -> no mitigation path to actionable 9+. FIFO sort means #9 surfaces once 8 oldest bumped -> NOT silently forgotten, ergonomic ceiling only. Wave N M-KDS-6 overflow chip is a genuine partial mitigation. Real >8-active rush-hour visibility/prioritization concern but no order loss -> downgraded P2->P3 (auditor's own framing 'pas une commande silencieusement oubliee' supports P3).

### S15-oss — S15-oss
Files examined: `resources/js/components/admin/orderStatusScreen/OrderStatusScreenComponent.vue`, `resources/js/components/admin/orderStatusScreen/PreparingAndReadyComponent.vue`, `resources/js/components/admin/orderStatusScreen/PopularItemComponent.vue`, `app/Http/Controllers/Admin/OrderStatusScreenController.php`, `app/Services/OrderStatusScreenOrderService.php`, `app/Http/Resources/CDSOrderDetailsResource.php`, `app/Http/Resources/PosShortcutOrderResource.php`, `resources/js/store/modules/orderStatusScreenOrder.js`, `resources/js/services/OssSyncService.js`, `resources/js/router/modules/orderStatusScreenRoutes.js`, `resources/js/router/index.js`, `routes/api.php`, `config/oss.php`, `app/Enums/OrderType.php`, `app/Enums/OrderStatus.php`, `resources/js/enums/modules/orderStatusEnum.js`, `app/Services/OrderService.php (queue_number allocation 2937-3008, POS status 744-808)`, `app/Services/KitchenDisplaySystemOrderService.php (POS visibility line 79)`, `resources/js/languages/fr.json (label.preparing/ready/oss_main_aria)`

**Confirmed corrections:**

- **[P3] POS comptoir orders never reach the PRET wall though they carry a queue number and are prepared in the kitchen** — `app/Services/OrderStatusScreenOrderService.php:59-62 and :220-223 (whereIn order_type [KIOSK, TAKEAWAY])` (functional, code)
  - **What** : list() and listForBranch() both hard-restrict to whereIn('order_type', [OrderType::KIOSK(25), OrderType::TAKEAWAY(10)]) (service lines 59-62 / 220-223). Yet a POS (order_type=15) walk-in order DOES get a queue_number: saveOrderWithQueueNumber()/allocateQueueNumber() at OrderService.php:2937-2993 stamps 'A0001'-style numbers regardless of type, and it IS prepared in the kitchen: posInitialStatus auto-promotes to OrderStatus::PREPARING (OrderService.php:758,778,803) and KitchenDisplaySystemOrderService.php:79 explicitly handles order_type=OrderType::POS. So a POS order transitions PREPARING->PREPARED in KDS but is filtered out of OSS. The customer-facing N°{{queue_number}} chip is shown for POS on PosComponent.vue:295/359, so the counter customer is given a N° but that N° never appears on the PRET wall.
  - **Why it matters** : On a single-counter fast-food (V1 Le Cayenne) the OSS wall is the pickup-call signal. A walk-in who paid at the cashier receives a queue number (N°A0001) but their order never lights up the green PRET column when the kitchen bumps it ready, while a kiosk customer with the identical number does. Either the counter staff must hand-call every walk-in verbally (defeating the wall) or the customer waits staring at a wall that will never show their number. This is an inconsistent, confusing pickup experience for half the order volume. If the owner intends counter orders to be hand-delivered directly this is acceptable-by-design, but the current state silently mixes the two models.
  - **Reproduce** : Create a POS counter order at /admin/pos (pay at counter), bump it to PREPARED in KDS. Observe N°A000N appears in KDS/PosComponent but NEVER in the OSS PRET column at /admin/order-status-screen. A kiosk order created the same minute does appear.
  - **Correction** : Confirm the pickup model with the owner. If the wall is the universal pickup signal, add OrderType::POS to the allowlist in BOTH list() (line 59-62) and listForBranch() (line 220-223), keeping them byte-identical per the service docstring. If counter orders are hand-delivered by design, leave as-is but document the rationale next to the allowlist so future audits don't re-flag it.
  - **RED verdict** : VERIFIED. OrderStatusScreenOrderService.php:59-62 and :220-223 hard-restrict to whereIn order_type [KIOSK(25), TAKEAWAY(10)] (OrderType.php:8-11 confirms values). POS=15 orders DO carry a queue_number (saveOrderWithQueueNumber unconditional at OrderService.php:2937-2950) and ARE kitchen-prepared (posInitialStatus auto-promotes to PREPARING via AutoPrepareOnPaidPolicy at OrderService.php:763-781; KDS service line 79 explicitly includes OrderType::POS+CASH). So a POS order reaches PREPARED in KDS but is filtered out of OSS. REAL inconsistency. BUT severity overstated as P1: (1) the whereIn allowlist is the deliberate [2026-05-18 RED R-3 heal] (lines 53-58) with a sister lock test OssCustomerScreenFilterTest.php — adding POS reverts a tested security/scoping decision; (2) the auditor's 'customer is given a N°' citation PosComponent.vue:295/359 is the cashier shortcut widget (class pos-shortcuts__num), NOT the customer-facing display, so that part of the evidence is unsupported. This is an owner product-model question (is the wall the universal pickup signal, or are counter orders hand-delivered?), not a clear code defect. Downgrade to P2; is_necessary=true only in the sense that the rationale must be confirmed/documented next to the allowlist.
  - **Self-audit re-rating** : P2 → P3
  - **Self-audit note** : Self-audit re-rating P2->P3 / design-decision: OSS list restricts to order_type [KIOSK, TAKEAWAY] by design (same allowlist the POS box audit flagged), so POS-counter orders not reaching the PRET wall may be intentional. OWNER to confirm whether counter orders should appear on the customer screen.

- **[P2] First load fires a flash+chime storm: every already-PREPARED order is treated as newly-ready on mount** — `resources/js/components/admin/orderStatusScreen/PreparingAndReadyComponent.vue:385-402 (_hydrateFromRows) called from list() at :403-415 on mount :114` (logic, code)
  - **What** : _hydrateFromRows computes prevPreparedIds from the CURRENT this.preparedItems (line 386). On the very first mount call (mounted()->list()->_hydrateFromRows), this.preparedItems is still the initial [] (data() line 90), so prevPreparedIds is empty and the loop at 393-397 calls _markNewReady(item.id) for EVERY order already in PREPARED state. _markNewReady (326-343) sets newReadyFlash=true and calls _playReadySound(). There is no first-load / hasHydratedOnce guard.
  - **Why it matters** : Open or refresh the wall at lunch rush with 8 orders already in PRET and the column green-flashes and pulses all 8 at once, and on an operator-attended surface (authBranchId>0, audio unlocked) the 4-tone chime fires up to 8 times overlapping. The flash/chime is supposed to mean 'a NEW order just became ready' (the attention signal customers rely on); firing it for the entire backlog on every page load / TV reboot trains customers and staff to ignore it, eroding the one realtime cue the wall exists to provide.
  - **Reproduce** : Have >=2 orders in PREPARED, then load/refresh /admin/order-status-screen. Observe the ready column flashes and (on authed surface) multiple chimes fire simultaneously for pre-existing orders.
  - **Correction** : Add a one-shot guard: on the first _hydrateFromRows after mount, seed prevPreparedIds from the incoming rows (or set a this._hydratedOnce flag) so _markNewReady only fires for transitions observed AFTER initial load.
  - **RED verdict** : PARTIALLY VERIFIED — visual storm REAL, chime storm OVERSTATED. _hydrateFromRows (line 385-402) computes prevPreparedIds from current this.preparedItems (386); on first mount preparedItems=[] (data line 90), so every already-PREPARED order hits _markNewReady (393-397). No hasHydratedOnce guard exists — confirmed. The VISUAL flash/pulse storm (newReadyFlash + per-card oss-pulse-ready) is real and matters because on the public wall (authBranchId<=0) the flash is the ONLY signal. HOWEVER the auditor's 'up to 8 overlapping chimes' claim is NOT reproducible: _audioCtx starts null (line 105) and is created only on the first pointerdown/keydown gesture AFTER mount (lines 142-147, {once:true}). On a fresh load/refresh, mounted()->list()->_hydrateFromRows runs BEFORE any gesture, so _playReadySound hits `if(!ctx) return` at line 363 — no chime. Correct the evidence: chime cannot storm on load; the visual-only first-load storm justifies P2.

- **[P3] Stale-prune window (now-8h) silently drops still-active overdue advance orders, contradicting the documented anti-zombie intent** — `app/Services/OrderStatusScreenOrderService.php:98-101 vs :132 (and mirror :243-245 vs :256)` (logic, code)
  - **What** : The advance-order OR-branch (lines 98-101) is explicitly commented '[AUDIT-52-BUG1] show ALL overdue advance orders ... Prevents zombie disappearance' and intentionally has NO lower datetime bound for active advance orders. But that whole date-window group is ANDed with the top-level stale prune at line 132: ->where('order_datetime', '>=', now(app.timezone)->subHours(config('oss.stale_window_hours',8))). So an advance order placed >8h ago that is still PREPARING/PREPARED is pruned by line 132 regardless, defeating the 'show ALL overdue advance orders' branch. The two documented intents directly contradict each other.
  - **Why it matters** : An advance order (commande a l'avance) prepared more than 8h after it was placed will vanish from the wall while still genuinely active and awaiting pickup. The comment promises the opposite ('prevents zombie disappearance'). This is a real flou: a reader cannot tell which behavior is the intended one, and the implemented behavior (prune wins) silently breaks the stated guarantee. Low frequency on a fast-food single-shift, but it is a latent correctness ambiguity the owner flagged ('things that are not good, unnecessary things').
  - **Reproduce** : Create an advance order with order_datetime > 8h in the past, status PREPARED, is_advance_order=YES. It satisfies the advance OR-branch (98-101) but is excluded by line 132 stale prune. Query returns 0 rows for it.
  - **Correction** : Decide one model and make the code match the comment. Either exempt active advance orders from the stale_window prune (move line 132 to apply only to the non-advance branch), or update the [AUDIT-52-BUG1] comment to state advance orders are also capped at stale_window_hours. Mirror in listForBranch().
  - **RED verdict** : VERIFIED. The advance-order OR-branch (lines 98-101) intentionally has no lower datetime bound ('show ALL overdue advance orders ... Prevents zombie disappearance', [AUDIT-52-BUG1]) and claims to 'Mirror KDS fix'. But that group is ANDed with the top-level stale prune at line 132 (order_datetime >= now()->subHours(stale_window_hours,8); config/oss.php:29 confirms default 8). CONFIRMED CONTRADICTION via sister-service comparison: KitchenDisplaySystemOrderService::list (lines 130-141) has the IDENTICAL advance branch but NO subHours stale prune — KDS retains overdue advance orders that OSS line 132 silently drops. So OSS does NOT mirror KDS for >8h advance orders, directly contradicting its own comment. Real comment-vs-code ambiguity. Mirror present in listForBranch (:243-245 vs :256). Severity P3 not P2 — the finding itself concedes 'low frequency on a fast-food single-shift'; it is a latent ambiguity, not an active fast-food defect.

- **[P3] Entire popular-items chain is dead on the OSS surface and its store getter reads an uninitialized state key** — `resources/js/components/admin/orderStatusScreen/OrderStatusScreenComponent.vue:11-21 (PopularItemComponent un-mounted); store getter orderStatusScreenOrder.js:13-14` (deadcode, code)
  - **What** : OrderStatusScreenComponent.vue mounts ONLY <PreparingAndReadyComponent /> (line 27); the [Wave Q-3] comment (11-16) confirms PopularItemComponent was removed from the layout. grep across resources/js shows admin/orderStatusScreen/PopularItemComponent.vue is imported by NOTHING (the only 'PopularItemComponent' imports are the unrelated frontend/home one). The store getter mostPopularItems (orderStatusScreenOrder.js:13-14) returns state.mostPopularItems, but state only declares { lists: [] } (line 6-8) - mostPopularItems is never initialized, so the getter returns undefined. The whole chain is dead on OSS: admin PopularItemComponent.vue + store action/getter/mutation mostPopularItems + OrderStatusScreenController::mostPopularItems & publicMostPopularItems + routes api.php:1218 & :1291-1293 + OrderStatusScreenOrderService::mostPopularItems (169-195).
  - **Why it matters** : Dead surface area: a Vue component, 2 controller methods, 2 public routes (throttled, enumerable), a service method with a DB query, and a store module branch all maintained for a panel no longer rendered. It also carries a latent bug (uninitialized getter) that would silently break if re-mounted. Carrying this confuses future auditors (the store comment still claims '4 callers') and is exactly the 'unnecessary things' the owner called out. Note the routes still resolve and could be polled directly.
  - **Reproduce** : grep -rn 'orderStatusScreen/PopularItemComponent' resources/js -> no importer. Read OrderStatusScreenComponent.vue template -> only PreparingAndReadyComponent mounted.
  - **Correction** : If popular-items is permanently dropped from OSS (owner Wave Q-3 directive), delete admin/orderStatusScreen/PopularItemComponent.vue, the mostPopularItems store action/getter/mutation, controller mostPopularItems/publicMostPopularItems, the 2 routes, and service mostPopularItems. If it may return, at minimum initialize state.mostPopularItems = [] to remove the latent undefined-getter bug and update the stale '4 callers' comment.
  - **RED verdict** : VERIFIED and the dead-code claim is STRONGER than stated. OrderStatusScreenComponent.vue mounts ONLY <PreparingAndReadyComponent /> (line 27); [Wave Q-3] comment (11-21) confirms PopularItemComponent removed. grep across resources/js: admin/orderStatusScreen/PopularItemComponent.vue has ZERO import statements (only the unrelated frontend/home/PopularItemComponent is imported by HomeComponent). The OrderStatusScreenComponent.vue comment claim 'still referenced by OssSyncService + admin PosComponent dashboard widget' is FALSE — grep of OssSyncService.js and PosComponent.vue returns no PopularItemComponent/mostPopularItems reference. Store getter mostPopularItems (orderStatusScreenOrder.js:13-14) returns state.mostPopularItems, but state only declares {lists:[]} (line 6) — getter returns undefined (latent bug confirmed). Controller methods mostPopularItems/publicMostPopularItems exist (OrderStatusScreenController.php:40,116); routes api.php:1218 & :1291-1293 exist; service mostPopularItems exists (:169-195). Dead chain fully confirmed. P3 appropriate — owner-gated cleanup, latent not active.

- **[P3] wsConnected reactive state is maintained but never read by the template or any computed** — `resources/js/components/admin/orderStatusScreen/PreparingAndReadyComponent.vue:93 (declared), 222-226 / 244-246 (written), never read` (deadcode, code)
  - **What** : data() declares wsConnected (line 93). It is written in _onWsConnected/_onWsDisconnected (222,226) and in the ossSync ws_state handler (244-246). grep of the template (lines 1-67) shows no {{ wsConnected }}, no :class/v-if referencing it, and no computed consumes it. Connection status is owned by the parent ConnectionStatusBanner (OrderStatusScreenComponent.vue:9).
  - **Why it matters** : Two event subscriptions and a Vuex sync handler exist purely to keep a property nothing displays. It is harmless but is exactly the kind of 'flou'/unnecessary plumbing the owner asked to be cleaned: it implies a connection indicator that does not exist on this component, misleading the next maintainer into thinking the wall renders its own WS status.
  - **Reproduce** : grep -n 'wsConnected' on the component: 4 writes, 0 template/computed reads.
  - **Correction** : Either render wsConnected somewhere intentional (a tiny offline dot) or remove the property and the _bindWsService/ws_state writes that exist only to feed it (keep the list() refresh on reconnect, drop the unused flag).
  - **RED verdict** : VERIFIED. wsConnected declared (line 93), written in _onWsConnected/_onWsDisconnected (222,226) and ossSync ws_state handler (245). grep of template (lines 1-67) and computed ({} empty, line 112) confirms ZERO reads — no {{wsConnected}}, no :class/v-if. Connection status is owned by parent ConnectionStatusBanner (OrderStatusScreenComponent.vue:9). Dead reactive flag confirmed. NOTE: the finding correctly scopes the cleanup — _onWsConnected ALSO calls this.list() on reconnect (line 223), which is load-bearing, so only the wsConnected flag and its writes are dead, not the whole _bindWsService binding. P3 cosmetic/maintainability cleanup, harmless.

**Dropped by adversarial pass:**
- ~~[P3] Token fallback can render a raw opaque token string on the customer wall (no N° prefix, unbounded length)~~ — EXISTS but DORMANT/UNREACHABLE in V1 flow — DROP. Template lines 37/62 do render `{{ item.queue_number ? 'N°'+item.queue_number : item.token }}`, and the migration 2022_11_17_110810_create_orders_table.php:23 confirms `$table->string('token')->nullable()` with no length cap. HOWEVER the else-branch (raw token) is unreachable for the allowlisted types: only KIOSK/TAKEAWAY pass the OSS whereIn (service :59-62/:220-223), and BOTH kiosk-originated types ALWAYS get a queue_number — FrontendOrderService::saveFrontendOrderWithQueueNumber assigns queue_number UNCONDITIONALLY (line 984) before save with no nullable path, and the frontend path is restricted to [KIOSK,TAKEAWAY] (FrontendOrderService.php:196). So for every allowed-type order queue_number is non-null and the N° branch always wins; the token else-branch is defensive dead code on this surface. The finding itself rates it P3/medium-confidence/needs-live-verify and concedes 'queue_number is always allocated so this is an edge/latent case'. No reachable defect in V1 — DROP (at most a 1-line defensive-hardening nice-to-have, not worth flagging).

**Coverage gaps (RED):**
- Sync-cadence divergence (TRAP-4, PreparingAndReadyComponent.vue:248-264): the public customer wall (authBranchId<=0) is poll-only and overrides intervalMsWhenConnected to 5000ms, while authed staff OSS (branchId>0) stays at the default 60000ms poll because it relies on Echo push. The auditor did not examine whether the authed staff OSS surface actually receives timely push (subscribeEcho joins branch.{id} OrderStatusChanged/OrderCreated at :280-301) or silently lags up to 60s if Echo push is dropped/degraded — no fallback fast-poll on the authed surface.
- Public-wall chime is structurally dead by design (authBranchId<=0 early-returns in _playReadySound :354 and skips audio-unlock listener wiring :142). The auditor did not flag that the ENTIRE 4-tone chime feature (lines 344-384) plus the wakeLock/AudioContext plumbing is inert on the only unattended surface that the comment (:73-77) says it exists for — i.e. the wall a customer watches. Visual flash is the sole cue; worth an owner note that audible pickup-call is intentionally absent.
- OSS surface confirmed fully read-only: grep of resources/js/components/admin/orderStatusScreen/ found ZERO @click/@change/v-on/button/<a> handlers, so there are no unaudited interactive buttons/flows — the auditor's behavioral coverage of the display itself is complete on that axis.

### S16-kiosk-borne — S16-kiosk-borne
Files examined: `resources/js/components/frontend/kiosk/KioskPaymentComponent.vue`, `resources/js/components/frontend/kiosk/KioskCartComponent.vue`, `resources/js/components/frontend/kiosk/KioskLoginComponent.vue`, `resources/js/components/frontend/kiosk/KioskUpsellComponent.vue`, `resources/js/components/frontend/kiosk/KioskIdleScreenComponent.vue`, `resources/js/components/frontend/kiosk/KioskWizardComponent.vue`, `resources/js/components/frontend/kiosk/KioskWaitingComponent.vue`, `resources/js/store/modules/kioskCart.js`, `resources/js/router/modules/kioskRoutes.js`, `routes/api.php`, `config/kiosk.php`, `resources/views/master.blade.php`, `app/Services/FrontendOrderService.php`, `app/Services/OrderService.php`, `app/Services/PaymentService.php`, `app/Services/Receipt/ReceiptDataService.php`, `app/Models/FrontendOrder.php`

**Confirmed corrections:**

- **[P1] Kiosk-counter-collected order never records the collecting cashier on the order — receipt 'Opérateur' stays the kiosk machine (live Plan B path)** — `app/Services/PaymentService.php:389-401 (confirmCounterPayment) + app/Services/Receipt/ReceiptDataService.php:70 + app/Services/FrontendOrderService.php:260-272` (nf525, code)
  - **What** : Live V1 path (Plan B, see S16-02): kiosk order is created in FrontendOrderService::store with 'user_id => Auth::user()->id' (the KioskMachine user) and NO creator_id/editor_id (lines 260-272). When the cashier encaisses at POS, collectKioskCash -> PaymentService::confirmCounterPayment writes the collecting cashier ONLY into an audit_logs row ('user_id' => Auth::id(), line 391) — it never sets creator_id/editor_id on the order row. ReceiptDataService::buildForOrderModel line 70 builds 'operator_name' => optional($order->user)->name, and FrontendOrder has only a user() relation (FrontendOrder.php:113), no creator()/editor(). So the printed/displayed 'Opérateur' is always the kiosk machine account, never the human who took the money.
  - **Why it matters** : NF525 requires the receipt to identify the operator who processed the sale. On the real Le Cayenne shift EVERY kiosk order is collected at the counter (Plan B default true), so EVERY one of those receipts mis-identifies the operator. The cashier identity exists in audit_logs but is unreachable by the receipt builder. This is the documented operator gap, root-caused here on the LIVE path (not only the dormant TPE path).
  - **Reproduce** : Place a kiosk order (lands PENDING_COUNTER). Encaisse it at POS as cashier X. Inspect the order: orders.user_id = kiosk machine user, creator_id/editor_id unchanged/NULL. ReceiptDataService returns operator_name = machine name. The audit_logs 'order.counter_payment_confirmed' row has the real user_id=X but the receipt never reads it.
  - **Correction** : At collection time in confirmCounterPayment, persist the collecting cashier onto the order (e.g. set editor_id = Auth::id() on $locked before save, line ~362) AND change ReceiptDataService:70 to resolve operator from editor_id/creator_id (the cashier) with a fallback, not from $order->user (the customer/machine). Coordinate with the receipt/POS owners; ReceiptDataService is fiscal-adjacent so gate the change.
  - **RED verdict** : VERIFIED REAL. FrontendOrderService.php:262 creates kiosk order 'user_id => Auth::user()->id' (machine), no creator_id/editor_id. PaymentService.php:193 confirmCounterPayment(Order) writes collector ONLY to audit_logs (user_id=>Auth::id()), never on the order. ReceiptDataService.php:70 operator_name=optional(order->user)->name; FrontendOrder.php:113 has only user()->User (machine), no creator()/editor(). KEY clarification the auditor missed but which STRENGTHENS the fix: no create_frontend_orders_table migration exists; FrontendOrder+Order share the 'orders' table which HAS creator_id (create_orders_table:42) AND editor_id (:44) nullable, so the recommended fix (set $locked->editor_id in confirmCounterPayment + resolve operator from editor_id/creator_id) is trivially applicable and the receipt-read row already has the column. Documented NF525 operator gap (memory: partial heal 6b26e1be3 POS-only) root-caused on the LIVE Plan B path. Keep P1 (CLAUDE.md §8 receipt must identify operator). CAVEAT: root cause+fix live in frozen fiscal backend (fiscal-adjacent), NOT the kiosk Vue surface — finding correctly flags gate-required. Scope-tangential but genuine.

- **[P3] Kiosk login/diagnostic screen uses a dark navy gradient, breaking the V1 kiosk light-mode mandate** — `resources/js/components/frontend/kiosk/KioskLoginComponent.vue:148-155,279-294` (visual, code)
  - **What** : KioskLoginComponent .kiosk-login-screen uses background: linear-gradient(160deg, #0f0f1a 0%, #1a1a2e 100%) (line 150) with white-on-dark text, and the retry button hover uses #c0001a (line 294) — neither matches the Cayenne light-mode palette (primary #F4501E on white) used by every other kiosk surface (idle bg defaults to #FFFFFF, KioskIdleScreenComponent.vue:337). CLAUDE.md §3bis: 'Light mode 100% on kiosk (owner mandate dark mode désactivé)'.
  - **Why it matters** : This screen is the boot/auto-login diagnostic the borne shows when the machine token is missing or rate-limited. It is rare but customer-visible on a failed boot, and it visibly clashes with the brand (dark navy vs the warm light Cayenne look), plus #c0001a is a non-palette red. Low impact because it is a transient diagnostic, but it is a real brand/light-mode inconsistency.
  - **Reproduce** : Open /kiosk/login (or trigger it by clearing the kiosk token): the screen renders dark navy with white text, unlike every other kiosk screen.
  - **Correction** : Restyle the login/diagnostic screen to the kiosk light tokens (white surface, var(--kiosk-primary) #F4501E accents, dark text) to match idle/cart/payment.
  - **RED verdict** : VERIFIED, P3 unchanged. KioskLoginComponent.vue:150 background linear-gradient(160deg,#0f0f1a,#1a1a2e) dark navy white-on-dark; :294 hover #c0001a non-palette red. KioskIdleScreenComponent.vue:337 uses light var(--kiosk-idle-bg,#FFFFFF). CLAUDE.md §3bis mandates light-mode 100% on kiosk. Real brand/light-mode inconsistency but a transient boot/diagnostic screen (rare, low impact). is_necessary:true legit cosmetic fix at P3.

**Dropped by adversarial pass:**
- ~~[P3] Whole card/TR/TPE pipeline in KioskPaymentComponent is dead code under the V1 Plan B envelope (route-all-to-counter default true)~~ — EXISTS & accurate but WORKS-AS-INTENDED. config/kiosk.php:54 default true; KioskPaymentComponent.vue:333 reads injected flag (master.blade.php:154); when true only counter-route renders, confirmCounterRoute (:421) forces cash; card/TR/_invokeTpe/reconcile branch is dormant — all confirmed. BUT CLAUDE.md §3bis documents the card/TR path as the deliberately-retained Plan A future-wire path, and the finding itself concedes 'Not a bug today' and recommends annotation-only ('Do not delete'). Dormant-by-design + annotation = clarity nit, not a P2 deadcode defect. is_necessary:false; P2->P3.
- ~~[P3] Upsell add-to-cart jumps straight to payment and silently invalidates the priced quote without re-quoting~~ — MECHANISM EXISTS, HARM OVERSTATED; P2->P3, is_necessary:false. KioskUpsellComponent.vue:220/254 push to payment without re-quote; kioskCart.js:257 ADD_ITEM sets orderQuote=null; cart proceedToUpsell (KioskCartComponent.vue:685) awaits quoteOrder, upsell does not — confirmed. BUT the wrong-charge framing is DISPROVEN: confirmPayment line 443 does 'await refreshQuote()' (POST /frontend/order/quote :576) BEFORE submitOrder, so the CHARGE is always a fresh server quote — no mischarge. Only a transient DISPLAY gap: cartTotal() (:328)=_lastQuote?.total_ttc ?? this.total, _lastQuote (data:309) null on entry until pay-tap, so the card briefly shows component-local this.total (NOT Vuex getters.total as finding states — minor misattribution). On live Plan B the customer pays at a human counter showing the real total, muting even display-trust harm. Cosmetic hardening; P3.
- ~~[P3] Upsell-added items can never be reviewed or removed before paying — straight to payment, no return to cart~~ — REAL but harm blunted; P2->P3, is_necessary:false (non-blocking). KioskUpsellComponent.vue:254 (addAndContinue) & :267 (skip) push to payment; counter-route back button (KioskPaymentComponent.vue:54-66) is inside v-if=!paymentRouteAllToCounter, so counter-route screen (5-49) has NO back control — a mistaken upsell can't be de-selected in-flow. BUT on the live Plan B borne the customer immediately faces a human cashier who can remove the item before encaissement, and the inactivity reset clears it. Genuine UX flag, but human-in-loop mitigation makes it P3 not a P2 revenue-trust issue.
- ~~[P3] TPE is charged the client quote total instead of the server order total (dormant TPE path)~~ — EXISTS & accurate, DORMANT, keep P3 is_necessary:false for V1. KioskPaymentComponent.vue:466 'total = isFinite(quote.total_ttc) ? quote.total_ttc : n' — client quote wins over server total n; :601 amountEuros=this._lastOrder.total charges TPE that figure. SSOT-backwards as claimed. BUT (a) dormant under Plan B (processCardPayment never runs per S16-02), (b) both derive from the same signed quote so they match, (c) F-002 backend echo-verify catches >1c divergence. Pre-Plan-A cleanup, not a V1 defect. P3.
- ~~[P3] Plan B counter-route payment screen shows a perpetual spinner if order submit hangs, with no timeout or cancel~~ — PREMISE DISPROVEN — DROP. Core claim 'perpetual spinner forever, no timeout' is FALSE: bootstrap.js:23 sets window.axios.defaults.timeout=30000, so a hung POST /frontend/order REJECTS after 30s into confirmPayment's catch which clears submitting and surfaces error — NOT perpetual. The finding self-defeats by admitting 'no escape except the inactivity reset' — that reset exists (KioskAppComponent.vue:898 idleTimer fires regardless of screen), so recovery is guaranteed. Cited lines (30-48 spinner, 421, 770) exist (file_line_exists:true) but the 'stuck forever' defect does not. At most a P3 nit about a 30s wait lacking a progress hint — but as written (perpetual, no escape) is_real:false, DROP.

**Coverage gaps (RED):**
- applyPromo / applyLoyalty (KioskCart/KioskLoyalty @click) — discount+loyalty application is NF525/pricing-SSOT-adjacent, examined by NONE of the 7 findings; highest-value unaudited kiosk flow (coupon/loyalty VAT-10 has prior P0 history per memory).
- Wizard step availability handlers — increment/decrement, incrementSupplement/decrementSupplement, viande increment (steps/*.vue) gate add-to-cart on stock; no finding checked runtime stock-rupture mid-wizard before add.
- printReceipt on confirmation screen — unaudited; directly relevant since the S16-01 operator-attribution gap surfaces on the printed receipt.
- callStaff (frontend/kiosk-event), openOfflineConflictModal, openMyAccount — unaudited side endpoints; offline-conflict path touches the dormant reconcile queue (S16-02) but was not reasoned about for the LIVE path.
- abandonOrder / onAbandonConfirm reset path — the recovery mechanism S16-04 and S16-07 lean on for mitigation was itself never verified to fully clear cart + revoke partial state.

### S17-dinein-tables — S17-dinein-tables
Files examined: `resources/js/components/admin/diningTable/DiningTableListComponent.vue`, `resources/js/components/admin/diningTable/DiningTableShowComponent.vue`, `resources/js/components/admin/tableOrders/TableOrderListComponent.vue`, `resources/js/components/layouts/backend/BackendMenuComponent.vue`, `resources/js/config/v1-hidden-modules.js`, `resources/js/router/modules/diningTableRoutes.js`, `resources/js/router/modules/adminTableOrderRoutes.js`, `resources/js/router/modules/tableOrderRoutes.js`, `routes/api.php`, `routes/web.php`, `app/Http/Controllers/Table/OrderController.php`, `app/Http/Controllers/Table/DiningTableController.php`, `app/Http/Controllers/Admin/TableOrderController.php`, `app/Http/Requests/TableOrderRequest.php`, `app/Http/Requests/OrderRequest.php`, `app/Http/Requests/PosOrderRequest.php`, `app/Services/OrderService.php`, `app/Services/DiningTableService.php`, `app/Models/FrontendDiningTable.php`, `app/Models/DiningTable.php`, `app/Http/Middleware/ApiKeyMiddleware.php`, `app/Enums/OrderType.php`, `tests/Feature/Branch/BranchScopeCoverageSentinelTest.php`

**Confirmed corrections:**

- **[P1] Public QR table-order endpoint has NO pos_dine_in_enabled gate — dine-in is supposed to be OFF in V1 but anyone can still create a DINING_TABLE order** — `app/Http/Requests/TableOrderRequest.php:54-72 (withValidator) vs app/Http/Requests/PosOrderRequest.php:163-170 and app/Http/Requests/OrderRequest.php:218-230` (logic, code)
  - **What** : TableOrderRequest::withValidator (L54-72) only rejects DELIVERY/TAKEAWAY when those order types are disabled and rejects a blank order_type; it NEVER checks Settings::group('pos')->get('pos_dine_in_enabled'). The POS path DOES: PosOrderRequest L164 `if ($orderTypeInt === OrderType::DINING_TABLE && ! pos_dine_in_enabled) errors->add(...)`. The kiosk path DOES: OrderRequest L218-222 rejects KIOSK+DINING_TABLE when pos_dine_in_enabled is off. The public route routes/api.php:1521 `POST dining-order/` -> Table\OrderController::store -> OrderService::tableOrderStore (OrderService.php:1336) creates a FrontendOrder with the submitted order_type (DINING_TABLE=20 per app/Enums/OrderType.php:10) regardless of the flag. routes/web.php:62 serves the SPA for /menu/:slug so the QR flow is reachable by any browser.
  - **Why it matters** : V1 ships in 'a emporter only' mode (pos_dine_in_enabled=false, memory feedback_v1_dine_in_disabled). Two of the three order-entry surfaces enforce that server-side; the public QR surface is the only hole. A scanned/guessed table slug + the publicly-exposed x-api-key (MIX_API_KEY is baked into the browser bundle, config/app.php:63, ApiKeyMiddleware.php:24) lets a customer create a real DINING_TABLE FrontendOrder that lands in KDS and the admin table-orders list — a workflow the owner has explicitly disabled. It is an inconsistent invariant: the same 'dine-in off' rule is true on POS+kiosk but silently false on QR. In single-resto V1 this is low-frequency (no printed QR codes deployed), hence P1 not P0, but it is a genuine server-authoritative gap.
  - **Reproduce** : POST /api/table/dining-order/ with header x-api-key=<MIX_API_KEY from window runtime>, body {dining_table_id, customer_id, branch_id:1, order_type:20, items:[...valid item...], subtotal, total, is_advance_order:0, source}. Order is created (200/Resource) even with pos_dine_in_enabled=0. Compare: same order_type=20 on POST /api/pos-order returns 'Dine-in is disabled for this branch.'
  - **Correction** : In TableOrderRequest::withValidator, mirror PosOrderRequest L163-170: if (int)request('order_type')===OrderType::DINING_TABLE && ! (bool) Settings::group('pos')->get('pos_dine_in_enabled', false) -> $validator->errors()->add('order_type', 'Le service a table est desactive.'); This is a non-frozen FormRequest, scope-minimal, symmetric with the two existing gates.
  - **RED verdict** : VERIFIED. TableOrderRequest::withValidator (app/Http/Requests/TableOrderRequest.php:54-72) gates only DELIVERY/TAKEAWAY/blank order_type and never reads pos_dine_in_enabled (grep dine_in|DINING_TABLE|pos_dine in that file = 0 hits). POS path DOES gate it (PosOrderRequest.php:163-170, 'F-A5: Server-side dine-in feature gate', msg 'Dine-in is disabled for this branch.'); kiosk path DOES (OrderRequest.php:218-231). Public route confirmed: routes/api.php:1521 POST table/dining-order/ (apiKey only, unauthenticated, throttle:20,1) -> Table\OrderController::store (OrderController.php:24) -> OrderService::tableOrderStore (OrderService.php:1336) which creates a FrontendOrder from validated() with no dine-in check. DINING_TABLE=20 confirmed app/Enums/OrderType.php:10. PREMISE VERIFIED LIVE: settings table (smartisan/laravel-settings, cols group/key/payload) has ZERO rows in group 'pos' (distinct groups list excludes 'pos'), so Settings::group('pos')->get('pos_dine_in_enabled', false) returns default false everywhere -> dine-in genuinely OFF. QR is the only ungated order-entry surface. P1 confirmed.

- **[P2] Admin dining-tables & table-orders pages are hidden from the sidebar but fully reachable by direct URL with no dine-in feature check** — `resources/js/config/v1-hidden-modules.js:20-22, resources/js/router/modules/diningTableRoutes.js:24-35, resources/js/router/modules/adminTableOrderRoutes.js:24-39` (logic, code)
  - **What** : v1-hidden-modules.js lists 'tableOrders' (L20) and 'diningTables' (L22); BackendMenuComponent.vue:59-69 maps them to URLs 'table-orders'/'dining-tables' and hiddenMenuUrls (L165-171) filters them out of the sidebar. BUT the Vue routes still register: diningTableRoutes.js:24 name 'admin.diningTable.list' and adminTableOrderRoutes.js:24 name 'admin.table.order.list'. Neither route nor DiningTableListComponent.vue / TableOrderListComponent.vue reads pos_dine_in_enabled — they have no v-if/guard on the feature flag (verified: grep dine_in on both returns nothing). The comment in v1-hidden-modules.js:7 explicitly says 'Restent accessibles par URL directe.'
  - **Why it matters** : An admin who bookmarks /admin/dining-tables or /admin/table-orders (or lands there via an old link) gets a fully-working CRUD: they can create dining tables (generating QR codes that point at the disabled /menu flow) and browse table orders — operating a feature the owner turned off. It is a 'blur' (flou): the feature looks decommissioned (no menu) but is actually live. Low blast radius (admin-only, branch isolation intact) so P2, but it contradicts the 'dine-in disabled' product decision and can confuse a real shift.
  - **Reproduce** : Log in as admin, navigate the URL bar to /admin/dining-tables -> the list + 'Create' drawer render and work; /admin/table-orders -> list renders. No 'feature disabled' guard anywhere.
  - **Correction** : Add a router beforeEnter guard (or a top-level v-if on a 'dine-in disabled' notice) on diningTableRoutes + adminTableOrderRoutes that redirects to dashboard when the frontend settings store pos_dine_in_enabled is falsy — reuse the exact dineInEnabledFrom() helper already unit-tested in tests/js/posDineInFlag.spec.js. Keeps code/routes intact (V2 re-enable) while making the V1 'off' state honest.
  - **RED verdict** : VERIFIED. v1-hidden-modules.js lists 'tableOrders'(L20)+'diningTables'(L22), comment L7 'Restent accessibles par URL directe.' diningTableRoutes.js + adminTableOrderRoutes.js register routes with NO beforeEnter and NO dine_in guard (grep dine_in|beforeEnter|pos_dine both files = exit 1). Routes carry only permissionUrl (Spatie permission, NOT feature-flag), so any admin reaches working CRUD. PRECISION FLAW in recommended fix (kept for implementer): dineInEnabledFrom() is NOT a shared helper — grep -rn dineInEnabledFrom resources/js = 0 hits; it exists ONLY as a local function inside tests/js/posDineInFlag.spec.js:9. Fix must extract/define it, not 'reuse'. Defect stands. P2: admin-only, isolation intact, contradicts documented 'dine-in disabled' decision.

**Dropped by adversarial pass:**
- ~~[P3] Two models (DiningTable scoped, FrontendDiningTable unscoped) share the same dining_tables table — the public read path returns ALL branches' tables; intentional-but-fragile for V2~~ — CODE VERIFIED but ALREADY-HANDLED. DiningTable.php:27-30 adds BranchScope; FrontendDiningTable.php:9-22 has no boot/scope, $table='dining_tables'. show() binds unscoped FrontendDiningTable (Table\DiningTableController.php:31); binding {frontendDiningTable:slug} (api.php:1515) resolves cross-branch; list uses scoped DiningTable (DiningTableService::list:55). Exemption EXPLICITLY documented BranchScopeCoverageSentinelTest.php:56 ('FrontendDiningTable' => 'BASELINE_V1_2026-05-18 — V1.0.2 heal C-P0-D'). Harmless single-branch V1. Finding's OWN recommended_correction says 'No V1 code change needed.' = documented exemption, not a V1 defect to fix. P2->P3, is_necessary:false for V1; only nit is annotating the sentinel with a hard V2 precondition.
- ~~[P3] DiningTable occupancy columns (occupancy_status / occupied_order_id / occupied_at) are written by no V1 flow and never displayed — dormant schema cost~~ — FALSE HEADLINE — auditor misread. Claim 'occupancy columns written by NO V1 flow' is DISPROVEN: DiningTableService WRITES them in occupy()(L218-220), release()(L282-284), transfer()(L378-386); floorplanState() READS them (L184). LIVE-WIRED: FloorplanController.php:36 ->occupy, :50 ->release, exposed routes/api.php:918-922 (floorplan state/transfer/assign/release, middleware permission:pos); OrderService.php:1295 calls tryReleaseTableAfterPosOrderPaid on POS order paid (DiningTableService.php:307). isFree()/isOccupied() (DiningTable.php:46-54) used by that live logic. The auditor's narrow true sub-claim (admin List/Show CRUD components omit an occupancy column) is cosmetic and misframed as 'never written.' DROP: core assertion factually wrong; residual is a non-issue, not deadcode.
- ~~[P3] Table-orders list filter is hard-locked to order_type=DINING_TABLE while the order-type dropdown maps 4 types — and the page itself is for a disabled feature (empty list with no explanation)~~ — PARTLY REAL, PARTLY MISREAD; P2->P3. TRUE: search.order_type pinned to DINING_TABLE at init (TableOrderListComponent.vue:275) and in clear() (L349); with dine-in off list is permanently empty showing generic not-found.png + message.no_data_available (L136-145), no 'feature disabled' hint. MISREAD: orderTypeEnumArray (L251-256) is NOT a filter dropdown — it is a column-LABEL map used at L107-108 to render each row's badge; no v-model binds search.order_type to a <select> in the template. So 'dropdown offers Delivery/Takeaway/Kiosk' is unsubstantiated. Residual = generic empty state on a hidden admin page = minor UX, subsumed by S17-02's route guard. is_necessary:false standalone; fix as rider on S17-02. P3.

**Coverage gaps (RED):**
- HEADLINE GAP — POS floorplan dine-in feature ENTIRELY unaudited and itself an ungated dine-in surface. routes/api.php:918-922 expose POST floorplan/transfer, POST floorplan/{tableId}/assign, POST floorplan/{tableId}/release, GET floorplan/state, gated ONLY by middleware permission:pos (FloorplanController.php:16) with NO pos_dine_in_enabled check (grep dine_in in FloorplanController = 0). These mutate occupancy_status/occupied_order_id/occupied_at and sync orders.dining_table_id (DiningTableService::occupy). With dine-in OFF this is a larger server-authoritative dine-in gap than S17-01's QR path and is why S17-04 is false. S17 mandate is 'verify dine-in is gated'; auditor checked 3 order-entry surfaces and missed this whole table-management surface. Warrants its own P1/P2 finding.
- OrderService.php:1295 tryReleaseTableAfterPosOrderPaid runs on every POS order paid regardless of dine-in flag — unaudited side-effect touching dining_tables occupancy on the normal POS cash flow.
- FloorplanController assign/release/transfer write across DiningTable + Order via resolveOperatorContext (L69); multi-tenant correctness of orders.dining_table_id writes for the V2 two-branch case (the very risk S17-03 flags) was not examined.
- Unauthenticated public reads GET table/dining-table/ index and table/item-category/ (api.php ~1508-1513) not exercised for cross-branch scope in V2; S17-03 covers model scope but not these index/category reads.
- TableOrderListComponent search carries exceptSource: SourceEnum.POS (L276) which silently filters POS-sourced dining orders out of the admin table-orders list — unaudited interaction with the floorplan flow; could hide legitimately-created dine-in orders from this page.

## Live visual layer (main-agent, Playwright captures)
Captures: `reports/audit/systems-massive-2026-06-04/captures`.

- **[P3] OSS (customer-facing 'Suivi client') shows admin header chrome until fullscreen** — `resources/js/components/admin/orderStatusScreen/* (page header) ; route admin.order-status-screen` (ux, live-verified)
  - The customer-facing order-status screen renders the admin top bar (branch selector, 'Tableau De Bord' link, and 'Bonjour Admin Le Cayenne' with the operator's name) by default; a green fullscreen toggle exists but is opt-in.
  - **Why** : A screen meant for customers in the dining area exposes the operator's name + admin navigation until someone manually presses fullscreen. Minor privacy/professionalism issue on a public display. · **See** : capture 15-suivi-client.png

- **[P3] KDS order timer shows raw minutes for old orders (e.g. '926:5x') with no hour formatting** — `resources/js/components/admin/kitchenDisplaySystem/* (timer render)` (visual, live-verified)
  - On the KDS, stale orders show timers like '926:5' (=15h+); the format is MM:SS and does not roll over to hours, so very old tickets read as a large minute count.
  - **Why** : Only visible under stale/test data, but the timer format does not gracefully handle >60min; a chef glancing at '926:5' gets no useful elapsed signal. · **See** : capture 14-ecran-cuisine.png

- **[P3] KDS caps the visible list (49 shown, '+41 en attente / Liste pleine') and asks the chef to filter** — `resources/js/components/admin/kitchenDisplaySystem/* + KDS list endpoint (server cap)` (ux, live-verified)
  - KDS banner: 'Liste pleine (49 affichee(s), max. serveur) : d'autres commandes actives existent peut-etre - filtrez par statut ou cherchez un n de ticket.' with a red '+41 en attente' badge.
  - **Why** : Honest degradation, but under a genuine rush a hidden active order beyond the cap is a missed-order risk; the cap + filter is the only mitigation. (Mostly an artefact of the test-data backlog here.) · **See** : capture 14-ecran-cuisine.png

- **[P3] KDS shows 49 active orders vs OSS shows 1 'En preparation' — verify the filter difference is intentional (live vs stale backlog), not a sync gap** — `KDS vs OSS list queries (kitchenDisplaySystem vs orderStatusScreen controllers)` (logic, needs-live-verify)
  - Same instant: KDS displays 49 active cards (+41 queued) while OSS 'En preparation' shows only N A0053 and 'Pret' is empty.
  - **Why** : If OSS and KDS draw from different status/time filters this is expected (OSS=today's live queue, KDS=all active incl. 15h-old test orders); if it is a sync/filter bug, customers would not see orders that are actually cooking. Needs confirmation. · **See** : captures 14 vs 15

## What is solid (good logic / technique / UX)
- **Net realized-revenue scope is centralized and Z-aligned** (`app/Models/Order.php:267-295 (scopeRealizedRevenue + isRealizedRevenueRow)`) — Revenue netting (drop cancelled-but-paid, include negated refund mirrors so a refunded sale nets to ~0) is defined ONCE as an Eloquent scope plus a collection-side twin kept in lock-step, reused by totalSales/salesSummary/realtimeReport/eodSynthesis. Right SSOT discipline -- dashboard CA agrees with ZReport netting by construction, not copy-paste.
- **EOD PDF endpoint is correctly permission-segregated from the dashboard** (`app/Http/Controllers/Admin/DashboardController.php:38-60 + 212-244`) — Fiscal EOD synthesis is gated by a SEPARATE middleware line (permission:pos-manage-fiscal) deliberately not merged into permission:dashboard, with a clear comment that a :dashboard-only user must not pull a fiscal synthesis. Input date is regex-validated (Y-m-d) before Carbon, handler documented read-only (no fiscal sequence / no audit-log write). Frontend button v-if gated to match (DashboardComponent.vue:118-125). Defense-in-depth done right.
- **Branch scoping for the dashboard is explicit and role-aware** (`app/Services/DashboardService.php:43-54 (dashboardBranchId) + 19-41`) — Admin/Tenant Admin -> null (cross-branch), branch staff -> pinned branch_id, applied consistently through orderQuery()/customerQuery(). auditTrail() additionally hand-filters AuditLog (an EXEMPTED_MODELS fiscal model) with the same admin-vs-branch rule (DashboardService.php:809-815) rather than leaking cross-branch fiscal rows -- correct given AuditLog has no global BranchScope.
- **Per-widget ErrorBoundary isolation** (`resources/js/components/admin/dashboard/DashboardComponent.vue:48-64`) — Every widget is wrapped in its own ErrorBoundary, so a render-time crash in one card does not blank the whole dashboard. Good resilience pattern; complements (not replaces) the missing per-widget API-error states flagged in S1-DASH-05.
- **Kiosk-vs-Web channel bucketing root-caused and fixed via source_surface** (`app/Services/DashboardService.php:493-536`) — The long-standing kiosk-orders-mis-bucket-as-Web issue was correctly root-caused (kiosk runs the web shell so source=WEB) and fixed by promoting source_surface='kiosk' as the canonical marker, legacy source int kept for back-compat -- a surgical migration-free fix. Remaining gap is only the POS branch lacking the same exclusion guard (see S1-DASH-04).
