- **Order soft-delete (POS) is correctly fenced for NF525 + branch + paid-order escalation** (`app/Services/OrderService.php:2623-2664`) — destroy() runs guards OUTSIDE the try/catch so abort() codes survive: branch isolation (403 unless global Admin or own branch), PAID orders blocked unless 'pos-destroy-paid' permission, and a closed-Z seal check (409 'NF525 immutability') when fiscal_sequence_no is set and the order falls in a closed Z window. Soft-delete + dual audit (ActionLog + tamper-evident AuditLogService) preserve the canonical record. This is exactly how a fiscal delete should be gated.
- **Status / payment changes are race-safe and re-validated under lock** (`app/Services/OrderService.php:2117-2253 (changeStatus) + :2392-2466 (changePaymentStatus)`) — Both mutate inside DB::transaction with Order::lockForUpdate()->firstOrFail(), idempotent early-return if another request already reached the target, and RE-validate the transition against the FRESH locked status (not the stale route-bound model) before saving. Prevents the multi-cashier double-deliver / double-PAID class of bugs. Domain events use DispatchableAfterCommit so a rollback drops side-effects. Solid concurrency engineering.
- **RETURNED removed from the status selector to force NF525 counter-entry refund path** (`resources/js/components/admin/posOrders/PosOrderShowComponent.vue:535-563 + onlineOrders/OnlineOrderShowComponent.vue:370-398`) — Both detail dropdowns deliberately EXCLUDE RETURNED so staff cannot cosmetically 'refund' an order by flipping status without emitting the NF525 mirror counter-entry. Refunds are routed through the dedicated PosRefundModal -> /refund-with-counter-entry (single endpoint that server-side picks pre-Z status=RETURNED+cashBack vs post-Z immutable mirror via SealedOrderGuard). RETURNED is kept only in the display map so historical refunds still render 'Retourné'. Correct fiscal discipline.
- **Cross-branch order access is unified to 403 with no existence enumeration** (`app/Http/Controllers/Admin/PosOrderController.php:261-285 + OrderHistoryController.php:64-99`) — show() loads withoutGlobalScope(BranchScope) then catches ModelNotFoundException and a foreign branch_id under a SINGLE 403 'Cross-branch access denied'. A non-existent id and a foreign-branch id return the same response shape, closing the timing/existence-enumeration leak. Backed by OrderShowBranchGuardSentinelTest. Good multi-tenant hygiene.
- **Order-line pricing/composition is genuinely backend-SSOT and never trusts client choices blindly** (`app/Services/Pricing/PricingService.php:602-660`) — At checkout the composer is re-projected server-side (project(), 607), every selection is re-validated against the PUBLISHED active profile: source_type whitelist (609), min/max per step (617-630), allow_repeat dedupe (632-642), and per-choice live availability with a hard 422 if a choice is unavailable (645-655). assertComposerSelectionsBelongToPublishedProfile blocks selections not in the published projection. The catalog editor sets base prices (correct — catalog is the price source), but the ORDER path computes everything from the snapshot, satisfying the NF525 SSOT mandate.
- **Runtime menu correctly serves only PUBLISHED profiles with branch-scope resolution** (`app/Services/Kiosk/KioskMenuService.php:433-447 / app/Services/Menu/MenuProjectionService.php:294-307`) — Both menu builders filter is_published=true, eager-load only active steps ordered by position, and resolve branch_id_scope (branch override beats global null) via a deterministic compareComposerProfiles tie-break. Draft/unpublished composer work never leaks to customers — the publish gate is real.
- **Item deletion guards order history (no NF525 data-loss via force delete)** (`app/Services/ItemService.php:428-490 + app/Http/Controllers/Admin/ItemController.php:166-184`) — Force-delete is refused with a 409 (translated message + error code) when the item is referenced by OrderItem history (config-gated), FK relax is restricted to the testing environment only, soft-delete cascades children, and ItemDeleted is dispatched afterCommit. This protects historical fiscal lines from being orphaned.
- **ItemRequest unique-name ignore-on-update works correctly (verified empirically)** (`app/Http/Requests/ItemRequest.php:47`) — Rule::unique('items','name')->ignore($this->route('item.id')) initially looked broken (no literal 'item.id' route param), but Laravel's Route::parameter dotted-resolution drills the bound Item model's id — tinker confirmed route('item.id')===42, and test_admin_can_update_item_price (same-name reprice) passes. No false-positive 422 on save. Flagging as a SOLID so a future reader does not 'fix' a working rule.
- **Per-order idempotency guard on daily-quota decrement is well-reasoned and race-safe** (`app/Services/Menu/AvailabilityService.php:291-325`) — Cache::add() SETNX-first-writer-wins guard keyed per (orderKind,branch,order) prevents OrderCreated re-fires from double-counting toward the quota. The inline comment correctly bounds the blast radius and clarifies daily_consumed_qty is a UX counter not a fiscal one — exactly the kind of honest invariant documentation that prevents future NF525 confusion.
- **All availability mutations dispatch events via DB::afterCommit (commit-before-broadcast invariant honored)** (`app/Services/Menu/AvailabilityService.php:383-393 + 607-630 + 760-771`) — Every event (item/extra/variation availability changed, and the release flip) is wrapped in DB::afterCommit, so the outbox/broadcast pipeline never observes uncommitted state. Combined with lockForUpdate on every toggle, concurrent rupture toggles serialize cleanly with order commit (assertItemsOrderableForBranch L253-259 also row-locks). Solid concurrency discipline.
- **Append-only StockMovement model is correctly enforced at the model layer** (`app/Models/StockMovement.php:9 + 48-56`) — UPDATED_AT=null plus updating()/deleting() hooks throwing LogicException make stock movements tamper-evident in code (independent of DB triggers), and the BranchScope global scope (L20) keeps movement listings tenant-isolated with the documented admin bypass. Good audit hygiene for the stock ledger.
- **Dashboard read endpoints are genuinely N+1-free with documented bulk-query discipline** (`app/Http/Controllers/Admin/StockRuptureDashboardController.php:135-182 + 294-451`) — lowAlerts and catalogOverview bucket polymorphic stockables by type and run one whereIn per type (the heal comment shows a real 1200->3 query reduction), and catalogOverview keeps the full-catalogue read to ~5 queries via keyed override maps. The branch scoping with explicit withoutGlobalScope + where('branch_id') is correct and intentional.
- **Realized-revenue SSOT kept in strict lock-step between SQL scope and collection helper** (`app/Models/Order.php:267-295 (scopeRealizedRevenue + isRealizedRevenueRow)`) — scopeRealizedRevenue (query path: items report withSum, item export) and isRealizedRevenueRow (collection path: salesReportOverview, PDF/Excel) implement the SAME paid-and-not-terminal OR refund-mirror predicate, with an explicit comment mandating they stay in lock-step. This is the right way to keep the sales report, items report and the Z agreeing on what counts as realized revenue — exactly the 'agree with the Z' owner requirement, with one definition not three.
- **Cash session report enforces branch isolation correctly via admin-only scope bypass** (`app/Http/Controllers/Admin/CashSessionReportController.php:77-80 + 114-121`) — Only branch_id=0 (global admin) gets ->withoutGlobalScope(BranchScope::class); branch staff fall through to the global BranchScope so they see only their branch. The optional branch_id filter is honoured ONLY for admin and silently ignored for staff (deliberately, to avoid leaking branch existence). Permission is checked via $user->can('cash-sessions-report') with explicit 401/403. Clean, leak-free multi-tenant read.
- **GDPR data-minimization on the sales report payload (phone shipped only for delivery)** (`app/Http/Resources/SimpleOrderResource.php:97-102`) — customer_phone is emitted only when order_type === DELIVERY; for POS/kiosk/takeaway rows it is null. The sales report and online-orders surfaces never receive customer phone over the wire even though the UI already gated rendering — defense in depth at the resource layer. Good privacy hygiene for a report screen.
- **Items export forces a true full-dataset total independent of screen pagination** (`app/Exports/ItemsReportExport.php:23-49`) — collection() explicitly $this->request->merge(['paginate'=>0]) before calling itemReport() and accumulates $total over the full set, so the Excel 'Total' row is a real grand total. This is the correct pattern (and is precisely the evidence that the on-screen tfoot total in S5-01 is the buggy one). The date-aware itemReport() (units_sold = SUM quantity, realized-only, scoped to the parent order's order_datetime) is also semantically correct, not a line-count.
- **Tax rate snapshotted onto order_items at creation — mid-day rate edits never corrupt past orders or the Z-report** (`app/Services/OrderService.php:520 ; app/Services/Pricing/PricingService.php:285 ; app/Services/FrontendOrderService.php:436`) — tax_rate is copied into order_items.tax_rate when the order is built, and ZReportService aggregates from those frozen per-line rates (total_by_tax_rate), so changing a Tax row's rate later only affects future orders. Correct NF525 immutability of fiscal history.
- **Settings fan-out on Tax/Currency mutations -> outbox -> POS/kiosk reflect** (`app/Http/Controllers/Admin/TaxController.php:39,51,63 ; app/Http/Controllers/Admin/CurrencyController.php:38,49,61`) — store/update/destroy each dispatch SettingsUpdated(['tax']) / (['currency']) (Wave 5G R9 heal), wiring catalog-config changes into the real-time settings sync contract so dependent surfaces refresh.
- **Loyalty config read live with sane defaults across every consumer** (`app/Http/Controllers/Frontend/LoyaltyController.php:445-447 ; app/Services/Loyalty/PosRedemptionService.php:96 ; app/Services/Pricing/DiscountCalculator.php:45`) — loyalty_points_per_euro / _for_1_euro_discount / _min_redeem_points are all read via Settings::group('loyalty_setup')->get(key, default), so a save in LoyaltySetup reflects immediately in POS redemption + award + discount math with no stale-cache risk, and defaults (10/100/50) match the form defaults.
- **BranchController captures old status BEFORE service mutation to fire BranchStatusChanged correctly** (`app/Http/Controllers/Admin/BranchController.php:67-73 and 94-99`) — Both update() and destroy() read $oldStatus before BranchService mutates the model in place (tap()->update), then dispatch BranchStatusChanged only on a real transition. Well-reasoned (documented Wave 5G R10 heal) — prevents stale token revocation gaps and avoids double-firing when status is unchanged. Good security-aware lifecycle handling.
- **OrderSetupRequest enforces min:0 on every numeric field with a clear rationale** (`app/Http/Requests/OrderSetupRequest.php:28-37`) — All durations, activity codes and money/distance fields are guarded against negatives ([P10] comment). Prevents a negative delivery charge or prep time from corrupting downstream pricing/scheduling — a small but correct invariant most settings forms miss.
- **ReceiptDataService is correctly wired to print fiscal identity (the data, not the renderer, is the gap)** (`app/Services/Receipt/ReceiptDataService.php:66-69`) — The receipt builder already pulls register_id/siret/vat_intra/legal_footer from the order's branch and exposes them via OrderDetailsResource.php:118-121 with a defensive optional(). The fiscal-omission problem (S7-01) is purely the missing settings UI/seed upstream — the rendering layer is production-correct and ready the moment the columns are populated.
- **Kiosk dine-in disablement is enforced server-authoritatively, not just hidden in the UI** (`app/Http/Requests/OrderRequest.php:209-231`) — V1 'à emporter only' is enforced backend-side: a kiosk token submitting order_type=KIOSK/DINING_TABLE while pos_dine_in_enabled is off is rejected with an FR-locked message, defending against UI bypass / replay. Correct defense-in-depth that matches the V1 dine-in-disabled mandate rather than relying on frontend gating.
- **Garde anti-vol 'phantom CARD' à double couche (HTTP + service) sur terminal_id** (`app/Http/Requests/PosOrderRequest.php:206-247 + app/Services/Payments/SplitPaymentService.php:111-139`) — Chaque tranche CARTE doit porter un terminal_id ACTIF appartenant à la branche de la commande, vérifié à la fois en FormRequest (HTTP) et en service (callers non-HTTP), avec BranchScope explicitement bypassé puis branch_id re-vérifié à la main pour empêcher toute fuite cross-branch. Excellente défense en profondeur contre le vecteur 'tranche carte fantôme + empochage cash'. (C'est la rigueur de cette garde qui rend S8-01 bloquant — la garde est correcte, c'est l'écran de config qui produit le mauvais branch_id.)
- **Archivage soft des TPE pour préserver la traçabilité fiscale** (`app/Http/Controllers/Admin/PaymentTerminalController.php:92-99`) — destroy() ne fait jamais de hard delete : il passe status=ARCHIVED pour préserver order_payments.terminal_id des Z-reports clôturés. Cohérent NF525 (rétention/traçabilité) et bien commenté.
- **Mots de passe borne hashés (bcrypt/Hash::make), jamais renvoyés par l'API** (`app/Services/KioskMachineService.php:67 (bcrypt) + :96-98 (Hash::make on update if filled) + app/Http/Resources/KioskMachineResource.php:18-28 (no password key)`) — Le password de pairing borne est hashé à la création et seulement re-hashé en update si fourni (préserve l'ancien sinon), et la resource n'expose jamais le hash. Bonne hygiène d'auth — le seul reproche est le pré-remplissage trompeur côté front (S8-03).
- **Lookup de pairing borne pré-auth avec BranchScope explicitement bypassé + verrou lockForUpdate** (`app/Http/Controllers/Auth/KioskMachineLoginController.php:52-56 + 85-101`) — La résolution du username se fait withoutGlobalScope(BranchScope) avec un commentaire clair sur la récursion Sanctum évitée, et la création du token se fait sous lockForUpdate dans une transaction qui révoque d'abord les anciens kiosk-tokens (anti token-sprawl). Token limité à l'ability kiosk:order. Solide — le seul manque est la non-révocation côté admin-logout (S8-02).
- **PermissionService self-role privilege-escalation guard (already healed)** (`app/Services/PermissionService.php:48-66`) — update() forbids syncing permissions on a role the caller holds (`$caller->roles->contains('id', $role->id)` -> 403), blocking a settings-admin from granting themselves the full permission set. M-R3-P0-D heal. Strong invariant.
- **RoleRequest reserves 'Tenant Admin' name + permission:settings authorize (already healed)** (`app/Http/Requests/RoleRequest.php:21,42`) — authorize() returns user can('settings'); rules() Rule::notIn(['Tenant Admin']) prevents API-created shadow super-admin role that 9 files dual-gate on. Defense-in-depth on top of controller middleware (RoleController.php:21-22).
- **LanguageService::validateLangFilePath LFI/RCE containment (already healed)** (`app/Services/LanguageService.php:205-240`) — realpath() + allowed-base-dir prefix check + extension allowlist (.php/.json) defeats stream-wrapper (php://, data://, phar://) and ../ traversal before include()/file_put_contents. fileText/fileTextStore also gated permission:settings (LanguageController.php:27). Solid containment of the original RCE primitive.
- **RoleService protects system roles from deletion** (`app/Services/RoleService.php:90-101`) — destroy() refuses to delete the seeded role set (ADMIN/CUSTOMER/DELIVERY_BOY/WAITER/CHEF via $roleArray) — prevents bricking RBAC by removing core roles. PermissionController index/update both gated permission:settings (PermissionController.php:33) closing the earlier ungated enumeration.
- **Customer = branch_id=0 User, exempt from BranchScope intentionally (task hint satisfied)** (`app/Models/Scopes/BranchScope.php:21-23 ; CustomerService.php:71 (store branch_id=0)`) — BranchScope::apply early-returns for `$model instanceof User` to avoid Sanctum guard re-entry/recursion (documented at :19-20). Customers are User rows seeded with branch_id=0, so they are deliberately not branch-filtered. The sentinel's `App\Models\Customer` exemption (BranchScopeCoverageSentinelTest:51) is a separate model; the User exemption is the relevant one here. Intentional and documented — not a defect.
- **WAVE5-SEC-001 target-role guard correctly protects the three customer mutators** (`app/Services/CustomerService.php:180-188 (assertTargetRole), :98 update, :196 changePassword, :218 changeImage`) — assertTargetRole() throws HttpException(403) if the route-bound User is not a Customer, placed BEFORE the try/catch so the 403 survives QueryExceptionLibrary rewrap. Blocks a customers_edit holder from rotating an Admin's password via PUT /customer/{anyUserId}. Correct (the read-side gap in S10-01 is the remaining hole).
- **Admin self-service password change verifies the old password; admin reset path correctly omits it** (`app/Http/Requests/ChangePasswordRequest.php:44-58 ; app/Http/Requests/UserChangePasswordRequest.php:33-36`) — ProfileController self-change uses ChangePasswordRequest whose withValidator/checkOldPassword (Hash::check against auth user) enforces old_password — so ProfileService::changePassword not re-checking it is fine (enforced at the FormRequest layer). The admin-reset-a-customer path (UserChangePasswordRequest) intentionally omits old_password (admin doesn't know it) and requires min:12 letters+numbers + confirmed. Both correct.
- **Address IDOR protection: ownership asserted on update/destroy/show** (`app/Services/UserAddressService.php:79, :101, :118`) — update/destroy/show all guard `if ($user->id == $address->user_id)` else throw, so a mismatched {customer}/{address} pair in the route cannot mutate or read another user's address. Prevents the cross-record IDOR the nested route shape would otherwise allow.
- **route('X.id') dot-notation in FormRequests resolves correctly (NOT a bug — verified)** (`app/Http/Requests/CustomerRequest.php:34,37,44,48,54 ; CustomerAddressRequest.php:28`) — Verified via tinker: Route::parameter -> Arr::get supports dot notation and the bound User implements ArrayAccess, so Arr::get(['customer'=>$user],'customer.id') returns the user's id (printed int(1)), and null on create (store, no param). Thus Rule::unique()->ignore(id) excludes the current row on update and password becomes nullable on update / required on create — exactly as intended. Same pattern across all sibling *Request classes. Recorded as a SOLID to pre-empt a false-positive.
- **callerMayGrantRole strict-subset entitlement gate blocks privilege escalation and peer-cloning on the employee endpoint** (`app/Services/EmployeeService.php:82-99`) — USR-RBAC-01 heal: a non-settings caller (Branch Manager) may grant ONLY a role whose permission set is a STRICT SUBSET of their own (targetPerms.diff(callerPerms).isEmpty() AND targetPerms.count() < callerPerms.count()). Correctly prevents a manager from granting a permission they lack or cloning a same-level peer. settings-holders keep full discretion. This is the real defense for the staff-create surface and it is well-reasoned.
- **EnforcesOwnBranchScope forces non-settings callers to their own branch_id, ignoring request-supplied branch** (`app/Services/Concerns/EnforcesOwnBranchScope.php:19-26`) — USR-RBAC-02 heal shared across Employee/Chef/Waiter/DeliveryBoy services. effectiveBranchId returns the caller's own branch_id for non-settings users, ignoring the request value — a clean, single-responsibility guard against cross-branch staff assignment that a Branch Manager could otherwise attempt by posting an arbitrary branch_id.
- **syncRoles moved inside the DB transaction in EmployeeService::update (atomicity heal)** (`app/Services/EmployeeService.php:148-163`) — USR-RBAC-03 heal: the role sync is now atomic with the user update — a failed sync rolls back the user mutation too. The comment documents the pre-heal bug (mutated user with stale role) precisely; good transactional discipline.
- **WAVE5-SEC-001 assertTargetRole positive-role guard in Waiter/Chef services (the pattern whose absence is finding S11-01/S11-05)** (`app/Services/WaiterService.php:181-189`) — Waiter and Chef services assert the route-bound User actually holds the expected role BEFORE the try/catch (so it surfaces as 403, not a 422 rewrite), preventing cross-role mutation via route-model-binding. Citing as a SOLID because it is the correct template — its absence on AdministratorService::update and EmployeeService is exactly what makes those findings real.
- **POS receipt reprint writes a hash-chained NF525 audit event with first-print vs DUPLICATA distinction and best-effort non-blocking semantics** (`app/Http/Controllers/Admin/Pos/PosReceiptPrintController.php:35-110`) — Every print/reprint atomically increments receipt_print_count (branch-scoped, concurrency-safe), marks DUPLICATA at count>=2, and emits a distinct audit action (pos.receipt.print vs pos.receipt.reprint) attributed to the order's actual branch. Audit failure is surfaced via audit_emitted=false without blocking the cashier — correct NF525 operational-continuity tradeoff.
- **openSession triple-defense against concurrent double-open** (`app/Services/Delivery/DeliveryBoyCashSessionService.php:79-107`) — Cache::lock per (branch,driver) + DB::transaction with lockForUpdate on the existing-OPEN probe + UNIQUE partial index (migration 120200). Mirrors the POS cash-drawer NF525 pattern; LockTimeout degrades to a clean 409, not a crash. Correct defense-in-depth for the 'one open shift per driver' invariant.
- **COD PAID-flip deferred to DELIVERED so the escrow audit row fires exactly once** (`app/Services/OrderService.php:1838-1896`) — Careful NF525 reasoning: on the 2-step driver flow (PREPARED->OFD->DELIVERED) the prior code flipped payment_status to PAID at OFD, skipping the escrow capture on DELIVERED. The fix delays the COD PAID flip until newStatus===DELIVERED so the cash-collection audit row is emitted exactly once on both single-jump and 2-step paths, on the same locked transaction. Genuinely subtle and correct.
- **selectDeliveryBoy branch fence + role lookup by NAME + audit trail** (`app/Services/OrderService.php:2564-2603`) — Uses role('Delivery Boy','sanctum') by stable NAME (avoids the AUTO_INCREMENT role-id drift bug), enforces same-branch assignment (403 cross-branch), and writes an order.delivery_boy_assigned audit row capturing who assigned which driver to which order. Solid isolation + traceability (only missing the status gate noted in S12-05).
- **Stable per-mount idempotency key on the cash-session form** (`resources/js/components/admin/deliveryBoyCashSession/DeliveryBoyCashSessionFormComponent.vue:177`) — Builds one stable X-Idempotency-Key per form mount (mode+sessionId+timestamp) so an accidental double-click on open/close/reconcile dedupes server-side, while a fresh operation gets a fresh key. Correctly satisfies the idempotency middleware the routes carry.
- **Coupon discount is fully server-recalculated — client discount value never trusted** (`app/Services/OrderService.php:372-374,543-563,589-595 ; app/Services/CouponService.php:397-409`) — OrderService unset()s client total/subtotal/discount and recomputes coupon discount via resolveCouponById + calculateDiscountAmount; OrderCoupon stores the SERVER value. calculateDiscountAmount clamps with min($amount,$subtotal), max(0,...) and honors maximum_discount, so a coupon cannot over-discount below zero or beyond the cart. Good SSOT discipline.
- **Offers mutation paths correctly hard-disabled in V1 to prevent display-vs-charge mismatch** (`app/Http/Controllers/Admin/OfferController.php:31-43 ; config/features.php:27`) — store/update/changeImage are abort_unless(config('features.offers_enabled')===true,403) with a clear French message; default FEATURE_OFFERS_ENABLED=false. Because PricingService does not apply offer discounts, blocking creation avoids a 'shows X, charges Y' defect. Read paths stay open. Honest dormancy.
- **Coupon advanced scopes (status/days/hours/branch/surface) are wired into the HTTP validation path** (`app/Services/CouponService.php:350-365,461-472 ; app/Models/Coupon.php:90-158`) — couponChecking reads branch_id+surface and validateCouponForOrder calls $coupon->isUsableNow($branchId,$surface,$now) with a generic non-leaking failure message, plus explicit minimum_order, date-window, and limit_per_user checks. The CV6 P0 wiring is present and the date guards null-safe (couponDateWise guards null start/end against Carbon crash).
- **Coupon list ordering/search hardened against SQL injection and LIKE-wildcard abuse** (`app/Services/CouponService.php:63-64,115-130`) — sanitizeOrderColumn (allow-list), sanitizeOrderDirection (asc/desc only), and escapeLike (escapes \ % _) protect the dynamic orderBy/where-like in list(). Integer enum filters (status/discount_type) use exact (int) match. Solid input hygiene on the admin list.
- **Marketing mutation endpoints consistently permission-gated (defense-in-depth)** (`app/Http/Controllers/Admin/CouponController.php:25-29 ; app/Http/Controllers/Admin/PushNotificationController.php:22-25 ; app/Http/Controllers/Admin/MessageController.php:22-25`) — Coupon store/update/toggle/delete each gated by the matching coupons_* permission; CouponRequest also re-checks can('coupons_create')||can('coupons_edit'); MessageController's changeStatus was explicitly added to the permission:messages ->only() list (2026-06-01 heal) closing a gap. Authz coverage on state-mutating marketing routes is consistent.
- **Optimistic-lock + verrou DB sur le bump (changeStatus), 409 propre, append-only NF525** (`app/Services/KitchenDisplaySystemOrderService.php:392-444`) — changeStatus s'execute dans DB::transaction avec lockForUpdate, compare expected_status (envoye par le front via kdsStatusPayload, kds.js:3-9) au statut verrouille et abort 409 si divergence -> pas de double-transition en course concurrente. La transition est journalisee via OrderStateMachine::recordTransition (append-only), conforme NF525. Re-check isolation branche sous verrou (:399-401). Solide et bien pense.
- **Recall 'Annuler bump' 60s en action compensatoire append-only (orders.status jamais mute)** (`app/Services/KitchenDisplaySystemOrderService.php:285-381`) — Le recall NF525-safe: au lieu de muter orders.status (interdit par OrderStateMachine frozen), il APPEND une ligne order_status_transitions reason='kitchen_recall', avec garde d'etat (PREPARED only), garde TTL 60s, cap N=1 (409 si deja rappele), isolation branche sous verrou, et assertion d'invariant verifiant que status reste PREPARED apres commit (:354-357). Diffuse KdsOrderRecalled apres commit pour re-injecter la carte sur les autres postes. Tres bonne maitrise de la contrainte fiscale.
- **Filtre JS-side des OrderStatusChanged + debounce evitant les refresh inutiles** (`resources/js/components/admin/kitchenDisplaySystem/KitchenDisplaySystemComponent.vue:1576-1587 + :1925-1929`) — _statusChangeAffectsKds ne declenche un refresh que si l'ancien OU le nouveau statut est dans l'ensemble KDS-visible (ACCEPT/PREPARING/PREPARED), miroir de KitchenReleaseRule::visibleStatuses. Les transitions hors-board (DELIVERED->REFUNDED, CANCELED...) ne provoquent plus de refresh + flicker. Couple a _debouncedRefresh 300ms qui absorbe la rafale Echo+manuel+interceptor. Bonne hygiene reseau.
- **Puce d'alerte overflow (>8) comme filet de securite chef-visibility** (`resources/js/components/admin/kitchenDisplaySystem/KdsV2Grid.vue:82-90 + :242-249`) — Meme si les commandes 9+ ne sont pas actionnables (cf S14-KDS-04), la puce '+N en attente' pulse en rouge Cayenne (avec respect de prefers-reduced-motion) pour signaler au chef que la file depasse les 8 slots visibles. Compte uniquement les actives (ACCEPT|PREPARING), pas les PREPARED du bandeau servies. Mitigation honnete d'une limitation structurelle.
- **Split KDS par allergens_snapshot (securite alimentaire) lors du merge d'items identiques** (`app/Services/KitchenDisplaySystemOrderService.php:514-543`) — orderItems() merge les lignes identiques pour le board 'items' mais inclut un allergensHash dans la cle de groupement -> deux items meme produit/variations/instruction mais avec des snapshots d'allergenes differents restent 2 lignes KDS distinctes. Evite que le chef voie 'Burger x2' avec les allergenes du premier seulement et masque la declaration du second client. Detail de securite alimentaire bien pris.
- **PII-free public resource correctly split from total-bearing admin resource** (`app/Http/Resources/CDSOrderDetailsResource.php:18-25 vs PosShortcutOrderResource.php:36-44`) — The public wall (publicIndex -> CDSOrderDetailsResource) exposes only id/order_serial_no/token/queue_number/order_type/status - no name/phone/address/total. The authed POS widget gets a separate PosShortcutOrderResource that adds total. Clean, documented privacy boundary: the amount can never leak onto a lobby TV. Exactly the right resource-per-audience split.
- **Deterministic FIFO ordering stops the customer token from jumping positions between polls** (`app/Services/OrderStatusScreenOrderService.php:144 (and :264)`) — orderBy('queue_number','asc')->orderBy('id','asc') gives a stable, queue-faithful sort so the wall does not reshuffle on every 5-60s poll. The [Sprint 5C] comment shows this was a real bug that was fixed - good attention to the customer-perceived stability of the wall.
- **Robust Echo+poll dedup prevents double chime/flash on the same ready transition** (`resources/js/components/admin/orderStatusScreen/PreparingAndReadyComponent.vue:288-292 / 392-399`) — Echo's OrderStatusChanged pre-registers the order id in _echoMarkedReady before calling list(); _hydrateFromRows skips ids already in that set and clears it one-shot. This correctly avoids the race where both the push event and the subsequent poll would each fire _markNewReady for the same order. (Note: the steady-state dedup is solid; the gap is only the first-load case in finding 02.)
- **Public-wall poll cadence override is a correct, in-band fix for the no-push surface** (`resources/js/components/admin/orderStatusScreen/PreparingAndReadyComponent.vue:259-264 + OssSyncService _clampCadence`) — Because the unauth wall (branchId<=0) never joins the private branch.{id} channel (subscribeEcho early-returns) it gets zero push, yet the WS transport reports 'connected' so the default 60s connected cadence would blow the 8s POS->OSS budget. Overriding intervalMsWhenConnected to 5000 for the public wall only (TRAP-4) is the right targeted fix, and 5000ms is well within the clamp floor/ceiling, so it is safe even though ctx.options bypasses the clamp.
- **Accessibility + TV-wall resilience handled thoughtfully** (`resources/js/components/admin/orderStatusScreen/PreparingAndReadyComponent.vue:497-504 (prefers-reduced-motion) and 200-217 (wakeLock)`) — All four attention animations are disabled under prefers-reduced-motion, and a screen wakeLock is acquired on mount and re-acquired on visibilitychange with graceful degrade on unsupported browsers - both genuinely needed for an always-on customer TV and implemented defensively (try/catch everywhere, feature-flag ossWakeLockEnabled).
- **Composition parity with POS is enforced server-side via PricingService SSOT** (`resources/js/store/modules/kioskCart.js:98-140 (sanitizeKioskOrderItem) + app/Services/FrontendOrderService.php:277-296`) — The kiosk wizard builds a rich local composition but the payload is sanitized to {item_id, quantity, item_variations:[{id}], item_extras:[{id}], item_addons:[{id}]} and the backend recomputes everything through PricingService::calculateOrder (forKiosk). Frontend never sets prices; this is exactly the SSOT discipline CLAUDE.md §8 mandates and means kiosk/POS price parity cannot drift from the client.
- **Order type is set explicitly at idle start and asserted, eliminating the null-order-type 422 risk** (`resources/js/components/frontend/kiosk/KioskIdleScreenComponent.vue:257-261 + KioskAppComponent.vue:948-953 + kioskCart.js:40-47 (assertExplicitKioskOrderType)`) — selectOrderTypeAndStart emits start-order -> KioskAppComponent.startOrder dispatches setOrderType BEFORE navigating to categories, and quoteOrder/submitOrder assert an explicit KIOSK/TAKEAWAY value. The dine-in tile is gated by dineInEnabled (false in V1), so a customer cannot reach the cart without a valid order_type. Clean guard chain.
- **Concurrent kiosk-login coalescing prevents token-rotation self-DoS** (`resources/js/store/modules/kioskCart.js:456-478 (_inFlightKioskLogin) + routes/api.php:167-168 (dedicated kiosk-login throttle)`) — A single module-scoped in-flight Promise coalesces the 3 racing login call sites (router guard, login component, 401 interceptor) into one server-side token rotation, and the route uses a dedicated 30/min kiosk-login limiter instead of the human 10/10min lockout. This is a well-reasoned fix to a real boot-time race that previously revoked freshly-minted tokens.
- **Offline cash queue refuses electronic payment and preserves idempotency key for safe replay** (`resources/js/store/modules/kioskCart.js:760-804`) — On network error submitOrder rejects card/TR with KIOSK_OFFLINE_ELECTRONIC_PAYMENT_REFUSED (never lets the borne pretend an electronic payment succeeded offline), and for cash it queues the order stripped of the stale quote token while keeping the original idempotency key so the replay layer re-quotes and cannot double-charge. Correct fail-safe behavior for a single-box LOCAL deployment.
- **Public QR table-order pricing is SSOT-locked and discount-gated — strong NF525 defense** (`app/Services/OrderService.php:1336-1400 (tableOrderStore) + assertDiscretionaryDiscountAllowed`) — tableOrderStore force-zeroes the request-supplied manual discount at entry (`$request->merge(['discount' => 0])`, documented SEC-P1 QR-DISCOUNT), recomputes everything via PricingService::calculateOrder(PricingRequest::forTable), and then runs assertDiscretionaryDiscountAllowed on the coupon discount so a coupon cannot sign a fiscally-incorrect Z when manual_discount_enabled!==true. subtotal/total/discount from the client are unset before persist. This is exactly the right pattern: client sends only item refs, backend is authoritative. Good defense-in-depth even though the dine-in gate itself is missing (S17-01).
- **Sidebar V1-hidden-modules mechanism is clean, declarative, and reversible** (`resources/js/config/v1-hidden-modules.js:11-47 + resources/js/components/layouts/backend/BackendMenuComponent.vue:165-188`) — Hidden modules are a single frozen array; BackendMenuComponent builds a Set and filters both parent rows and children, with a documented 'remove the key to re-show' contract. tableOrders + diningTables are correctly listed, so the nav surface is honest about V1 scope. The only gap is that route-level guards were not added alongside the nav filter (S17-02) — but the menu layer itself is well-engineered.
- **Dine-in feature flag is enforced server-side on the two primary surfaces with a shared, unit-tested parser** (`app/Http/Requests/PosOrderRequest.php:163-170, app/Http/Requests/OrderRequest.php:218-230, tests/js/posDineInFlag.spec.js`) — Both POS and kiosk paths reject DINING_TABLE server-authoritatively (not just UI hiding), with clear FR error strings and explicit comments explaining the UI-bypass/replay threat model. The frontend parser dineInEnabledFrom() is unit-tested against int/string/bool/dotted-key variants. This is solid invariant engineering — the QR path (S17-01) is the lone surface that was missed, and the fix is a 4-line copy of the existing PosOrderRequest gate.
- **Admin back-office renders clean** (`0 console errors and 0 raw i18n labels across all 29 auto-enumerated admin pages`) — 0 console errors and 0 raw i18n labels across all 29 auto-enumerated admin pages
- **Dashboard KPIs are live and correctly formatted** (`Total ventes 32 056,20 EUR / 3483 commandes / 45 articles / today CA 252,30 EUR / ticket moyen 6,31 EUR — FR currency correct (comma + space + EUR after) — capture 01`) — Total ventes 32 056,20 EUR / 3483 commandes / 45 articles / today CA 252,30 EUR / ticket moyen 6,31 EUR — FR currency correct (comma + space + EUR after) — capture 01
- **OSS is clean and purpose-built** (`Dual-column En preparation / Pret, large readable order numbers, fullscreen toggle for display mode — capture 15`) — Dual-column En preparation / Pret, large readable order numbers, fullscreen toggle for display mode — capture 15
- **KDS degrades honestly** (`Loud 'liste pleine - filtrez' banner + color-coded BORNE/CAISSE source and status badges + full composition text for the kitchen — capture 14`) — Loud 'liste pleine - filtrez' banner + color-coded BORNE/CAISSE source and status badges + full composition text for the kitchen — capture 14

## Self-audit attestation (the report audited before delivery)

The finished report was re-audited by 4 independent hostile reviewers. Result: **SUBSTANTIALLY SOUND after corrections. 4 hostile reviewers independently re-opened every cited file:line: S1-S8 = 49/49 confirmed REAL (zero false-confirms, one bug even reproduced via php -r); S9-S17+LIVE = 33/39 reverified, the rest severity-recalibrated not refuted. P0=0 is correct. The operator-identity NF525 P1s (S11-02, S16-01), the public-QR dine-in gate (S17-01) and the .env APP_DEBUG toggle (S7-03) all hold. Net self-audit effect: 1 stale headline reframed (S7-01), ~8 severity re-ratings, 2 wrongly-dropped re-instated, ~8 citation/wording fixes.**.

**Corrections applied as a result of the self-audit:**
- Reframed S7-01 (P1->P2): the 'receipt prints SIRET/TVA blank' headline is now STALE — branch fiscal identity is populated (E.DELICE SAS, verified live); the real residual is 'no admin UI, CLI-only, not reseed-durable'.
- Severity re-ratings: S1-DASH-04 P1->P3, S4-01 P1->P2 (dup root cause of S4-02), S8-02 P1->P2 (scope to logout token-revocation), S11-01 P1->P3 (defense-in-depth), S13-06 P2->P3, S15-oss-01 P2->P3 (design decision), S13-04 P2->P3.
- Re-instated 2 wrongly-dropped/coverage items: GAP-items-pdf (P2, items PDF export truncates to 10 rows) and S8-03 (P3, kiosk-machine edit prefill).
- Citation/framing fixes: S8-01 (+SplitPaymentService path; tied to the owner terminal task), S8-02 (KioskMachineLoginController path), S7-03 (operational footgun, critical during cutover), S14-KDS-02 (raw-label not 409).
- Confirmed the operator-identity NF525 P1s (S11-02 POS receipt operator=customer, S16-01 kiosk-counter order records no cashier) and the public-QR dine-in gate (S17-01) hold end-to-end under independent re-verification.

**Standing caveats / residual challenges:**
- GAP-confirmCashPayment (PENDING_COUNTER->PAID counter cash collection) was NOT directly audited by S2 — recommend a targeted follow-up (idempotency, cash-trail) since it is the highest money/fiscal mutation on that surface.
- GAP-destroy-order and GAP-drainFailed were checked by the self-audit and found correctly guarded (branch+PAID+Z-seal; older-than-1h queue maintenance) — noted for completeness, NOT defects.
- S9-04 (LanguageService::fileTextStore writes arbitrary values into .php lang files via str_replace) was confirmed by the self-audit as a real injection-adjacent risk on an admin-only route — review input sanitisation.
- S5-06 export-pagination framing was flagged imprecise by two reviewers (over-export vs current-page-only) — verify the actual ItemsReportExport vs sales export merge(paginate=0) before acting; the items PDF truncation (GAP-items-pdf) is the confirmed concrete instance.
- All backend confirmations are static (file:line + control-flow), per the project worktree vendor-shadow note; no fiscal-mutating live-fire was performed (no-touch).

**Per-reviewer verdicts:**
- **RED re-verify S1-S8 (file:line)** — 49/49 CONFIRMED findings independently re-verified REAL — zero false-confirms, zero hallucinations; S1 timezone bug reproduced via php -r.
- **RED re-verify S9-S17 + LIVE** — 33/39 reverified_ok; operator-identity NF525 (S11-02/S16-01) and QR dine-in gate (S17-01) REAL end-to-end; 5 severity recalibrations, none refuted.
- **RED completeness + wrongly-dropped** — CONDITIONAL PASS: all 17 micro-systems present, counts consistent; re-instated S8-03 + GAP-items-pdf; destroy-order/drainFailed gaps found correctly guarded.
- **RED severity + reasoning** — Solid P1s hold (S6-01,S10-01,S11-02,S16-01); required fixes = S7-01 stale framing + severity cluster (S1-DASH-04, S4-01, S8-02) — all applied.

## Appendix — methodology
- 17 micro-systems audited in parallel (logic-first), each adversarially challenged for existence (file:line re-greped) + necessity (NO-OP filter).
- Raw findings + verdicts: `reports/audit/systems-massive-2026-06-04/findings/*.json`; screenshots: `.../captures/*.png`.
- Frozen zones (kiosk Wizard/App/Upsell, fiscal services) READ-ONLY; corrections flagged owner-gate.
